### [1] [AutoVeriFix+: High-Correctness RTL Generation via Trace-Aware Causal Fix and Semantic Redundancy Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11489)
*Yan Tan,Xiangchen Meng,Zijun Jiang,Yangdi Lyu*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: AutoVeriFix+是一个三阶段框架，通过结合高级语义推理和状态空间探索，提升LLM生成Verilog代码的功能正确性和设计效率，在VerilogEval-machine数据集上达到90.2%的pass@10分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成高级编程语言代码方面表现出色，但在硬件描述语言（如Verilog）方面面临挑战，主要原因是高质量训练数据稀缺。现有方法通常只关注语法正确性，导致生成的代码存在功能错误。

Method: 提出AutoVeriFix+三阶段框架：1) LLM生成定义电路行为的Python参考模型；2) LLM生成初始Verilog RTL候选并迭代修复语法错误；3) 引入Concolic测试引擎执行深度时序逻辑并识别边界情况漏洞，利用周期精确执行轨迹和内部寄存器快照为LLM提供因果上下文，同时生成覆盖率报告识别功能冗余分支进行语义剪枝。

Result: 在严格基准测试中实现超过80%的功能正确性，在VerilogEval-machine数据集上达到90.2%的pass@10分数。通过轨迹感知优化，在基准测试中平均消除25%的冗余逻辑。

Conclusion: AutoVeriFix+通过集成高级语义推理和状态空间探索，有效解决了LLM生成Verilog代码的功能正确性问题，同时实现了显著的逻辑优化，为硬件设计自动化提供了新方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generating software code for high-level programming languages such as Python and C++. However, their application to hardware description languages, such as Verilog, is challenging due to the scarcity of high-quality training data. Current approaches to Verilog code generation using LLMs often focus on syntactic correctness, resulting in code with functional errors. To address these challenges, we propose AutoVeriFix+, a novel three-stage framework that integrates high-level semantic reasoning with state-space exploration to enhance functional correctness and design efficiency. In the first stage, an LLM is employed to generate high-level Python reference models that define the intended circuit behavior. In the second stage, another LLM generates initial Verilog RTL candidates and iteratively fixes syntactic errors. In the third stage, we introduce a Concolic testing engine to exercise deep sequential logic and identify corner-case vulnerabilities. With cycle-accurate execution traces and internal register snapshots, AutoVeriFix+ provides the LLM with the causal context necessary to resolve complex state-transition errors. Furthermore, it will generate a coverage report to identify functionally redundant branches, enabling the LLM to perform semantic pruning for area optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that AutoVeriFix+ achieves over 80% functional correctness on rigorous benchmarks, reaching a pass@10 score of 90.2% on the VerilogEval-machine dataset. In addition, it eliminates an average of 25% redundant logic across benchmarks through trace-aware optimization.

</details>

### [2] [BEACON: Budget-Aware Entity Matching Across Domains (Extended Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11391)
*Nicholas Pulsone,Roee Shraga,Gregory Goren*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BEACON是一个分布感知、预算感知的跨领域低资源实体匹配框架，通过利用候选匹配对的嵌入表示来指导在有限领域监督下有效选择跨领域样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体匹配是数据集成中的核心任务，深度学习特别是预训练语言模型和大语言模型微调已成为新标准。然而微调需要大量标注数据，成本高昂且耗时。在电子商务匹配中，不同领域的标注稀缺性差异很大，如何在有限标注数据下智能训练准确的领域特定实体匹配模型成为关键问题。

Method: BEACON框架假设用户只有特定目标领域的有限标注数据，但可以访问其他领域的标注数据。该框架利用候选匹配对的嵌入表示来指导在有限领域监督下有效选择跨领域样本。它是一个分布感知、预算感知的框架，专门针对跨领域的低资源实体匹配场景设计。

Result: 在从已建立的实体匹配基准数据集派生的多个领域划分数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明BEACON在不同训练预算下始终优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: BEACON框架通过智能利用跨领域数据和候选匹配对的嵌入表示，在有限标注数据条件下实现了更有效的实体匹配模型训练，为解决低资源跨领域实体匹配问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Entity Matching (EM)--the task of determining whether two data records refer to the same real-world entity--is a core task in data integration. Recent advances in deep learning have set a new standard for EM, particularly through fine-tuning Pretrained Language Models (PLMs) and, more recently, Large Language Models (LLMs). However, fine-tuning typically requires large amounts of labeled data, which are expensive and time-consuming to obtain. In the context of e-commerce matching, labeling scarcity varies widely across domains, raising the question of how to intelligently train accurate domain-specific EM models with limited labeled data. In this work we assume users have only limited amount of labels for a specific target domain but have access to labeled data from other domains. We introduce BEACON, a distribution-aware, budget-aware framework for low-resource EM across domains. BEACON leverages the insight that embedding representations of pairwise candidate matches can guide the effective selection of out-of-domain samples under limited in-domain supervision. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple domain-partitioned datasets derived from established EM benchmarks, demonstrating that BEACON consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods under different training budgets.

</details>


### [3] [Faster Relational Algorithms Using Geometric Data Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11402)
*Aryan Esmailpour,Stavros Sintos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出RBBD树框架，通过随机化BBD树和关系连接的高效采样/计数技术，避免物化连接结果，在关系数据上实现快速几何近似算法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据上的优化任务（如聚类）通常需要昂贵的连接操作来访问完整数据集，而几何数据结构（如BBD树）在关系数据中的应用因连接输出规模过大而受限

Method: 开发RBBD树（关系BBD树），这是BBD树的随机化变体，专为关系设置设计。通过关系连接的高效采样和计数技术，实现RBBD树的按需扩展，仅维护必要部分，无需物化连接结果即可模拟几何查询过程

Result: 提出的算法将关系k-center/means/median聚类的最先进运行时间提高了k倍，同时保持相同的近似保证。方法具有通用性，可应用于关系设置中的各种优化问题

Conclusion: RBBD树框架成功地将几何洞察力应用于关系数据，通过避免物化连接结果实现了高效的近似算法，显著提升了关系聚类任务的性能

Abstract: Optimization tasks over relational data, such as clustering, often suffer from the prohibitive cost of join operations, which are necessary to access the full dataset. While geometric data structures like BBD trees yield fast approximation algorithms in the standard computational setting, their application to relational data remains unclear due to the size of the join output. In this paper, we introduce a framework that leverages geometric insights to design faster algorithms when the data is stored as the results of a join query in a relational database. Our core contribution is the development of the RBBD tree, a randomized variant of the BBD tree tailored for relational settings. Instead of completely constructing the RBBD tree, by leveraging efficient sampling and counting techniques over relational joins, we enable on-the-fly efficient expansion of the RBBD tree, maintaining only the necessary parts. This allows us to simulate geometric query procedures without materializing the join result. As an application, we present algorithms that improve the state-of-the-art for relational $k$-center/means/median clustering by a factor of $k$ in running time while maintaining the same approximation guarantees. Our method is general and can be applied to various optimization problems in the relational setting.

</details>


### [4] [LHGstore: An In-Memory Learned Graph Storage for Fast Updates and Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11596)
*Pengpeng Qiao,Zhiwei Zhang,Xinzhou Wang,Zhetao Li,Xiaochun Cao,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LHGstore：首个将学习索引集成到图管理中的度感知分层图存储系统，通过两级层次结构解耦顶点和边访问，针对不同度顶点使用自适应数据结构，在高度倾斜度分布下实现高更新吞吐量和快速分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中需要内存动态图高效处理频繁更新同时支持低延迟分析，但更新效率与遍历局部性之间存在权衡，特别是在高度倾斜的度分布下，这促使设计针对现代多核CPU优化的图索引方案。

Method: LHGstore采用度感知学习分层图存储，设计两级层次结构解耦顶点和边访问，根据顶点度自适应组织边数据结构：低度顶点使用轻量数组最大化遍历局部性，高度顶点应用学习索引提高更新吞吐量。

Result: 实验表明LHGstore相比最先进的内存图存储系统实现5.9-28.2倍更高吞吐量，分析速度显著更快。

Conclusion: LHGstore首次将学习索引集成到图管理中，通过度感知分层设计有效平衡更新效率和遍历局部性，在高度倾斜度分布下为内存动态图提供高效存储解决方案。

Abstract: Various real-world applications rely on in-memory dynamic graphs that must efficiently handle frequent updates while supporting low-latency analytics on evolving structures. Achieving both objectives remains challenging due to the trade-off between update efficiency and traversal locality, particularly under highly skewed degree distributions. This motivates the design of graph indexing schemes optimized for in-memory graph management on modern multi-core CPUs. We present LHGstore, a degree-aware Learned Hierarchical Graph storage that, for the first time, integrates learned indexing into graph management. LHGstore designs a two-level hierarchy that decouples vertex and edge access and further organizes each vertex's edges using data structures adaptive to its degree. Lightweight arrays are used for low-degree vertices to maximize traversal locality, while learned indexes are applied to high-degree vertices to improve update throughput. Extensive experiments show that LHGstore achieves 5.9-28.2$\times$ higher throughput and significantly faster analytics than SOTA in-memory graph storage systems.

</details>


### [5] [Sema: A High-performance System for LLM-based Semantic Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11622)
*Kangkang Qi,Dongyang Xie,Wenbo Li,Hao Zhang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Jeffrey Xu Yu,Kangfei Zhao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Sema是一个基于DuckDB的高性能语义查询引擎，将LLM驱动的语义操作符作为一等公民，通过SemaSQL声明式方言实现端到端优化，在保持结果质量的同时获得2-10倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统要么依赖缺乏高效执行基础设施的DataFrame原语，要么使用将语义逻辑与查询优化器隔离的SQL UDF，给用户带来实现复杂性。LLM驱动的语义操作符由于高成本和非确定性特性，传统优化规则和成本模型不适用。

Method: Sema在逻辑层面压缩自然语言表达式并从语义操作符推导关系约束；运行时采用自适应查询执行动态重排操作符、融合语义操作和应用提示批处理，寻求在准确性约束下平衡令牌消耗和延迟的帕累托最优执行路径。

Result: 在分类、摘要和提取任务的20个语义查询评估中，Sema相比三个基线系统实现2-10倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的结果质量。

Conclusion: Sema通过将LLM驱动的语义操作符作为一等公民，提供声明式SemaSQL方言和端到端优化执行，有效解决了现有系统在LLM集成数据分析和优化方面的局限性。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into data analytics has unlocked powerful capabilities for reasoning over bulk structured and unstructured data. However, existing systems typically rely on either DataFrame primitives, which lack the efficient execution infrastructure of modern DBMSs, or SQL User-Defined Functions (UDFs), which isolate semantic logic from the query optimizer and burden users with implementation complexities. The LLM-powered semantic operators also bring new challenges due to the high cost and non-deterministic nature of LLM invocation, where conventional optimization rules and cost models are inapplicable for their optimization.
  To bridge these gaps, we present Sema, a high-performance semantic query engine built on DuckDB that treats LLM-powered semantic operators as first-class citizens. Sema introduces SemaSQL, a declarative dialect that allows users seamlessly inject natural language expressions into standard SQL clauses, enabling end-to-end optimization and execution. At the logical level, the optimizer of Sema compresses natural language expressions and deduces relational constraints from semantic operators. At runtime, Sema employs Adaptive Query Execution (AQE) to dynamically reorder operators, fuse semantic operations, and apply prompt batching. This approach seeks a Pareto-optimal execution path balancing token consumption and latency under accuracy constraints. We evaluate Sema on 20 semantic queries across classification, summarization, and extraction tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that Sema achieves $2-10 \times$ speedup against three baseline systems while achieving competitive result quality.

</details>


### [6] [OMNIA: Closing the Loop by Leveraging LLMs for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11820)
*Frédéric Ieng,Soror Sahri,Mourad Ouzzani,Massinissa Hammaz,Salima Benbernou,Hanieh Khorashadizadeh,Sven Groppe,Farah Benamara*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OMNIA：一种用于知识图谱补全的两阶段方法，通过聚类和LLM验证结合结构推理与语义推理，无需外部数据源


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱补全方法存在局限性：基于结构嵌入的方法忽略语义信息，而基于语言模型的方法忽视图结构且依赖外部数据源。LLM自动构建的知识图谱通常存在不完整和噪声问题，特别是隐含语义的缺失。

Method: OMNIA采用两阶段方法：1）候选三元组生成阶段：在知识图谱内部对语义相关的实体和关系进行聚类；2）验证阶段：先通过轻量级嵌入过滤，再使用LLM进行语义验证。该方法完全基于内部知识图谱，无需外部数据源。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，OMNIA相比传统基于嵌入的模型显著提高了F1分数。其聚类和过滤阶段有效减少了搜索空间和验证成本，同时保持了高质量的补全效果。

Conclusion: OMNIA成功地将结构推理与语义推理相结合，专门针对LLM生成图谱中最常见的隐含语义问题，提供了一种高效且有效的知识图谱补全解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are widely used to represent structured knowledge, yet their automatic construction, especially with Large Language Models (LLMs), often results in incomplete or noisy outputs. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to infer and add missing triples, but most existing methods either rely on structural embeddings that overlook semantics or language models that ignore the graph's structure and depend on external sources. In this work, we present OMNIA, a two-stage approach that bridges structural and semantic reasoning for KGC. It first generates candidate triples by clustering semantically related entities and relations within the KG, then validates them through lightweight embedding filtering followed by LLM-based semantic validation. OMNIA performs on the internal KG, without external sources, and specifically targets implicit semantics that are most frequent in LLM-generated graphs. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that OMNIA significantly improves F1-score compared to traditional embedding-based models. These results highlight OMNIA's effectiveness and efficiency, as its clustering and filtering stages reduce both search space and validation cost while maintaining high-quality completion.

</details>


### [7] [Numerical benchmark for damage identification in Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12069)
*Francesca Marafini,Giacomo Zini,Alberto Barontini,Nuno Mendes,Alice Cicirello,Michele Betti,Gianni Bartoli*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于结构健康监测(SHM)验证的模拟数据集，包含动态和静态测量数据，考虑了环境操作变化、损伤、噪声和传感器故障等多种真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工程领域面临验证数据驱动策略和混合物理-数据方法的挑战，数据所有权、安全性、访问和元数据可用性等问题阻碍了结构健康监测等领域的进展，需要高质量的模拟数据集来促进研究。

Method: 采用固定-固定钢梁结构作为数值基准，使用单自由度系统生成加速度记录，欧拉-伯努利梁理论生成位移测量，通过并行计算生成包含环境操作变化、不同类型损伤、测量噪声和传感器故障的模拟数据。

Result: 生成了一个全面的SHM模拟数据集，包含动态和静态测量数据，提供了开源代码和数据，确保了可重复性、可再现性和可访问性，为研究人员开发或改进SHM技术提供了宝贵资源。

Conclusion: 该模拟数据集通过提供高质量、可访问的合成数据，解决了SHM领域的数据可用性问题，促进了该领域的研究进展，为验证数据驱动方法和混合方法提供了重要工具。

Abstract: The availability of a dataset for validation and verification purposes of novel data-driven strategies and/or hybrid physics-data approaches is currently one of the most pressing challenges in the engineering field. Data ownership, security, access and metadata handiness are currently hindering advances across many fields, particularly in Structural Health Monitoring (SHM) applications. This paper presents a simulated SHM dataset, comprised of dynamic and static measurements (i.e., acceleration and displacement), and includes the conceptual framework designed to generate it. The simulated measurements were generated to incorporate the effects of Environmental and Operational Variations (EOVs), different types of damage, measurement noise and sensor faults and malfunctions, in order to account for scenarios that may occur during real acquisitions. A fixed-fixed steel beam structure was chosen as reference for the numerical benchmark. The simulated monitoring was operated under the assumptions of a Single Degree of Freedom (SDOF) for generating acceleration records and of the Euler-Bernoulli beam for the simulated displacement measurements. The generation process involved the use of parallel computation, which is detailed within the provided open-source code. The generated data is also available open-source, thus ensuring reproducibility, repeatability and accessibility for further research. The comprehensive description of data types, formats, and collection methodologies makes this dataset a valuable resource for researchers aiming to develop or refine SHM techniques, fostering advancements in the field through accessible, high-quality synthetic data.

</details>


### [8] [Structure Selection for Fairness-Constrained Differentially Private Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12112)
*Naeim Ghahramanpour,Mostafa Milani*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PrivCI：一种在差分隐私合成数据生成中强制执行条件独立性约束的方法，通过CI感知的贪心最小生成树算法提高公平性基准的保真度和预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差分隐私合成数据生成器会保留数据中的所有依赖关系，包括敏感属性与结果之间的虚假相关性，这在公平性关键场景中会重现不希望的偏见。需要一种原则性方法来强制执行条件独立性约束，以编码领域知识或法律要求。

Method: 提出PrivCI方法，在差分隐私合成的测量步骤中通过CI感知的贪心最小生成树算法强制执行条件独立性约束。该算法将可行性检查集成到Kruskal构造中，并在指数机制下运行，提高了与竞争方法相比的准确性。

Result: 在标准公平性基准测试上的实验表明，PrivCI在满足指定CI约束的同时，比现有基线方法实现了更强的保真度和预测准确性。

Conclusion: PrivCI通过在差分隐私合成数据生成的测量步骤中强制执行条件独立性约束，提供了一种有效的方法来生成既保护隐私又满足公平性要求的数据，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) enables safe data release, with synthetic data generation emerging as a common approach in recent years. Yet standard synthesizers preserve all dependencies in the data, including spurious correlations between sensitive attributes and outcomes. In fairness-critical settings, this reproduces unwanted bias. A principled remedy is to enforce conditional independence (CI) constraints, which encode domain knowledge or legal requirements that outcomes be independent of sensitive attributes once admissible factors are accounted for. DP synthesis typically proceeds in two phases: (i) a measure- ment step that privatizes selected marginals, often structured via minimum spanning trees (MSTs), and (ii) a reconstruction step that fits a probabilistic model consistent with the noisy marginals. We propose PrivCI, which enforces CI during the measurement step via a CI-aware greedy MST algorithm that integrates feasibility checks into Kruskal's construction under the exponential mechanism, improving accuracy over competing methods. Experiments on standard fairness benchmarks show that PrivCI achieves stronger fidelity and predictive accuracy than prior baselines while satisfying the specified CI constraints.

</details>

### [9] [Enhancing Value Alignment of LLMs with Multi-agent system and Combinatorial Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11126)
*Yuanhong Wu,Djallel Bouneffouf,D. Frank Hsu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: VAS-CFA框架通过多智能体融合分析实现LLM价值对齐，优于单智能体基线方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF等方法依赖单一评估者或狭窄的奖励信号，难以捕捉伦理多元主义，限制了价值对齐能力

Method: 提出VAS-CFA框架：实例化多个代表不同规范视角的道德智能体，使用组合融合分析（CFA）结合基于排名和分数的聚合方法融合其输出

Result: 实证评估显示VAS-CFA在标准指标上优于单智能体基线和先前的聚合方法

Conclusion: 多智能体融合为LLM价值对齐提供了稳健有效的机制，能更好地反映人类价值观

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values is a central challenge for ensuring trustworthy and safe deployment. While existing methods such as Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) and its variants have improved alignment, they often rely on a single evaluator or narrowly defined reward signals, limiting their ability to capture ethical pluralism. In this work, we propose the Value Alignment System using Combinatorial Fusion Analysis (VAS-CFA), a framework that operationalizes multi-agent fusion alignment. It instantiates multiple moral agents, each fine-tuned to represent a distinct normative perspective, and fuses their outputs using CFA with both rank- and score-based aggregation. This design leverages cognitive diversity, between agents, to mitigate conflicts and redundancies across multiple agents, producing responses that better reflect human values. Empirical evaluation demonstrates that VAS-CFA outperforms both single agent baselines and prior aggregation approaches on standard metrics, showing that multi-agent fusion provides a robust and effective mechanism for advancing value alignment in LLMs.

</details>


### [10] [How Intelligence Emerges: A Minimal Theory of Dynamic Adaptive Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11560)
*Stefano Grassi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个多智能体系统中自适应协调的动力学理论，将协调视为耦合动力学的结构特性而非集中式优化问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通过均衡优化或智能体中心学习来分析协调，但本文旨在建立更全面的框架，将智能体、激励和环境建模为递归闭合的反馈架构，以理解协调作为耦合动力学结构特性的本质。

Method: 构建包含持久环境、分布式激励场和自适应智能体的递归闭合反馈架构。持久环境存储累积的协调信号，分布式激励场局部传输这些信号，自适应智能体响应更新。采用耗散性假设和谱分析技术，通过最小线性规范说明耦合、持久性和耗散如何通过雅可比矩阵的谱条件控制局部稳定性和振荡机制。

Result: 建立了三个结构结果：1) 在耗散性假设下，诱导闭环系统存在有界前向不变区域，确保可行性而无需全局最优性；2) 当激励信号非平凡地依赖于持久环境记忆时，所得动力学通常不能简化为仅定义在智能体状态空间上的静态全局目标；3) 持久环境状态诱导历史敏感性，除非系统全局收缩。通过谱条件阐明了耦合、持久性和耗散如何控制局部稳定性和振荡机制。

Conclusion: 智能协调动力学可以从持久环境内激励介导的自适应交互中涌现，无需预设福利最大化、理性预期或集中式设计。协调应被视为耦合动力学的结构特性，而非集中式优化问题的解，这为理解多智能体系统协调提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a dynamical theory of adaptive coordination in multi-agent systems. Rather than analyzing coordination through equilibrium optimization or agent-centric learning alone, the framework models agents, incentives, and environment as a recursively closed feedback architecture. A persistent environment stores accumulated coordination signals, a distributed incentive field transmits those signals locally, and adaptive agents update in response. Coordination is thus treated as a structural property of coupled dynamics rather than as the solution to a centralized objective.
  The paper establishes three structural results. First, under dissipativity assumptions, the induced closed-loop system admits a bounded forward-invariant region, ensuring viability without requiring global optimality. Second, when incentive signals depend non-trivially on persistent environmental memory, the resulting dynamics generically cannot be reduced to a static global objective defined solely over the agent state space. Third, persistent environmental state induces history sensitivity unless the system is globally contracting.
  A minimal linear specification illustrates how coupling, persistence, and dissipation govern local stability and oscillatory regimes through spectral conditions on the Jacobian. The results establish structural conditions under which intelligent coordination dynamics emerge from incentive-mediated adaptive interaction within a persistent environment, without presuming welfare maximization, rational expectations, or centralized design.

</details>


### [11] [The price of decentralization in managing engineering systems through multi-agent reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11884)
*Prateek Bhustali,Pablo G. Morato,Konstantinos G. Papakonstantinou,Charalampos P. Andriotis*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文研究多组件系统检查与维护规划中的多智能体深度强化学习，通过设计包含不同冗余度的基准环境，评估MADRL算法的协调性能，发现冗余度增加会加剧协调挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检查与维护规划涉及不确定性和不完全信息下的序贯决策，可建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程。单智能体深度强化学习虽能提供POMDP近似解，但在多组件系统中扩展性差。多智能体深度强化学习通过分散决策实现可扩展性，但分散化可能引发合作病理，降低学习策略的最优性。

Method: 引入一组退化系统，其中冗余度被系统性地变化。这些基准环境设计使得集中式（近似）最优策略的计算保持可处理，便于直接比较不同解决方法。实现并基准测试了涵盖完全集中式和分散式训练范式的广泛MADRL算法，包括价值分解和演员-评论家方法。

Result: 结果显示冗余度对协调有明确影响：MADRL算法在串联式设置中实现接近最优性能，而增加冗余度会放大协调挑战并可能导致最优性损失。尽管如此，分散智能体学习到的结构化策略始终优于优化的启发式基线。

Conclusion: 研究突显了分散学习在可扩展维护规划中的前景和当前局限性，表明虽然冗余系统存在协调挑战，但MADRL仍能超越传统启发式方法，为复杂系统维护规划提供了有希望的解决方案。

Abstract: Inspection and maintenance (I&M) planning involves sequential decision making under uncertainties and incomplete information, and can be modeled as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP). While single-agent deep reinforcement learning provides approximate solutions to POMDPs, it does not scale well in multi-component systems. Scalability can be achieved through multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MADRL), which decentralizes decision-making across multiple agents, locally controlling individual components. However, this decentralization can induce cooperation pathologies that degrade the optimality of the learned policies. To examine these effects in I&M planning, we introduce a set of deteriorating systems in which redundancy is varied systematically. These benchmark environments are designed such that computation of centralized (near-)optimal policies remains tractable, enabling direct comparison of solution methods. We implement and benchmark a broad set of MADRL algorithms spanning fully centralized and decentralized training paradigms, from value-factorization to actor-critic methods. Our results show a clear effect of redundancy on coordination: MADRL algorithms achieve near-optimal performance in series-like settings, whereas increasing redundancy amplifies coordination challenges and can lead to optimality losses. Nonetheless, decentralized agents learn structured policies that consistently outperform optimized heuristic baselines, highlighting both the promise and current limitations of decentralized learning for scalable maintenance planning.

</details>


### [12] [CogSearch: A Cognitive-Aligned Multi-Agent Framework for Proactive Decision Support in E-Commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11927)
*Zhouwei Zhai,Mengxiang Chen,Haoyun Xia,Jin Li,Renquan Zhou,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CogSearch是一个认知导向的多智能体框架，将电商搜索重新构想为主动决策支持系统，通过四个专业智能体模拟人类认知工作流程，显著降低决策成本并提升转化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商搜索引擎基于被动检索-排序模型，难以支持复杂决策过程，导致用户面临认知摩擦和决策困难。需要从被动检索转向主动决策支持。

Method: 提出CogSearch框架，包含四个专业智能体协同工作：1）分解复杂用户意图；2）融合内部和外部异构知识；3）提供高度可操作的洞察。框架模拟人类认知工作流程，实现主动决策支持。

Result: 离线基准测试验证了CogSearch在咨询性和复杂搜索场景中的卓越表现。在JD.com的在线A/B测试显示：决策成本降低5%，整体UCVR提升0.41%，决策密集型查询的转化率显著提升30%。

Conclusion: CogSearch代表了信息检索的根本转变，从传统的以相关性为中心的范式转向整体、协作的决策智能，为电商搜索提供了新的发展方向。

Abstract: Modern e-commerce search engines, largely rooted in passive retrieval-and-ranking models, frequently fail to support complex decision-making, leaving users overwhelmed by cognitive friction. In this paper, we introduce CogSearch, a novel cognitive-oriented multi-agent framework that reimagines e-commerce search as a proactive decision support system. By synergizing four specialized agents, CogSearch mimics human cognitive workflows: it decomposes intricate user intents, fuses heterogeneous knowledge across internal and external sources, and delivers highly actionable insights. Our offline benchmarks validate CogSearch's excellence in consultative and complex search scenarios. Extensive online A/B testing on JD.com demonstrates the system's transformative impact: it reduced decision costs by 5% and achieved a 0.41% increase in overall UCVR, with a remarkable 30% surge in conversion for decision-heavy queries. CogSearch represents a fundamental shift in information retrieval, moving beyond traditional relevance-centric paradigms toward a future of holistic, collaborative decision intelligence.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [13] [Comparison of Outlier Detection Algorithms on String Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11049)
*Philip Maus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文比较了两种字符串异常检测算法：基于编辑距离的局部离群因子变体和基于分层正则表达式学习的新算法，实验表明两种方法都能有效检测字符串异常，但各有适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前异常检测研究主要集中在数值数据，而字符串数据的异常检测研究较少。开发稳健的字符串异常检测算法有助于数据清洗和系统日志文件中的异常检测。

Method: 1. 提出局部离群因子算法的变体，使用Levenshtein距离计算数据集密度，并引入考虑分层字符类别的加权Levenshtein度量；2. 提出基于分层左正则表达式学习的新算法，通过推断期望数据的正则表达式来检测异常。

Result: 实验表明两种算法都能在概念上检测字符串数据中的异常。基于正则表达式的算法在期望值具有明显结构且与异常结构差异足够大时表现优异；而局部离群因子算法在异常与期望数据之间的编辑距离与期望数据内部编辑距离差异明显时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 两种字符串异常检测算法各有优势：正则表达式方法适用于结构化数据，而编辑距离方法适用于基于相似性的异常检测。算法选择应基于数据特征和异常类型。

Abstract: Outlier detection is a well-researched and crucial problem in machine learning. However, there is little research on string data outlier detection, as most literature focuses on outlier detection of numerical data. A robust string data outlier detection algorithm could assist with data cleaning or anomaly detection in system log files. In this thesis, we compare two string outlier detection algorithms. Firstly, we introduce a variant of the well-known local outlier factor algorithm, which we tailor to detect outliers on string data using the Levenshtein measure to calculate the density of the dataset. We present a differently weighted Levenshtein measure, which considers hierarchical character classes and can be used to tune the algorithm to a specific string dataset. Secondly, we introduce a new kind of outlier detection algorithm based on the hierarchical left regular expression learner, which infers a regular expression for the expected data. Using various datasets and parameters, we experimentally show that both algorithms can conceptually find outliers in string data. We show that the regular expression-based algorithm is especially good at finding outliers if the expected values have a distinct structure that is sufficiently different from the structure of the outliers. In contrast, the local outlier factor algorithms are best at finding outliers if their edit distance to the expected data is sufficiently distinct from the edit distance between the expected data.

</details>


### [14] [Structure-Aware Epistemic Uncertainty Quantification for Neural Operator PDE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11052)
*Haoze Song,Zhihao Li,Mengyi Deng,Xin Li,Duyi Pan,Zhilu Lai,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向神经算子的结构化认知不确定性量化方法，通过仅在lifting模块注入随机性，实现计算高效且空间保真的不确定性估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为PDE求解器的快速替代模型，其预测存在显著的认知不确定性（源于有限数据、优化不完美和分布偏移）。在实际科学计算应用中，不确定性量化需要计算高效且空间保真，即不确定性带应与局部残差结构对齐，这对下游风险管理至关重要。

Method: 利用现代神经算子的模块化结构（lifting-propagation-recovering），提出结构化认知不确定性量化方案。不采用非结构化权重扰动（如朴素dropout），而是将蒙特卡洛采样限制在模块对齐的子空间：仅在lifting模块注入随机性，将学习到的求解器动力学（传播和恢复）视为确定性。具体实现两种轻量级lifting层扰动：通道级乘法特征dropout和具有匹配方差的高斯特征扰动，随后进行标准校准以构建不确定性带。

Result: 在具有挑战性的PDE基准测试（包括不连续系数Darcy流和几何偏移的3D汽车CFD替代模型）上实验表明，所提出的结构化设计相比常见基线方法，能提供更可靠的覆盖、更紧的不确定性带和更好的残差-不确定性对齐，同时保持实际运行时间。

Conclusion: 通过利用神经算子的模块化结构，仅在lifting模块注入随机性的结构化不确定性量化方法，能够实现计算高效且空间保真的认知不确定性估计，为科学计算中神经算子的实际部署提供了可靠的不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Neural operators (NOs) provide fast, resolution-invariant surrogates for mapping input fields to PDE solution fields, but their predictions can exhibit significant epistemic uncertainty due to finite data, imperfect optimization, and distribution shift. For practical deployment in scientific computing, uncertainty quantification (UQ) must be both computationally efficient and spatially faithful, i.e., uncertainty bands should align with the localized residual structures that matter for downstream risk management. We propose a structure-aware epistemic UQ scheme that exploits the modular anatomy common to modern NOs (lifting-propagation-recovering). Instead of applying unstructured weight perturbations (e.g., naive dropout) across the entire network, we restrict Monte Carlo sampling to a module-aligned subspace by injecting stochasticity only into the lifting module, and treat the learned solver dynamics (propagation and recovery) as deterministic. We instantiate this principle with two lightweight lifting-level perturbations, including channel-wise multiplicative feature dropout and a Gaussian feature perturbation with matched variance, followed by standard calibration to construct uncertainty bands. Experiments on challenging PDE benchmarks (including discontinuous-coefficient Darcy flow and geometry-shifted 3D car CFD surrogates) demonstrate that the proposed structure-aware design yields more reliable coverage, tighter bands, and improved residual-uncertainty alignment compared with common baselines, while remaining practical in runtime.

</details>


### [15] [Fingerprinting Concepts in Data Streams with Supervised and Unsupervised Meta-Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11094)
*Ben Halstead,Yun Sing Koh,Patricia Riddle,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Albert Bifet,Russel Pears*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FiCSUM是一个用于数据流概念漂移检测的通用框架，通过构建包含多种元信息特征的概念指纹来更准确地区分不同概念，并采用动态加权策略学习哪些特征对特定数据集的概念漂移描述最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着实时数据收集能力的增强，数据流处理面临概念漂移（数据分布随时间变化）的挑战。现有概念表示方法通常依赖少量元信息特征，难以有效区分不同概念，导致系统对概念漂移的脆弱性。

Method: 提出FiCSUM框架：1）构建概念指纹，即包含多种不同元信息特征的向量，能够唯一标识更多概念；2）采用动态加权策略，学习在特定数据集中哪些元信息特征最能描述概念漂移；3）支持监督和无监督行为的概念表示。

Result: 在11个真实世界和合成数据集上，FiCSUM在准确性和建模底层概念漂移方面均优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: FiCSUM通过丰富的元信息特征概念表示和动态特征加权，显著提升了概念漂移检测能力，为数据流处理中的概念漂移适应提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Streaming sources of data are becoming more common as the ability to collect data in real-time grows. A major concern in dealing with data streams is concept drift, a change in the distribution of data over time, for example, due to changes in environmental conditions. Representing concepts (stationary periods featuring similar behaviour) is a key idea in adapting to concept drift. By testing the similarity of a concept representation to a window of observations, we can detect concept drift to a new or previously seen recurring concept. Concept representations are constructed using meta-information features, values describing aspects of concept behaviour. We find that previously proposed concept representations rely on small numbers of meta-information features. These representations often cannot distinguish concepts, leaving systems vulnerable to concept drift. We propose FiCSUM, a general framework to represent both supervised and unsupervised behaviours of a concept in a fingerprint, a vector of many distinct meta-information features able to uniquely identify more concepts. Our dynamic weighting strategy learns which meta-information features describe concept drift in a given dataset, allowing a diverse set of meta-information features to be used at once. FiCSUM outperforms state-of-the-art methods over a range of 11 real world and synthetic datasets in both accuracy and modeling underlying concept drift.

</details>


### [16] [Task-Conditioned Routing Signatures in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11114)
*Mynampati Sri Ranganadha Avinash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现稀疏混合专家模型的路由机制具有任务条件化结构，同一任务类别的提示会诱导高度相似的路由签名，而不同任务类别的路由签名相似度显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家架构通过条件计算实现了大语言模型的高效扩展，但负责专家选择的路由机制仍然缺乏深入理解。本研究旨在探究MoE路由是否展现出任务条件化结构。

Method: 引入路由签名作为向量表示，总结给定提示在各层的专家激活模式。使用OLMoE-1B-7B-0125-Instruct作为实证测试平台，分析路由签名的相似性模式。建立置换和负载均衡基线以确保统计有效性，分析层间信号强度和路由签名的低维投影。

Result: 同一任务类别内的路由相似度（0.8435±0.0879）显著高于跨类别相似度（0.6225±0.1687），Cohen's d=1.44。仅基于路由签名的逻辑回归分类器在四向任务分类中达到92.5%±6.1%的交叉验证准确率。任务结构在更深层中变得更加明显。

Conclusion: 稀疏变换器中的路由不仅仅是平衡机制，而是条件计算中可测量的任务敏感组件。研究发布了MOE-XRAY工具包用于路由遥测和分析。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable efficient scaling of large language models through conditional computation, yet the routing mechanisms responsible for expert selection remain poorly understood. In this work, we introduce routing signatures, a vector representation summarizing expert activation patterns across layers for a given prompt, and use them to study whether MoE routing exhibits task-conditioned structure. Using OLMoE-1B-7B-0125-Instruct as an empirical testbed, we show that prompts from the same task category induce highly similar routing signatures, while prompts from different categories exhibit substantially lower similarity. Within-category routing similarity (0.8435 +/- 0.0879) significantly exceeds across-category similarity (0.6225 +/- 0.1687), corresponding to Cohen's d = 1.44. A logistic regression classifier trained solely on routing signatures achieves 92.5% +/- 6.1% cross-validated accuracy on four-way task classification. To ensure statistical validity, we introduce permutation and load-balancing baselines and show that the observed separation is not explained by sparsity or balancing constraints alone. We further analyze layer-wise signal strength and low-dimensional projections of routing signatures, finding that task structure becomes increasingly apparent in deeper layers. These results suggest that routing in sparse transformers is not merely a balancing mechanism, but a measurable task-sensitive component of conditional computation. We release MOE-XRAY, a lightweight toolkit for routing telemetry and analysis.

</details>


### [17] [Learning Tree-Based Models with Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11117)
*Sascha Marton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过梯度下降学习硬轴对齐决策树的新方法，使用直通算子和密集表示进行反向传播，实现所有树参数的联合优化，在多个领域达到SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树学习方法（如CART）存在两个主要限制：1）使用贪婪搜索过程，在节点处做局部最优决策，导致次优树结构；2）需要定制训练方法，无法与现代机器学习方法无缝集成。

Method: 提出基于梯度下降的决策树学习方法，使用直通算子进行反向传播，通过密集决策树表示实现所有树参数的联合优化，克服传统方法的局限性。

Result: 在多个领域取得最先进结果：小表格数据集的解释性决策树、复杂表格数据的高级模型、多模态学习以及无信息损失的解释性强化学习。

Conclusion: 通过弥合决策树与基于梯度的优化之间的差距，该方法显著提升了树模型在各种机器学习领域的性能和适用性。

Abstract: Tree-based models are widely recognized for their interpretability and have proven effective in various application domains, particularly in high-stakes domains. However, learning decision trees (DTs) poses a significant challenge due to their combinatorial complexity and discrete, non-differentiable nature. As a result, traditional methods such as CART, which rely on greedy search procedures, remain the most widely used approaches. These methods make locally optimal decisions at each node, constraining the search space and often leading to suboptimal tree structures. Additionally, their demand for custom training methods precludes a seamless integration into modern machine learning (ML) approaches.
  In this thesis, we propose a novel method for learning hard, axis-aligned DTs through gradient descent. Our approach utilizes backpropagation with a straight-through operator on a dense DT representation, enabling the joint optimization of all tree parameters, thereby addressing the two primary limitations of traditional DT algorithms. First, gradient-based training is not constrained by the sequential selection of locally optimal splits but, instead, jointly optimizes all tree parameters. Second, by leveraging gradient descent for optimization, our approach seamlessly integrates into existing ML approaches e.g., for multimodal and reinforcement learning tasks, which inherently rely on gradient descent.
  These advancements allow us to achieve state-of-the-art results across multiple domains, including interpretable DTs rees for small tabular datasets, advanced models for complex tabular data, multimodal learning, and interpretable reinforcement learning without information loss. By bridging the gap between DTs and gradient-based optimization, our method significantly enhances the performance and applicability of tree-based models across various ML domains.

</details>


### [18] [Group Resonance Network: Learnable Prototypes and Multi-Subject Resonance for EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11119)
*Renwei Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRN通过整合个体EEG动态与离线群体共振建模，利用可学习群体原型和多被试共振分支，有效提升跨被试情绪识别的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨被试EEG情绪识别方法主要学习被试不变特征，但往往未能充分利用刺激锁定的跨被试群体规律性，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出Group Resonance Network (GRN)，包含三个组件：个体编码器提取频带特征、可学习群体原型实现原型诱导共振、多被试共振分支编码PLV/相干性同步性。通过共振感知融合模块结合个体和群体表征进行分类。

Result: 在SEED和DEAP数据集上，无论是被试内还是留一被试外协议，GRN均优于竞争基线方法。消融研究证实原型学习和多被试共振建模具有互补优势。

Conclusion: GRN通过整合个体EEG动态与群体共振建模，有效利用了刺激锁定的跨被试规律性，显著提升了跨被试EEG情绪识别的性能。

Abstract: Electroencephalography(EEG)-basedemotionrecognitionre- mains challenging in cross-subject settings due to severe inter-subject variability. Existing methods mainly learn subject-invariant features, but often under-exploit stimulus-locked group regularities shared across sub- jects. To address this issue, we propose the Group Resonance Network (GRN), which integrates individual EEG dynamics with offline group resonance modeling. GRN contains three components: an individual en- coder for band-wise EEG features, a set of learnable group prototypes for prototype-induced resonance, and a multi-subject resonance branch that encodes PLV/coherence-based synchrony with a small reference set. A resonance-aware fusion module combines individual and group-level rep- resentations for final classification. Experiments on SEED and DEAP under both subject-dependent and leave-one-subject-out protocols show that GRN consistently outperforms competitive baselines, while abla- tion studies confirm the complementary benefits of prototype learning and multi-subject resonance modeling.

</details>


### [19] [Higher-Order Modular Attention: Fusing Pairwise and Triadic Interactions for Protein Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11133)
*Shirin Amiraslani,Xin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HOMA引入高阶模块化注意力，融合成对注意力和三元交互通路，在蛋白质序列预测任务中优于标准自注意力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer自注意力计算成对token交互，但蛋白质序列到表型关系常涉及三个或更多残基的协同依赖，点积注意力无法显式捕获这些高阶相互作用

Method: 提出高阶模块化注意力(HOMA)，统一融合成对注意力与显式三元交互通路；为在长序列上实用，采用块结构、窗口化的三元注意力

Result: 在TAPE基准测试（二级结构、荧光、稳定性）上，HOMA相比标准自注意力及高效变体（块注意力、Linformer）在所有任务中均取得一致改进

Conclusion: 显式三元项为蛋白质序列预测提供了互补的表征能力，且额外计算成本可控，表明高阶相互作用对蛋白质建模的重要性

Abstract: Transformer self-attention computes pairwise token interactions, yet protein sequence to phenotype relationships often involve cooperative dependencies among three or more residues that dot product attention does not capture explicitly. We introduce Higher-Order Modular Attention, HOMA, a unified attention operator that fuses pairwise attention with an explicit triadic interaction pathway. To make triadic attention practical on long sequences, HOMA employs block-structured, windowed triadic attention. We evaluate on three TAPE benchmarks for Secondary Structure, Fluorescence, and Stability. Our attention mechanism yields consistent improvements across all tasks compared with standard self-attention and efficient variants including block-wise attention and Linformer. These results suggest that explicit triadic terms provide complementary representational capacity for protein sequence prediction at controllable additional computational cost.

</details>


### [20] [Scaling Reasoning Efficiently via Relaxed On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11137)
*Jongwoo Ko,Sara Abdali,Young Jin Kim,Tianyi Chen,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REOPOLD是一种稳定的在线蒸馏框架，通过将师生对数似然比视为token奖励，采用混合奖励裁剪、基于熵的动态采样和探索-精炼训练策略，显著提升训练效率和推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线蒸馏对于将推理能力迁移到容量受限模型至关重要，但传统方法存在不稳定性和负迁移问题。作者发现在线蒸馏可解释为一种策略优化形式，师生对数似然比可作为token奖励，这为改进蒸馏稳定性提供了理论基础。

Method: 提出REOPOLD框架：1) 混合奖励裁剪：适度选择性地利用教师奖励；2) 基于熵的token级动态采样；3) 统一的探索到精炼训练策略。这些方法放松了标准在线蒸馏的严格模仿约束，稳定了优化过程。

Result: 在数学、视觉和工具使用推理任务上，REOPOLD超越基线方法，训练时样本效率提高6.7-12倍，推理时测试扩展性能增强。7B学生模型在视觉推理上匹配32B教师，推理速度提升约3.32倍。

Conclusion: REOPOLD通过将在线蒸馏重新解释为策略优化问题，并引入放松约束的稳定化技术，有效解决了传统在线蒸馏的不稳定性问题，实现了高效的推理能力迁移和显著的性能提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is pivotal for transferring reasoning capabilities to capacity-constrained models, yet remains prone to instability and negative transfer. We show that on-policy distillation can be interpreted, both theoretically and empirically, as a form of policy optimization, where the teacher-student log-likelihood ratio acts as a token reward. From this insight, we introduce REOPOLD (Relaxed On-Policy Distillation) a framework that stabilizes optimization by relaxing the strict imitation constraints of standard on-policy distillation. Specifically, REOPOLD temperately and selectively leverages rewards from the teacher through mixture-based reward clipping, entropy-based token-level dynamic sampling, and a unified exploration-to-refinement training strategy. Empirically, REOPOLD surpasses its baselines with superior sample efficiency during training and enhanced test-time scaling at inference, across mathematical, visual, and agentic tool-use reasoning tasks. Specifically, REOPOLD outperforms recent RL approaches achieving 6.7~12x greater sample efficiency and enables a 7B student to match a 32B teacher in visual reasoning with a ~3.32x inference speedup.

</details>


### [21] [H2LooP Spark Preview: Continual Pretraining of Large Language Models for Low-Level Embedded Systems Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11139)
*Amit Singh,Vedant Nipane,Pulkit Agrawal,Jatin Kishnani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H2LooP Spark Preview，一种针对嵌入式系统编程的持续预训练管道，通过BF16 LoRA技术将OLMo-3-7B模型适配到该专业领域，在13个嵌入式领域上显著提升代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在通用编程语言上表现良好，但在嵌入式系统等专业领域受限。该领域涉及硬件寄存器操作、厂商特定SDK、实时操作系统API和硬件抽象层等，这些内容在标准预训练语料中代表性不足。

Method: 采用持续预训练管道，使用BF16 LoRA（秩稳定缩放，r=512）在8个NVIDIA H100 GPU上适配OLMo-3-7B模型。训练语料基于仓库-数据表对构建，覆盖100B原始嵌入式系统数据，使用SpecMap提出的分层数据表到代码映射方法处理，最终得到23.5B tokens的精选数据集。

Result: 持续预训练带来显著提升：域内困惑度降低70.4%，保留仓库困惑度降低66.1%。在13个嵌入式领域的生成式代码补全基准测试中，7B模型在8个类别上的token准确率超过Claude Opus 4.6和Qwen3-Coder-30B。

Conclusion: 针对性的持续预训练使较小的开源模型能够在专业技术任务上与前沿系统竞争。作者在Huggingface上发布了生产训练检查点作为开源成果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong code generation abilities in general-purpose programming languages but remain limited in specialized domains such as low-level embedded systems programming. This domain involves hardware register manipulation, vendor-specific SDKs, real-time operating system APIs, and hardware abstraction layers that are underrepresented in standard pretraining corpora. We introduce H2LooP Spark Preview, a continual pretraining (CPT) pipeline that adapts the OLMo-3-7B-a fully open language model to the embedded systems domain using BF16 LoRA with rank-stabilized scaling on 8 NVIDIA H100 GPUs. Our training corpus is constructed from repository-datasheet pairs covering 100B tokens of raw embedded systems data across 117 manufacturers, processed using the hierarchical datasheet-to-code mapping approach proposed in SpecMap (Nipane et al., 2026). The resulting curated dataset split contains 23.5B tokens across 13 embedded domains. Continual pretraining with high-rank LoRA (r=512) yields substantial gains, reducing in-domain perplexity by 70.4% and held-out repository perplexity by 66.1%. On generative code completion benchmarks spanning 13 embedded domains, our 7B model outperforms Claude Opus 4.6 and Qwen3-Coder-30B on 8 categories in token accuracy, showing that targeted continual pretraining enables smaller open-weight models to rival frontier systems on specialized technical tasks. We release the production training checkpoint on Huggingface as an open-source artifact.

</details>


### [22] [Procedural Fairness via Group Counterfactual Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11140)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GCIG是一种通过正则化强制解释跨群体不变性的方法，将程序公平性定义为群体反事实解释稳定性，在保持预测性能的同时显著减少跨群体解释差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习公平性研究主要关注结果导向的公平标准（如Equalized Odds），而对程序导向的公平性关注较少。忽视程序公平性可能导致模型为不同受保护群体生成不同的解释，从而侵蚀信任。

Method: 提出Group Counterfactual Integrated Gradients (GCIG)，这是一种处理中正则化框架，强制跨群体的解释不变性（以真实标签为条件）。GCIG为每个输入计算相对于多个群体条件基线的解释，并在训练期间惩罚这些归因的跨群体变化。

Result: GCIG与六种最先进方法相比，在保持竞争性预测性能和准确率-公平性权衡的同时，显著减少了跨群体解释差异。结果表明，跨群体对齐模型推理为实现超越结果平等的公平性提供了原则性和实用的途径。

Conclusion: GCIG将程序公平性形式化为群体反事实解释稳定性，补充了仅约束预测的现有公平性目标。跨群体对齐模型推理为实现超越结果平等的公平性提供了原则性和实用的途径。

Abstract: Fairness in machine learning research has largely focused on outcome-oriented fairness criteria such as Equalized Odds, while comparatively less attention has been given to procedural-oriented fairness, which addresses how a model arrives at its predictions. Neglecting procedural fairness means it is possible for a model to generate different explanations for different protected groups, thereby eroding trust. In this work, we introduce Group Counterfactual Integrated Gradients (GCIG), an in-processing regularization framework that enforces explanation invariance across groups, conditioned on the true label. For each input, GCIG computes explanations relative to multiple Group Conditional baselines and penalizes cross-group variation in these attributions during training. GCIG formalizes procedural fairness as Group Counterfactual explanation stability and complements existing fairness objectives that constrain predictions alone. We compared GCIG empirically against six state-of-the-art methods, and the results show that GCIG substantially reduces cross-group explanation disparity while maintaining competitive predictive performance and accuracy-fairness trade-offs. Our results also show that aligning model reasoning across groups offers a principled and practical avenue for advancing fairness beyond outcome parity.

</details>


### [23] [Attention Gathers, MLPs Compose: A Causal Analysis of an Action-Outcome Circuit in VideoViT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11142)
*Sai V R Chereddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视频分类模型内部存在计算"成功vs失败"信号的专用电路，通过注意力头收集证据、MLP块组合概念，形成分布式冗余计算结构，揭示了模型训练中产生的超越显式任务的"隐藏知识"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索视频分类模型如何表示不影响最终分类结果的细微语义信息，这是可信AI模型的关键挑战。研究模型内部是否形成了超越训练任务的复杂表示能力，为构建可解释、可信的AI系统提供机制理解。

Method: 使用机制可解释性技术，在预训练的视频视觉变换器中逆向工程"成功vs失败"信号的计算电路。主要采用因果分析，特别是激活修补技术，辅以消融实验结果，分析注意力头和MLP块的功能分工。

Result: 发现"成功vs失败"信号通过独特的放大级联计算：从第0层开始有低层差异，但从第5到11层逐步放大抽象语义表示。注意力头作为"证据收集器"提供低层信息，MLP块作为"概念组合器"生成主要信号。电路具有分布式和冗余特性，对简单消融具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 即使在仅训练简单分类任务的模型中，也存在表示复杂结果的精密电路，表明模型可能发展出超越显式任务的"隐藏知识"。这强调了机制监督对于构建真正可解释、可信的AI部署系统的必要性。

Abstract: The paper explores how video models trained for classification tasks represent nuanced, hidden semantic information that may not affect the final outcome, a key challenge for Trustworthy AI models. Through Explainable and Interpretable AI methods, specifically mechanistic interpretability techniques, the internal circuit responsible for representing the action's outcome is reverse-engineered in a pre-trained video vision transformer, revealing that the "Success vs Failure" signal is computed through a distinct amplification cascade. While there are low-level differences observed from layer 0, the abstract and semantic representation of the outcome is progressively amplified from layers 5 through 11. Causal analysis, primarily using activation patching supported by ablation results, reveals a clear division of labor: Attention Heads act as "evidence gatherers", providing necessary low-level information for partial signal recovery, while MLP Blocks function as robust "concept composers", each of which is the primary driver to generate the "success" signal. This distributed and redundant circuit in the model's internals explains its resilience to simple ablations, demonstrating a core computational pattern for processing human-action outcomes. Crucially, the existence of this sophisticated circuit for representing complex outcomes, even within a model trained only for simple classification, highlights the potential for models to develop forms of 'hidden knowledge' beyond their explicit task, underscoring the need for mechanistic oversight for building genuinely Explainable and Trustworthy AI systems intended for deployment.

</details>


### [24] [Systematic Scaling Analysis of Jailbreak Attacks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11149)
*Xiangwen Wang,Ananth Balashankar,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于计算预算的越狱攻击缩放定律框架，系统评估了不同攻击方法在不同模型和危害类型上的效率差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对大型语言模型越狱攻击的理解缺乏系统性，特别是攻击成功率如何随攻击者计算预算在不同攻击方法、模型家族和危害类型上变化。需要建立统一的评估框架来量化攻击效率。

Method: 提出了基于FLOPs（浮点运算次数）的缩放定律框架，将各种越狱攻击视为计算受限的优化过程。系统评估了四种代表性攻击范式：基于优化的攻击、自我精炼提示、基于采样的选择和遗传优化。通过拟合饱和指数函数来分析FLOPs与攻击成功率的关系，并推导可比较的效率指标。

Result: 1. 提示类方法在计算效率上优于优化类方法；2. 攻击方法在成功率-隐蔽性操作点上分布不同，提示类方法占据高成功率、高隐蔽性区域；3. 漏洞具有强烈的目标依赖性，涉及错误信息的危害比其他非错误信息危害更容易引发。

Conclusion: 通过建立统一的FLOPs轴评估框架，揭示了越狱攻击的效率差异和模式。提示类攻击在计算效率和隐蔽性方面具有优势，而模型对不同类型危害的脆弱性存在显著差异，这为防御策略的设计提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Large language models remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, yet we still lack a systematic understanding of how jailbreak success scales with attacker effort across methods, model families, and harm types. We initiate a scaling-law framework for jailbreaks by treating each attack as a compute-bounded optimization procedure and measuring progress on a shared FLOPs axis. Our systematic evaluation spans four representative jailbreak paradigms, covering optimization-based attacks, self-refinement prompting, sampling-based selection, and genetic optimization, across multiple model families and scales on a diverse set of harmful goals. We investigate scaling laws that relate attacker budget to attack success score by fitting a simple saturating exponential function to FLOPs--success trajectories, and we derive comparable efficiency summaries from the fitted curves. Empirically, prompting-based paradigms tend to be the most compute-efficient compared to optimization-based methods. To explain this gap, we cast prompt-based updates into an optimization view and show via a same-state comparison that prompt-based attacks more effectively optimize in prompt space. We also show that attacks occupy distinct success--stealthiness operating points with prompting-based methods occupying the high-success, high-stealth region. Finally, we find that vulnerability is strongly goal-dependent: harms involving misinformation are typically easier to elicit than other non-misinformation harms.

</details>


### [25] [Huntington Disease Automatic Speech Recognition with Biomarker Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11168)
*Charles L. Wang,Cady Chen,Ziwei Gong,Julia Hirschberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了亨廷顿病（HD）病理语音的自动语音识别（ASR），通过临床语音语料库比较多种ASR架构，发现Parakeet-TDT表现最佳，并提出HD特异性适应方法将WER从6.99%降至4.95%，同时分析了基于生物标志物的辅助监督如何重塑错误模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理语音的自动语音识别（ASR）研究不足，特别是亨廷顿病（HD）语音具有不规则时序、不稳定发声和发音畸变等特征，对现有模型构成挑战。需要系统研究HD-ASR并开发有效的适应方法。

Method: 使用先前未用于端到端ASR训练的高保真临床语音语料库，在统一评估框架下比较多种ASR架构家族，分析WER以及替换、删除和插入错误模式。提出HD特异性适应方法，包括基于生物标志物的辅助监督策略。

Result: HD语音导致架构特定的错误模式，Parakeet-TDT优于编码器-解码器和CTC基线。HD特异性适应将WER从6.99%降低至4.95%。基于生物标志物的辅助监督以严重程度依赖的方式重塑错误行为，而非均匀改善WER。

Conclusion: 该研究为HD病理语音ASR提供了系统分析框架，展示了架构选择和特定适应策略的重要性。提出的方法不仅降低WER，还揭示了错误模式与疾病严重程度的关系。所有代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) for pathological speech remains underexplored, especially for Huntington's disease (HD), where irregular timing, unstable phonation, and articulatory distortion challenge current models. We present a systematic HD-ASR study using a high-fidelity clinical speech corpus not previously used for end-to-end ASR training. We compare multiple ASR families under a unified evaluation, analyzing WER as well as substitution, deletion, and insertion patterns. HD speech induces architecture-specific error regimes, with Parakeet-TDT outperforming encoder-decoder and CTC baselines. HD-specific adaptation reduces WER from 6.99% to 4.95% and we also propose a method for using biomarker-based auxiliary supervision and analyze how error behavior is reshaped in severity-dependent ways rather than uniformly improving WER. We open-source all code and models.

</details>


### [26] [Bayesian Optimization of Partially Known Systems using Hybrid Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11199)
*Eike Cramer,Luis Kutschat,Oliver Stollenwerk,Joel A. Paulson,Alexander Mitsos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合贝叶斯优化与部分已知物理机理模型的混合建模方法，用于昂贵黑箱系统的高效优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化在高维非线性系统中收敛缓慢，需要大量实验，而完全机理模型又难以获得。需要结合已知物理知识与数据驱动方法的优势。

Method: 构建混合模型：将已知物理方程作为约束，对缺失方程部分使用高斯过程建模。最终形成带约束的非线性随机规划问题，采用样本平均近似进行离散化求解。

Result: 在单级蒸馏的仿真优化中，基于质量守恒定律的混合贝叶斯优化模型显著优于标准贝叶斯优化，能在少至1次迭代内收敛，而标准方法在25次迭代内均未收敛。

Conclusion: 提出的混合贝叶斯优化方案为部分已知系统提供了一种有前景的优化方法，有效结合了机理建模和数据驱动优化的优势。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) has gained attention as an efficient algorithm for black-box optimization of expensive-to-evaluate systems, where the BO algorithm iteratively queries the system and suggests new trials based on a probabilistic model fitted to previous samples. Still, the standard BO loop may require a prohibitively large number of experiments to converge to the optimum, especially for high-dimensional and nonlinear systems. We present a hybrid model-based BO formulation that combines the iterative Bayesian learning of BO with partially known mechanistic physical models. Instead of learning a direct mapping from inputs to the objective, we write all known equations for a physics-based model and infer expressions for variables missing equations using a probabilistic model, in our case, a Gaussian process (GP). The final formulation then includes the GP as a constraint in the hybrid model, thereby allowing other physics-based nonlinear and implicit model constraints. This hybrid model formulation yields a constrained, nonlinear stochastic program, which we discretize using the sample-average approximation. In an in-silico optimization of a single-stage distillation, the hybrid BO model based on mass conservation laws yields significantly better designs than a standard BO loop. Furthermore, the hybrid model converges in as few as one iteration, depending on the initial samples, whereas, the standard BO does not converge within 25 for any of the seeds. Overall, the proposed hybrid BO scheme presents a promising optimization method for partially known systems, leveraging the strengths of both mechanistic modeling and data-driven optimization.

</details>


### [27] [Representation Finetuning for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11201)
*Haihua Luo,Xuming Ran,Tommi Kärkkäinen,Huiyan Xue,Zhonghua Chen,Qi Xu,Fengyu Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoRe框架将微调从权重空间转移到表示空间，通过在隐藏表示的低秩线性子空间进行任务特定干预，实现参数高效且抗遗忘的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前参数高效微调方法在权重层面进行黑盒优化，缺乏对表示漂移的显式控制，导致对领域偏移敏感且在持续学习场景中容易发生灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出Continual Representation Learning框架，在隐藏表示的低秩线性子空间中进行任务特定干预，采用具有明确目标的学习过程，确保对过去任务的稳定性同时保持对新任务的可塑性。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准测试中，CoRe不仅保持了参数效率，还显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 表示微调作为一种新的、更有效且可解释的范式，为持续学习提供了新的方向。

Abstract: The world is inherently dynamic, and continual learning aims to enable models to adapt to ever-evolving data streams. While pre-trained models have shown powerful performance in continual learning, they still require finetuning to adapt effectively to downstream tasks. However, prevailing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) methods operate through empirical, black-box optimization at the weight level. These approaches lack explicit control over representation drift, leading to sensitivity to domain shifts and catastrophic forgetting in continual learning scenarios. In this work, we introduce Continual Representation Learning (CoRe), a novel framework that for the first time shifts the finetuning paradigm from weight space to representation space. Unlike conventional methods, CoRe performs task-specific interventions within a low-rank linear subspace of hidden representations, adopting a learning process with explicit objectives, which ensures stability for past tasks while maintaining plasticity for new ones. By constraining updates to a low-rank subspace, CoRe achieves exceptional parameter efficiency. Extensive experiments across multiple continual learning benchmarks demonstrate that CoRe not only preserves parameter efficiency but also significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. Our work introduces representation finetuning as a new, more effective and interpretable paradigm for continual learning.

</details>


### [28] [Reference-Guided Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11210)
*Jonas Mirlach,Sonia Laguna,Julia E. Vogt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGUn提出了一种基于分布不可区分性的机器遗忘框架，利用独立保留数据集提供类别条件参考，在遗忘-效用权衡上优于现有近似基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似遗忘方法依赖性能退化启发式（如损失最大化或随机标记），这些信号可能条件不佳，导致优化不稳定并损害模型泛化能力。作者认为遗忘应优先考虑分布不可区分性，使模型在遗忘数据上的行为与其在真正未见数据上的行为一致。

Method: 提出Reference-Guided Unlearning (ReGUn)框架，利用不相交的保留数据集提供原则性的类别条件参考进行蒸馏。该方法通过分布对齐实现遗忘，而非依赖性能退化启发式。

Result: 在各种模型架构、自然图像数据集和不同遗忘比例下，ReGUn始终优于标准近似基线方法，实现了更优的遗忘-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 基于分布不可区分性的遗忘方法比依赖性能退化启发式的方法更有效，ReGUn框架通过利用保留数据集提供参考，实现了稳定优化和更好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific data from trained models while preserving general utility. Existing approximate unlearning methods often rely on performance-degradation heuristics, such as loss maximization or random labeling. However, these signals can be poorly conditioned, leading to unstable optimization and harming the model's generalization. We argue that unlearning should instead prioritize distributional indistinguishability, aligning the model's behavior on forget data with its behavior on truly unseen data. Motivated by this, we propose Reference-Guided Unlearning (ReGUn), a framework that leverages a disjoint held-out dataset to provide a principled, class-conditioned reference for distillation. We demonstrate across various model architectures, natural image datasets, and varying forget fractions that ReGUn consistently outperforms standard approximate baselines, achieving a superior forgetting-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [29] [Monitoring and Prediction of Mood in Elderly People during Daily Life Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11230)
*Daniel Bautista-Salinas,Joaquín Roca González,Inmaculada Méndez,Oscar Martinez Mozos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用于监测和预测老年人日常活动中情绪状态的智能可穿戴系统，结合腕带生理数据采集和移动应用生态瞬时评估，使用机器学习进行情绪状态自动分类


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够监测和预测老年人日常活动中情绪状态的智能可穿戴系统，以改善老年人的心理健康监测和干预

Method: 系统由记录生理活动的腕带和用于生态瞬时评估的移动应用组成，使用机器学习训练分类器基于智能腕带数据自动预测不同情绪状态

Result: 在情绪识别准确性方面显示出有希望的结果，在特定幸福感（happiness）和活跃度（activeness）检测方面与现有技术水平相当

Conclusion: 提出的智能可穿戴系统能够有效监测和预测老年人的情绪状态，为老年人心理健康监测提供了可行的技术方案

Abstract: We present an intelligent wearable system to monitor and predict mood states of elderly people during their daily life activities. Our system is composed of a wristband to record different physiological activities together with a mobile app for ecological momentary assessment (EMA). Machine learning is used to train a classifier to automatically predict different mood states based on the smart band only. Our approach shows promising results on mood accuracy and provides results comparable with the state of the art in the specific detection of happiness and activeness.

</details>


### [30] [Beyond the Class Subspace: Teacher-Guided Training for Reliable Out-of-Distribution Detection in Single-Domain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11269)
*Hong Yang,Devroop Kar,Qi Yu,Travis Desell,Alex Ororbia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种解决单域训练中域敏感性崩溃问题的新方法TGT，通过多域教师蒸馏来增强OOD检测能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前OOD检测方法在多域基准测试上表现良好，但实际系统通常在单域数据上训练。作者发现这种训练机制会导致域敏感性崩溃（DSC），即监督训练将特征压缩到低秩类子空间中，抑制了携带域偏移信号的方向，从而降低距离和logit基OOD评分对域偏移的敏感性。

Method: 提出教师引导训练（TGT），在训练期间从冻结的多域教师（DINOv2）向学生模型蒸馏类别抑制的残差结构。教师和辅助头在训练后被丢弃，不增加推理开销。

Result: 在八个单域基准测试中，TGT显著改善了距离基评分器的远OOD FPR@95：MDS提高11.61个百分点，ViM提高10.78个百分点，kNN提高12.87个百分点（ResNet-50平均值），同时保持或略微改善域内OOD检测和分类准确率。

Conclusion: TGT方法有效解决了单域训练中的域敏感性崩溃问题，通过多域教师蒸馏增强了OOD检测能力，且不增加推理开销，为实际部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection methods perform well on multi-domain benchmarks, yet many practical systems are trained on single-domain data. We show that this regime induces a geometric failure mode, Domain-Sensitivity Collapse (DSC): supervised training compresses features into a low-rank class subspace and suppresses directions that carry domain-shift signal. We provide theory showing that, under DSC, distance- and logit-based OOD scores lose sensitivity to domain shift. We then introduce Teacher-Guided Training (TGT), which distills class-suppressed residual structure from a frozen multi-domain teacher (DINOv2) into the student during training. The teacher and auxiliary head are discarded after training, adding no inference overhead. Across eight single-domain benchmarks, TGT yields large far-OOD FPR@95 reductions for distance-based scorers: MDS improves by 11.61 pp, ViM by 10.78 pp, and kNN by 12.87 pp (ResNet-50 average), while maintaining or slightly improving in-domain OOD and classification accuracy.

</details>


### [31] [KEPo: Knowledge Evolution Poison on Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11501)
*Qizhi Chen,Chao Qi,Yihong Huang,Muquan Li,Rongzheng Wang,Dongyang Zhang,Ke Qin,Shuang Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出针对GraphRAG的知识演化投毒攻击方法KEPo，通过伪造事件背景和知识演化路径，在知识图谱中植入有毒知识，误导LLM生成目标有害回答


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphRAG通过构建知识图谱增强LLM的时效性和准确性，但其对外部数据的依赖引入了新的攻击面。现有攻击方法主要针对传统RAG系统，对GraphRAG无效，因为知识图谱抽象会重组注入文本，使LLM基于重构上下文而非原始有毒段落进行推理。需要揭示GraphRAG的潜在安全漏洞。

Method: 提出知识演化投毒攻击方法KEPo：1）针对目标查询生成包含投毒知识的有毒事件；2）伪造事件背景和从原始事实到有毒事件的知识演化路径；3）投毒知识图谱，误导LLM将投毒知识视为最终结果。在多目标攻击场景中，进一步连接多个攻击语料库，使投毒知识相互增强并扩大投毒社区规模。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验结果表明，KEPo在单目标和多目标攻击中均实现了最先进的攻击成功率，显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: GraphRAG虽然通过知识图谱抽象增强了对抗传统RAG攻击的鲁棒性，但仍存在被专门设计的投毒攻击方法（如KEPo）利用的潜在安全漏洞。该方法通过伪造知识演化路径有效绕过GraphRAG的防御机制，揭示了知识图谱增强型RAG系统的安全风险。

Abstract: Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) constructs the Knowledge Graph (KG) from external databases to enhance the timeliness and accuracy of Large Language Model (LLM) generations.However,this reliance on external data introduces new attack surfaces.Attackers can inject poisoned texts into databases to manipulate LLMs into producing harmful target responses for attacker-chosen queries.Existing research primarily focuses on attacking conventional RAG systems.However,such methods are ineffective against GraphRAG.This robustness derives from the KG abstraction of GraphRAG,which reorganizes injected text into a graph before retrieval,thereby enabling the LLM to reason based on the restructured context instead of raw poisoned passages.To expose latent security vulnerabilities in GraphRAG,we propose Knowledge Evolution Poison (KEPo),a novel poisoning attack method specifically designed for GraphRAG.For each target query,KEPo first generates a toxic event containing poisoned knowledge based on the target answer.By fabricating event backgrounds and forging knowledge evolution paths from original facts to the toxic event,it then poisons the KG and misleads the LLM into treating the poisoned knowledge as the final result.In multi-target attack scenarios,KEPo further connects multiple attack corpora,enabling their poisoned knowledge to mutually reinforce while expanding the scale of poisoned communities,thereby amplifying attack effectiveness.Experimental results across multiple datasets demonstrate that KEPo achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates for both single-target and multi-target attacks,significantly outperforming previous methods.

</details>


### [32] [Exponential-Family Membership Inference: From LiRA and RMIA to BaVarIA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11799)
*Rickard Brännvall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文统一了三种成员推理攻击方法（LiRA、RMIA、BASE），提出了一个指数族对数似然比框架，并基于此开发了BaVarIA攻击方法，在低影子模型预算下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前成员推理攻击方法（LiRA、RMIA、BASE）看似使用不同的评分策略，且BASE被证明与RMIA等价，这使得实践者难以选择合适的方法。需要统一这些方法并解决在小影子模型预算下的性能瓶颈问题。

Method: 1. 提出统一的指数族对数似然比框架，将LiRA、RMIA、BASE视为该框架的不同实例，仅区别在于分布假设和每个数据点估计的参数数量；2. 建立层次结构（BASE1-4）连接RMIA和LiRA作为模型复杂度谱系的端点；3. 识别方差估计为小影子模型预算下的关键瓶颈；4. 提出BaVarIA（贝叶斯方差推理攻击），使用共轭正态-逆伽马先验替代基于阈值的参数切换；5. 生成学生t预测（BaVarIA-t）或具有稳定方差的高斯分布（BaVarIA-n）。

Result: 在12个数据集和7种影子模型预算下，BaVarIA匹配或优于LiRA和RMIA，在实践重要的低影子模型和离线场景中获得最大性能提升。BaVarIA无需额外超参数调优即可提供稳定性能。

Conclusion: 该研究统一了主要成员推理攻击方法，揭示了它们的内在联系，并通过贝叶斯方差估计解决了小影子模型预算下的性能瓶颈问题。BaVarIA在低预算场景中表现优异，为隐私审计提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are becoming standard tools for auditing the privacy of machine learning models. The leading attacks -- LiRA (Carlini et al., 2022) and RMIA (Zarifzadeh et al., 2024) -- appear to use distinct scoring strategies, while the recently proposed BASE (Lassila et al., 2025) was shown to be equivalent to RMIA, making it difficult for practitioners to choose among them. We show that all three are instances of a single exponential-family log-likelihood ratio framework, differing only in their distributional assumptions and the number of parameters estimated per data point. This unification reveals a hierarchy (BASE1-4) that connects RMIA and LiRA as endpoints of a spectrum of increasing model complexity. Within this framework, we identify variance estimation as the key bottleneck at small shadow-model budgets and propose BaVarIA, a Bayesian variance inference attack that replaces threshold-based parameter switching with conjugate normal-inverse-gamma priors. BaVarIA yields a Student-t predictive (BaVarIA-t) or a Gaussian with stabilized variance (BaVarIA-n), providing stable performance without additional hyperparameter tuning. Across 12 datasets and 7 shadow-model budgets, BaVarIA matches or improves upon LiRA and RMIA, with the largest gains in the practically important low-shadow-model and offline regimes.

</details>


### [33] [Client-Conditional Federated Learning via Local Training Data Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11307)
*Rickard Brännvall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种联邦学习方法，通过将全局模型基于各客户端的本地PCA统计数据进行条件化，无需额外通信，在多种异构数据场景下性能优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法在处理数据异构性时存在局限：FedAvg忽略客户端差异，IFCA需要昂贵的聚类发现，Ditto维护每个客户端的模型。当数据稀疏或异构性多维时，这些方法性能下降

Method: 提出条件化单个全局模型的方法，基于各客户端训练数据的本地计算PCA统计数据进行条件化，无需额外通信开销

Result: 在97种配置（4种异构类型×4个数据集×7个基线方法）的评估中，该方法在所有设置下匹配知道真实聚类分配的Oracle基线，在组合异构性场景下超越Oracle 1-6%，且在所有测试方法中唯一具有稀疏性鲁棒性

Conclusion: 通过基于本地PCA统计条件化全局模型的方法，能够在无需额外通信的情况下有效处理联邦学习中的数据异构性问题，在多种异构场景下表现优异且具有稀疏性鲁棒性

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) under data heterogeneity remains challenging: existing methods either ignore client differences (FedAvg), require costly cluster discovery (IFCA), or maintain per-client models (Ditto). All degrade when data is sparse or heterogeneity is multi-dimensional. We propose conditioning a single global model on locally-computed PCA statistics of each client's training data, requiring zero additional communication. Evaluating across 97~configurations spanning four heterogeneity types (label shift, covariate shift, concept shift, and combined heterogeneity), four datasets (MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100), and seven FL baseline methods, we find that our method matches the Oracle baseline -- which knows true cluster assignments -- across all settings, surpasses it by 1--6% on combined heterogeneity where continuous statistics are richer than discrete cluster identifiers, and is uniquely sparsity-robust among all tested methods.

</details>


### [34] [STAMP: Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12237)
*Fengwei Tian,Payel Bhattacharjee,Heidi Hanson,Geoffrey D. Rubin,Joseph Y. Lo,Ravi Tandon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAMP框架通过选择性分配隐私预算和极坐标机制，在文本隐私化中实现了更好的隐私-效用平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本隐私化方法通常在隐私保护和下游任务效用之间存在权衡问题，需要更精细的控制机制来平衡不同token的隐私敏感性和任务重要性

Method: STAMP框架包含两个核心组件：1) 选择性隐私预算分配，基于token的任务重要性和隐私敏感性；2) 极坐标机制，仅扰动单位球面上嵌入向量的方向而保持幅度不变，通过余弦最近邻搜索进行解码

Result: 在SQuAD、Yelp和AG News数据集上的实验表明，STAMP结合归一化极坐标机制在不同token隐私预算下始终实现优越的隐私-效用权衡

Conclusion: STAMP通过细粒度的token级隐私预算分配和几何对齐的扰动机制，为任务感知的文本隐私化提供了有效的解决方案，显著改善了隐私保护与下游任务性能之间的平衡

Abstract: We present STAMP (Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy), a new framework for task-aware text privatization that achieves an improved privacy-utility trade-off. STAMP selectively allocates privacy budgets across tokens by jointly considering (i) each token's importance to the downstream task (as measured via a task- or query-specific representation), and (ii) its privacy sensitivity (e.g., names, dates, identifiers). This token-level partitioning enables fine-grained, group-wise control over the level of noise applied to different parts of the input, balancing privacy protection with task relevance. To privatize individual token embeddings, we introduce the polar mechanism, which perturbs only the direction of embeddings on the unit sphere while preserving their magnitude. Decoding is performed via cosine nearest-neighbor search, aligning the perturbation geometry with the decoding geometry. Unlike isotropic noise mechanisms, the polar mechanism maintains semantic neighborhoods in the embedding space and better preserves downstream utility. Experimental evaluations on SQuAD, Yelp, and AG News datasets demonstrate that STAMP, when combined with the normalized polar mechanism, consistently achieves superior privacy-utility trade-offs across varying per-token privacy budgets.

</details>


### [35] [On the Robustness of Langevin Dynamics to Score Function Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11319)
*Daniel Yiming Cao,August Y. Chen,Karthik Sridharan,Yuchen Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明Langevin动力学对分数函数估计中的L^2误差不鲁棒，即使误差任意小，多项式时间内也会产生远离目标分布的样本，这为扩散模型提供了进一步的理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于分数的生成建模对分数函数估计误差的鲁棒性。在实践中，从数据中学习分数函数时不可避免地存在估计误差，需要理解不同采样方法对这种误差的敏感性。

Method: 通过理论分析证明Langevin动力学对L^2误差（更一般地L^p误差）的非鲁棒性。对比扩散模型在相同误差条件下的表现，展示两者在鲁棒性方面的根本差异。

Result: 即使对于高维简单分布，当分数函数估计存在任意小的L^2误差时，Langevin动力学在任何多项式时间内都会产生与目标分布在总变差距离上远离的分布。这与扩散模型在相同条件下的良好表现形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 研究结果为选择扩散模型而非Langevin动力学提供了进一步的理论依据，并警示在实际应用中使用带有估计分数的Langevin动力学的风险。分数函数估计误差在实践中不可避免，因此鲁棒性成为选择采样方法的关键考量因素。

Abstract: We consider the robustness of score-based generative modeling to errors in the estimate of the score function. In particular, we show that Langevin dynamics is not robust to the L^2 errors (more generally L^p errors) in the estimate of the score function. It is well-established that with small L^2 errors in the estimate of the score function, diffusion models can sample faithfully from the target distribution under fairly mild regularity assumptions in a polynomial time horizon. In contrast, our work shows that even for simple distributions in high dimensions, Langevin dynamics run for any polynomial time horizon will produce a distribution far from the target distribution in Total Variation (TV) distance, even when the L^2 error (more generally L^p) of the estimate of the score function is arbitrarily small. Considering such an error in the estimate of the score function is unavoidable in practice when learning the score function from data, our results provide further justification for diffusion models over Langevin dynamics and serve to caution against the use of Langevin dynamics with estimated scores.

</details>


### [36] [Hindsight-Anchored Policy Optimization: Turning Failure into Feedback in Sparse Reward Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11321)
*Yuning Wu,Ke Wang,Devin Chen,Kai Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HAPO通过后见之锚定策略优化解决GRPO在稀疏奖励环境中的困境，使用合成成功注入机制选择性锚定教师演示，实现渐进一致性并避免分布偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于组的可验证奖励强化学习方法（如GRPO）在稀疏奖励环境中面临关键困境：纯RL方法存在优势崩溃和高方差梯度估计问题，而混合策略优化则引入持续的分布偏差。

Method: 提出后见之锚定策略优化（HAPO），采用合成成功注入（SSI）算子作为后见机制，在失败时选择性锚定到教师演示。该注入由受汤普森采样启发的门控机制控制，创建自主、自适应的课程学习。

Result: 理论上证明HAPO实现渐进一致性：随着策略改进自然衰减教师信号，HAPO恢复无偏的在线策略梯度。这确保离策略指导作为临时支架而非永久上限，使模型超越静态教师强制的限制。

Conclusion: HAPO解决了GRPO在稀疏奖励环境中的困境，通过合成成功注入机制实现渐进一致性，使离策略指导作为临时支架，最终使模型能够超越教师演示的限制。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a promising paradigm for post-training reasoning models. However, group-based methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) face a critical dilemma in sparse-reward settings: pure Reinforcement Learning (RL) suffers from advantage collapse and high-variance gradient estimation, while mixed-policy optimization introduces persistent distributional bias. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce Hindsight-Anchored Policy Optimization (HAPO). HAPO employs the Synthetic Success Injection (SSI) operator, a hindsight mechanism that selectively anchors optimization to teacher demonstrations during failure. This injection is governed by a Thompson sampling-inspired gating mechanism, creating an autonomous, self-paced curriculum. Theoretically, we demonstrate that HAPO achieves \textit{asymptotic consistency}: by naturally annealing the teacher signal as the policy improves, HAPO recovers the unbiased on-policy gradient. This ensures off-policy guidance acts as a temporary scaffold rather than a persistent ceiling, enabling the model to surpass the limitations of static teacher forcing.

</details>


### [37] [Meta-Reinforcement Learning with Self-Reflection for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11327)
*Teng Xiao,Yige Yuan,Hamish Ivison,Huaisheng Zhu,Faeze Brahman,Nathan Lambert,Pradeep Dasigi,Noah A. Smith,Hannaneh Hajishirzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MR-Search是一种基于上下文元强化学习的智能搜索框架，通过跨回合自我反思提升探索效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习在单回合稀疏奖励环境下优化策略效果有限，需要一种能够跨回合学习并适应搜索策略的方法，使智能体能够在测试时通过上下文学习改进探索能力

Method: 提出MR-Search框架：1) 训练策略时考虑历史回合作为上下文；2) 每回合后生成显式自我反思作为后续尝试的额外指导；3) 引入多回合RL算法，在回合级别估计密集相对优势，实现细粒度信用分配

Result: 在多个基准测试中，MR-Search相比基于RL的基线方法表现出显著优势，相对改进率达到9.2%到19.3%，展现出强大的泛化能力

Conclusion: MR-Search通过跨回合自我反思和上下文元强化学习，有效提升了智能搜索的探索效率和性能，为agentic search提供了新的解决方案

Abstract: This paper introduces MR-Search, an in-context meta reinforcement learning (RL) formulation for agentic search with self-reflection. Instead of optimizing a policy within a single independent episode with sparse rewards, MR-Search trains a policy that conditions on past episodes and adapts its search strategy across episodes. MR-Search learns to learn a search strategy with self-reflection, allowing search agents to improve in-context exploration at test-time. Specifically, MR-Search performs cross-episode exploration by generating explicit self-reflections after each episode and leveraging them as additional context to guide subsequent attempts, thereby promoting more effective exploration during test-time. We further introduce a multi-turn RL algorithm that estimates a dense relative advantage at the turn level, enabling fine-grained credit assignment on each episode. Empirical results across various benchmarks demonstrate the advantages of MR-Search over baselines based RL, showing strong generalization and relative improvements of 9.2% to 19.3% across eight benchmarks. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/tengxiao1/MR-Search.

</details>


### [38] [Teleodynamic Learning a new Paradigm For Interpretable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11355)
*Enrique ter Horst,Juan Diego Zambrano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Teleodynamic Learning新范式，将学习视为约束下功能组织的涌现与稳定，而非固定目标的最小化，基于三个耦合演化量：表示能力、参数适应和内部资源可持续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生命系统启发，传统优化方法无法自然捕捉自稳定、结构化学习动态和基于信息几何的收敛保证等现象，需要统一正则化、架构搜索和资源受限推理的新学习框架。

Method: 将学习形式化为具有两个交互时间尺度的约束动态过程：内部动态用于连续参数适应，外部动态用于离散结构变化，通过内生资源变量连接。实例化为Distinction Engine (DE11)，基于Spencer-Brown的《形式法则》、信息几何和热带优化。

Result: DE11在标准基准测试中取得：IRIS 93.3%、WINE 92.6%、乳腺癌数据集94.7%的测试准确率，同时产生从学习动态中内生产生的可解释逻辑规则，而非手动设计。

Conclusion: Teleodynamic Learning在单一原则下统一了正则化、架构搜索和资源受限推理：学习作为结构、参数和资源在约束下的协同演化，为自适应、可解释和自组织AI开辟了热力学基础路径。

Abstract: We introduce Teleodynamic Learning, a new paradigm for machine learning in which learning is not the minimization of a fixed objective, but the emergence and stabilization of functional organization under constraint. Inspired by living systems, this framework treats intelligence as the coupled evolution of three quantities: what a system can represent, how it adapts its parameters, and which changes its internal resources can sustain. We formalize learning as a constrained dynamical process with two interacting timescales: inner dynamics for continuous parameter adaptation and outer dynamics for discrete structural change, linked by an endogenous resource variable that both shapes and is shaped by the trajectory. This perspective reveals three phenomena that standard optimization does not naturally capture: self-stabilization without externally imposed stopping rules, phase-structured learning dynamics that move from under-structuring through teleodynamic growth to over-structuring, and convergence guarantees grounded in information geometry rather than convexity. We instantiate the framework in the Distinction Engine (DE11), a teleodynamic learner grounded in Spencer-Brown's Laws of Form, information geometry, and tropical optimization. On standard benchmarks, DE11 achieves 93.3 percent test accuracy on IRIS, 92.6 percent on WINE, and 94.7 percent on Breast Cancer, while producing interpretable logical rules that arise endogenously from the learning dynamics rather than being imposed by hand. More broadly, Teleodynamic Learning unifies regularization, architecture search, and resource-bounded inference within a single principle: learning as the co-evolution of structure, parameters, and resources under constraint. This opens a thermodynamically grounded route to adaptive, interpretable, and self-organizing AI.

</details>


### [39] [Multilingual Financial Fraud Detection Using Machine Learning and Transformer Models: A Bangla-English Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11358)
*Mohammad Shihab Uddin,Md Hasibul Amin,Nusrat Jahan Ema,Bushra Uddin,Tanvir Ahmed,Arif Hassan Zidan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在孟加拉语-英语多语言环境下使用机器学习进行金融欺诈检测，发现传统线性SVM模型在准确率和F1分数上优于Transformer模型，同时分析了欺诈消息的语言特征模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前金融欺诈检测研究主要集中在英语数据上，而孟加拉语（使用者超过2.5亿）在该领域几乎未被探索。随着数字金融平台的快速发展，需要解决多语言环境下的欺诈检测问题，特别是针对低资源语言。

Method: 研究使用包含合法和欺诈金融消息的数据集，评估了传统机器学习模型（逻辑回归、线性SVM、集成分类器）使用TF-IDF特征，以及基于Transformer的架构。采用5折分层交叉验证进行实验。
