Many practical optimisation heuristics implicitly navigate this trade-off in different ways -- for instance, some aim to whiten gradient noise, while others aim to align updates with expected loss curvature. When the geometry of the population risk curvature and the geometry of the gradient noise do not match, an aggressive choice that improves one aspect can amplify instability along the other, leading to suboptimal statistical behavior. In this paper we employ on-average algorithmic stability to connect generalisation of PSGD to the effective dimension that depends on these sources of curvature. While existing techniques for on-average stability of SGD are limited to a single pass, as first contribution we develop a new on-average stability analysis for multipass SGD that handles the correlations induced by data reuse. This allows us to derive excess risk bounds that depend on the effective dimension. In particular, we show that an improperly chosen preconditioner can yield suboptimal effective dimension dependence in both optimisation and generalisation. Finally, we complement our upper bounds with matching, instance-dependent lower bounds.

</details>


### [77] [Slow-Fast Inference: Training-Free Inference Acceleration via Within-Sentence Support Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12038)
*Xingyu Xie,Zhaochen Yu,Yue Liao,Tao Wang,Kim-Chuan Toh,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的推理框架SFI，通过将解码过程分解为频繁的低成本快速步骤和偶尔的密集注意力慢速步骤，显著提升长上下文自回归解码的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文自回归解码成本高昂，因为每个解码步骤都需要重复处理不断增长的历史。观察到在解码过程中，句子内部和语义连贯的短跨度内，主导注意力支持通常保持相对稳定。

Method: 提出Slow-Fast Inference (SFI)框架：快速步骤重用紧凑的稀疏内存进行高效解码；慢速步骤在语义边界附近触发，重新审视更广泛的上下文，并使用选择器刷新后续快速步骤的选定内存。

Result: 在评估的上下文长度范围内，SFI提供约1.6倍到14.4倍更高的解码吞吐量，同时在长上下文和长思维链设置中保持与完整KV基线相当的质量。

Conclusion: SFI无需训练且可直接应用于现有检查点，为当代自回归推理模型在长上下文、长视野和智能体工作负载中降低推理成本提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Long-context autoregressive decoding remains expensive because each decoding step must repeatedly process a growing history. We observe a consistent pattern during decoding: within a sentence, and more generally within a short semantically coherent span, the dominant attention support often remains largely stable. Motivated by this observation, we propose Slow-Fast Inference (SFI), a training-free decoding framework that decouples generation into frequent low-cost fast steps and occasional dense-attention slow steps. Fast steps reuse a compact sparse memory for efficient decoding. Slow steps are triggered near semantic boundaries. At slow steps, the model revisits the broader context and uses the Selector to refresh the selected memory for subsequent fast steps. Across the evaluated context lengths, SFI delivers approximately $1.6\times$--$14.4\times$ higher decoding throughput while generally maintaining quality on par with the full-KV baseline across long-context and long-CoT settings. Because SFI is training-free and applies directly to existing checkpoints, it offers a practical path to reducing inference cost for contemporary autoregressive reasoning models in long-context, long-horizon, and agentic workloads.

</details>


### [78] [Cross-Domain Policy Optimization via Bellman Consistency and Hybrid Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12087)
*Ming-Hong Chen,Kuan-Chen Pan,You-De Huang,Xi Liu,Ping-Chun Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAvatar：通过跨域贝尔曼一致性和混合批评器解决跨域强化学习中的状态/动作空间差异和负迁移问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域强化学习面临两个核心挑战：1）源域和目标域可能具有不同的状态空间或动作空间，需要复杂的域间映射；2）源域模型的迁移性难以预先确定，容易产生负迁移效应

Method: 提出QAvatar方法：1）引入跨域贝尔曼一致性作为源域模型迁移性的度量；2）设计混合批评器，将源域和目标域的Q函数通过自适应无超参数权重函数结合

Result: 理论分析表明QAvatar具有收敛性并能实现可靠迁移；实验证明在多种RL基准任务（包括运动控制和机器人手臂操作）上表现出良好的迁移能力

Conclusion: QAvatar通过跨域贝尔曼一致性和混合批评器设计，有效解决了跨域强化学习中的空间差异和负迁移问题，实现了可靠的知识迁移

Abstract: Cross-domain reinforcement learning (CDRL) is meant to improve the data efficiency of RL by leveraging the data samples collected from a source domain to facilitate the learning in a similar target domain. Despite its potential, cross-domain transfer in RL is known to have two fundamental and intertwined challenges: (i) The source and target domains can have distinct state space or action space, and this makes direct transfer infeasible and thereby requires more sophisticated inter-domain mappings; (ii) The transferability of a source-domain model in RL is not easily identifiable a priori, and hence CDRL can be prone to negative effect during transfer. In this paper, we propose to jointly tackle these two challenges through the lens of \textit{cross-domain Bellman consistency} and \textit{hybrid critic}. Specifically, we first introduce the notion of cross-domain Bellman consistency as a way to measure transferability of a source-domain model. Then, we propose $Q$Avatar, which combines the Q functions from both the source and target domains with an adaptive hyperparameter-free weight function. Through this design, we characterize the convergence behavior of $Q$Avatar and show that $Q$Avatar achieves reliable transfer in the sense that it effectively leverages a source-domain Q function for knowledge transfer to the target domain. Through experiments, we demonstrate that $Q$Avatar achieves favorable transferability across various RL benchmark tasks, including locomotion and robot arm manipulation. Our code is available at https://rl-bandits-lab.github.io/Cross-Domain-RL/.

</details>


### [79] [Taming the Adversary: Stable Minimax Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient via Fractional Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12110)
*Taeho Lee,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MMDDPG框架，通过极小极大优化学习对抗扰动的鲁棒策略，在连续控制任务中实现对外部扰动和模型不确定性的强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在控制决策任务中表现出色，但面对意外外部扰动和模型不确定性时性能不稳定或下降。确保在这种条件下的可靠性能仍是关键挑战。

Method: 提出极小极大深度确定性策略梯度（MMDDPG）框架，将训练过程建模为用户策略与对抗扰动策略之间的极小极大优化问题。引入分数目标函数平衡任务性能与扰动幅度，防止过度激进的扰动并促进鲁棒学习。

Result: 在MuJoCo环境中的实验评估表明，MMDDPG在对抗外部力扰动和模型参数变化方面实现了显著改进的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MMDDPG框架通过极小极大优化有效学习对抗扰动的鲁棒策略，解决了强化学习在不确定环境中性能不稳定的问题，为连续控制任务提供了可靠的鲁棒性解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved remarkable success in a wide range of control and decision-making tasks. However, RL agents often exhibit unstable or degraded performance when deployed in environments subject to unexpected external disturbances and model uncertainties. Consequently, ensuring reliable performance under such conditions remains a critical challenge. In this paper, we propose minimax deep deterministic policy gradient (MMDDPG), a framework for learning disturbance-resilient policies in continuous control tasks. The training process is formulated as a minimax optimization problem between a user policy and an adversarial disturbance policy. In this problem, the user learns a robust policy that minimizes the objective function, while the adversary generates disturbances that maximize it. To stabilize this interaction, we introduce a fractional objective that balances task performance and disturbance magnitude. This objective prevents excessively aggressive disturbances and promotes robust learning. Experimental evaluations in MuJoCo environments demonstrate that the proposed MMDDPG achieves significantly improved robustness against both external force perturbations and model parameter variations.

</details>


### [80] [Cornserve: A Distributed Serving System for Any-to-Any Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12118)
*Jae-Won Chung,Jeff J. Ma,Jisang Ahn,Yizhuo Liang,Akshay Jajoo,Myungjin Lee,Mosharaf Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cornserve是一个用于Any-to-Any多模态模型的分布式服务系统，通过组件解耦和独立扩展实现高效服务，提供灵活的任务抽象和基于记录-重放的执行模型，相比现有方案可提升3.81倍吞吐量并降低5.79倍尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Any-to-Any多模态模型（接受文本、图像、视频、音频等多种输入并生成多种输出）的服务面临挑战：不同请求的输入输出模态组合不同，导致计算图路径不同；模型各组件具有不同的扩展特性，传统服务系统难以有效处理这种异构性。

Method: Cornserve采用组件解耦架构，提供灵活的任务抽象来表达Any-to-Any模型计算图。系统使用高效的记录-重放执行模型跟踪数据依赖，直接在生产者与消费者之间转发张量数据。基于Kubernetes实现，包含约23K行Python代码。

Result: Cornserve支持多样化的Any-to-Any模型，相比现有方案可提供高达3.81倍的吞吐量提升和5.79倍的尾部延迟降低。系统已开源，演示视频可在YouTube上获取。

Conclusion: Cornserve通过创新的分布式架构解决了Any-to-Any多模态模型服务的核心挑战，实现了组件级解耦和独立扩展，显著提升了服务效率和性能，为复杂多模态模型的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Any-to-Any models are an emerging class of multimodal models that accept combinations of multimodal data (e.g., text, image, video, audio) as input and generate them as output. Serving these models are challenging; different requests with different input and output modalities traverse different paths through the model computation graph, and each component of the model have different scaling characteristics.
  We present Cornserve, a distributed serving system for generic Any-to-Any models. Cornserve provides a flexible task abstraction for expressing Any-to-Any model computation graphs, enabling component disaggregation and independent scaling. The distributed runtime dispatches compute to the data plane via an efficient record-and-replay execution model that keeps track of data dependencies, and forwards tensor data between components directly from the producer to the consumer. Built on Kubernetes with approximately 23K new lines of Python, Cornserve supports diverse Any-to-Any models and delivers up to 3.81$\times$ higher throughput and 5.79$\times$ lower tail latency. Cornserve is open-source, and the demo video is available on YouTube.

</details>


### [81] [IsoCompute Playbook: Optimally Scaling Sampling Compute for LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12151)
*Zhoujun Cheng,Yutao Xie,Yuxiao Qu,Amrith Setlur,Shibo Hao,Varad Pimpalkhute,Tongtong Liang,Feng Yao,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Virginia Smith,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Zhiting Hu,Taylor Killian,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型强化学习后训练的计算最优分配问题，发现并行rollout数量随计算预算增加而可预测增长并最终饱和，这一趋势在不同难度问题上由不同机制驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然扩展定律指导了LLM预训练的计算分配，但针对大型语言模型强化学习后训练的类似指导原则仍然缺乏理解。研究者希望建立RL后训练的计算最优分配规则，为计算高效的LLM RL后训练提供实用指导。

Method: 将RL扩展问题框架化为计算约束下的优化问题，研究三个关键资源：每个问题的并行rollout数量、每批问题数量、更新步数。通过实验分析这些参数在不同计算预算下的最优分配，并探讨不同难度问题上的驱动机制。

Result: 发现计算最优的并行rollout数量随计算预算增加而可预测增长并最终饱和。在简单问题上由解决方案锐化驱动，在困难问题上由覆盖扩展驱动。增加并行rollout数量能减轻问题间干扰，而每批问题数量主要影响训练稳定性且可在较宽范围内选择。

Conclusion: 研究结果将RL扩展定律重新构建为规定性分配规则，为计算高效的LLM RL后训练提供了实用指导。这些发现在不同基础模型和数据分布上得到验证，有助于优化RL后训练的计算资源分配。

Abstract: While scaling laws guide compute allocation for LLM pre-training, analogous prescriptions for reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) remain poorly understood. We study the compute-optimal allocation of sampling compute for on-policy RL methods in LLMs, framing scaling as a compute-constrained optimization over three resources: parallel rollouts per problem, number of problems per batch, and number of update steps. We find that the compute-optimal number of parallel rollouts per problem increases predictably with compute budget and then saturates. This trend holds across both easy and hard problems, though driven by different mechanisms: solution sharpening on easy problems and coverage expansion on hard problems. We further show that increasing the number of parallel rollouts mitigates interference across problems, while the number of problems per batch primarily affects training stability and can be chosen within a broad range. Validated across base models and data distributions, our results recast RL scaling laws as prescriptive allocation rules and provide practical guidance for compute-efficient LLM RL post-training.

</details>


### [82] [A Quantitative Characterization of Forgetting in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12163)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在双模态混合抽象框架下，从理论上分析了生成模型持续后训练中的遗忘现象，区分了质量遗忘和旧分量漂移两种形式，证明了前向KL和后向KL目标的不同遗忘特性，并分析了回放机制及三种近期方法的遗忘控制条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的持续后训练被广泛使用，但对于何时以及为何发生遗忘现象缺乏原则性理解。本文旨在建立理论框架，精确量化遗忘现象，理解其与散度方向、几何行为重叠、采样机制以及训练中过去行为可见性之间的相互作用。

Method: 在Chen等人(2025)提出的双模态混合抽象框架下（代表旧任务和新任务），形式化两种遗忘形式：质量遗忘和旧分量漂移。针对等协方差高斯模态，分析前向KL和后向KL目标在训练新分布数据时的行为差异。进一步量化回放机制与这些目标的相互作用，并分析三种近期提出的近策略后训练方法（SDFT、TTT-Discover、OAPL）的遗忘控制条件。

Result: 理论证明：前向KL目标训练新分布数据会导致旧权重归零（质量遗忘），而后向KL目标收敛到真实目标（避免质量遗忘），旧均值仅通过由Bhattacharyya系数控制的重叠门控错误分配概率发生扰动，产生随模态分离指数衰减的漂移，且具有局部良条件几何和指数收敛性。回放机制分析表明：前向KL需要修改训练分布来改变总体最优解；后向KL保持总体目标不变，但通过有界重要性加权防止有限批次中的旧模态饥饿。三种近期方法在特定条件下能够保留旧质量并表现出重叠控制的漂移。

Conclusion: 遗忘现象可以根据散度方向、几何行为重叠、采样机制以及训练中过去行为可见性之间的相互作用进行精确量化。后向KL目标相比前向KL目标在避免质量遗忘方面具有优势，而回放机制和特定训练方法能够在不同条件下控制遗忘现象。

Abstract: Continual post-training of generative models is widely used, yet a principled understanding of when and why forgetting occurs remains limited. We develop theoretical results under a two-mode mixture abstraction (representing old and new tasks), proposed by Chen et al. (2025) (arXiv:2510.18874), and formalize forgetting in two forms: (i) mass forgetting, where the old mixture weight collapses to zero, and (ii) old-component drift, where an already-correct old component shifts during training. For equal-covariance Gaussian modes, we prove that forward-KL objectives trained on data from the new distribution drive the old weight to zero, while reverse-KL objectives converge to the true target (thereby avoiding mass forgetting) and perturb the old mean only through overlap-gated misassignment probabilities controlled by the Bhattacharyya coefficient, yielding drift that decays exponentially with mode separation and a locally well-conditioned geometry with exponential convergence. We further quantify how replay interacts with these objectives. For forward-KL, replay must modify the training distribution to change the population optimum; for reverse-KL, replay leaves the population objective unchanged but prevents finite-batch old-mode starvation through bounded importance weighting. Finally, we analyze three recently proposed near-on-policy post-training methods, SDFT (arxiv:2601.19897), TTT-Discover (arxiv:2601.16175), and OAPL (arxiv:2602.19362), via the same lens and derive explicit conditions under which each retains old mass and exhibits overlap-controlled drift. Overall, our results show that forgetting can by precisely quantified based on the interaction between divergence direction, geometric behavioral overlap, sampling regime, and the visibility of past behavior during training.

</details>


### [83] [Neural Thickets: Diverse Task Experts Are Dense Around Pretrained Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12228)
*Yulu Gan,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将预训练结果视为参数向量分布而非单一向量，发现大模型中任务专家密度高，可通过随机采样扰动选择最佳参数进行集成预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将预训练参数作为迭代优化的起点，本文提出新视角：预训练结果应视为参数向量分布，其中已包含任务特定专家，大模型中这些专家密度显著增加

Method: 提出完全并行的后训练方法：随机采样N个参数扰动，选择前K个最佳扰动，通过多数投票集成预测结果

Result: 该方法在当代大规模模型中与PPO、GRPO、ES等标准后训练方法具有竞争力，尽管方法简单但效果显著

Conclusion: 预训练参数分布中已包含高质量任务专家，大模型中专家密度显著增加，简单的随机采样集成方法可有效利用这一特性，为后训练提供新思路

Abstract: Pretraining produces a learned parameter vector that is typically treated as a starting point for further iterative adaptation. In this work, we instead view the outcome of pretraining as a distribution over parameter vectors, whose support already contains task-specific experts. We show that in small models such expert solutions occupy a negligible fraction of the volume of this distribution, making their discovery reliant on structured optimization methods such as gradient descent. In contrast, in large, well-pretrained models the density of task-experts increases dramatically, so that diverse, task-improving specialists populate a substantial fraction of the neighborhood around the pretrained weights. Motivated by this perspective, we explore a simple, fully parallel post-training method that samples $N$ parameter perturbations at random, selects the top $K$, and ensembles predictions via majority vote. Despite its simplicity, this approach is competitive with standard post-training methods such as PPO, GRPO, and ES for contemporary large-scale models.

</details>


### [84] [Matching Features, Not Tokens: Energy-Based Fine-Tuning of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12248)
*Samy Jelassi,Mujin Kwun,Rosie Zhao,Yuanzhi Li,Nicolo Fusi,Yilun Du,Sham M. Kakade,Carles Domingo-Enrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EBFT是一种基于能量模型的微调方法，通过序列级特征匹配提供密集语义反馈，在保持低验证交叉熵的同时提升下游任务准确率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉熵训练虽然提供密集且可扩展的监督，但仅优化教师强制下的下一个token预测，而非模型rollout下的序列级行为。需要一种能提供序列级语义反馈但不依赖任务特定验证器或偏好模型的方法。

Method: 提出能量基微调(EBFT)：1) 使用跨步块并行采样从嵌套前缀并发生成多个rollout；2) 对这些rollout进行批量特征提取；3) 使用得到的嵌入执行基于策略的策略梯度更新。该方法连接了KL正则化特征匹配和能量基建模的理论视角。

Result: 在问答编码、非结构化编码和翻译任务上，EBFT在匹配RLVR的同时优于监督微调(SFT)的下游准确率，且验证交叉熵低于两种方法。

Conclusion: EBFT通过序列级特征匹配为语言模型微调提供了一种有效的替代方案，能够在保持低验证交叉熵的同时提升下游任务性能，无需任务特定验证器或偏好模型。

Abstract: Cross-entropy (CE) training provides dense and scalable supervision for language models, but it optimizes next-token prediction under teacher forcing rather than sequence-level behavior under model rollouts. We introduce a feature-matching objective for language-model fine-tuning that targets sequence-level statistics of the completion distribution, providing dense semantic feedback without requiring a task-specific verifier or preference model. To optimize this objective efficiently, we propose energy-based fine-tuning (EBFT), which uses strided block-parallel sampling to generate multiple rollouts from nested prefixes concurrently, batches feature extraction over these rollouts, and uses the resulting embeddings to perform an on-policy policy-gradient update. We present a theoretical perspective connecting EBFT to KL-regularized feature-matching and energy-based modeling. Empirically, across Q&A coding, unstructured coding, and translation, EBFT matches RLVR and outperforms SFT on downstream accuracy while achieving a lower validation cross-entropy than both methods.

</details>


### [85] [The Latent Color Subspace: Emergent Order in High-Dimensional Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12261)
*Mateusz Pach,Jessica Bader,Quentin Bouniot,Serge Belongie,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种解释FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中颜色表示的方法，揭示了反映色调、饱和度和亮度的结构，并基于此开发了无需训练的颜色控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型虽然发展迅速，但实现细粒度控制仍然困难，主要原因是缺乏对语义信息编码方式的理解。本文旨在解释FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中的颜色表示机制。

Method: 开发了潜在颜色子空间（LCS）解释方法，揭示了潜在空间中反映色调、饱和度和亮度的结构。基于此提出了完全无需训练的方法，仅通过闭式潜在空间操作在FLUX中实现颜色预测和显式控制。

Result: 验证了LCS解释的有效性，证明其既能预测颜色，又能显式控制颜色。该方法完全基于闭式潜在空间操作，无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 成功解释了FLUX.1潜在空间中的颜色表示结构，并开发了基于该解释的训练免费颜色控制方法，为理解生成模型中的语义编码提供了新视角。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation models have advanced rapidly, yet achieving fine-grained control over generated images remains difficult, largely due to limited understanding of how semantic information is encoded. We develop an interpretation of the color representation in the Variational Autoencoder latent space of FLUX.1 [Dev], revealing a structure reflecting Hue, Saturation, and Lightness. We verify our Latent Color Subspace (LCS) interpretation by demonstrating that it can both predict and explicitly control color, introducing a fully training-free method in FLUX based solely on closed-form latent-space manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/LCS.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Type-safe Monitoring of Parameterized Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11104)
*Jan Baumeister,Bernd Finkbeiner,Florian Kohn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了将参数化流安全集成到流监控框架RTLola中的类型系统，用于处理无界数据域，确保监控器无运行时错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流监控是无人机等复杂信息物理系统的实时安全保障机制，监控器作为安全关键组件必须确保无运行时错误。当监控器需要处理无界数据域（如无限数量的空域参与者）时，需要使用动态数据结构，这给保证运行时安全带来了挑战。

Method: 提出了参数化流的概念，将单个流泛化为无界数量的流实例集合，并提供了系统化的内存管理机制。开发了一个精化类型系统，该类型系统保证所有内存引用要么成功，要么有默认值支持，从而有效确保运行时错误的不存在。

Result: 证明了运行时错误的缺失在一般情况下是不可判定的，但可以通过精化类型系统有效确保。在包括自主飞机监控在内的多个基准测试示例规范上报告了类型分析的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过参数化流与RTLola框架的类型安全集成，为处理无界数据域的流监控器提供了运行时安全保障，解决了动态数据结构带来的内存管理挑战。

Abstract: Stream-based monitoring is a real-time safety assurance mechanism for complex cyber-physical systems such as unmanned aerial vehicles. The monitor aggregates streams of input data from sensors and other sources to give real-time statistics and assessments of the system's health. Since the monitor is a safety-critical component, it is mandatory to ensure the absence of runtime errors in the monitor. Providing such guarantees is particularly challenging when the monitor must handle unbounded data domains, like an unlimited number of airspace participants, requiring the use of dynamic data structures. This paper provides a type-safe integration of parameterized streams into the stream-based monitoring framework RTLola. Parameterized streams generalize individual streams to sets of an unbounded number of stream instances and provide a systematic mechanism for memory management. We show that the absence of runtime errors is, in general, undecidable but can be effectively ensured with a refinement type system that guarantees all memory references are either successful or backed by a default value. We report on the performance of the type analysis on example specifications from a range of benchmarks, including specifications from the monitoring of autonomous aircraft.

</details>


### [87] [Quality-Driven Agentic Reasoning for LLM-Assisted Software Design: Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT) as a Time-Series Self-QA Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11082)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Yun-Cheng Tsai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QoT是一种质量驱动的推理时框架，通过将用户目标转化为有序的工程步骤和逐步自我提问，提升LLM在软件工程任务中的代码质量，特别是在API设计、数据通信和文件系统等复杂领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型在AI辅助软件开发方面取得了进展，但实际部署仍受到不完整实现、弱模块化和不一致安全实践的制约。需要一种系统化方法来提高LLM生成代码的质量和可靠性。

Method: 提出Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT)框架，将用户目标转化为：(1)有序的工程步骤序列，(2)逐步自我提问以验证约束和减少遗漏错误，同时维护轻量级推理记录以稳定后续设计决策。在API设计、数据通信和文件系统三个后端工程领域进行评估，使用基于ISO/IEC的质量评估标准（可扩展性、完整性、模块化、安全性）进行评分。

Result: QoT在较大模型和更复杂领域中表现出持续的质量改进，而较小模型在有限上下文和规划预算下可能表现出权衡。质量改进表现为QoT得分减去NoQoT得分的差值，显示容量依赖的改进模式。

Conclusion: QoT框架有效提升了LLM在软件工程任务中的代码质量，特别是在复杂领域和大模型中表现更佳。作者发布了包含提示、评分指南、原始生成和脚本的开源工具包，支持应用AI和数据分析研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have accelerated AI-assisted software development, yet practical deployment remains constrained by incomplete implementations, weak modularization, and inconsistent security practices. We introduce Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT), a quality-driven inference-time scaffold that turns a user goal into (i) an ordered sequence of engineering steps and (ii) stepwise self-questioning to verify constraints and reduce omission errors, while maintaining a lightweight reasoning record that stabilizes subsequent design decisions.
  We evaluate QoT across three representative backend engineering domains: API Design, Data Communication, and File Systems. Each task requires multi-module decomposition and exposes standard failure modes in LLM-generated systems. To enable data-driven comparison, we score generated artifacts using an ISO/IEC-inspired quality rubric that measures Scalability, Completeness, Modularity, and Security. We report domain-wise gains as the change in total quality score, defined as the QoT score minus the NoQoT score. Results show capacity-dependent improvements: QoT yields consistent quality improvements for larger models and more complex domains, while smaller models may exhibit trade-offs under tight context and planning budgets.
  We release an open artifact with prompts, scoring guidelines, raw generations, and scripts that reproduce the reported tables and figures to support applied AI and data analytics research.

</details>


### [88] [Understanding by Reconstruction: Reversing the Software Development Process for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11103)
*Zhiyuan Zeng,Yichi Zhang,Yong Shan,Kai Hua,Siyuan Fang,Zhaiyu Liu,Jiaheng Liu,Haozhe Wang,Yining Zheng,Ming Ding,Ke Shen,Ge Zhang,Wenhao Huang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出"通过重构理解"的新范式，通过反向工程静态代码库背后的潜在智能体轨迹（规划、推理、调试步骤）来增强大语言模型的代码生成能力，使用多智能体模拟生成这些轨迹，并通过搜索优化确保逻辑严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成方面取得了显著成功，但在需要深度、长视野推理的复杂软件工程任务上表现不佳。作者认为这种局限性源于标准预训练数据的性质：静态软件仓库只代表复杂智力过程的终端状态，抽象掉了中间的规划、调试和迭代优化过程。

Method: 提出"通过重构理解"的新范式，使用多智能体模拟框架合成这些轨迹，该过程基于源代码仓库的结构现实（如依赖图、文件层次结构）以确保保真度。采用基于搜索的优化技术迭代优化思维链推理，最大化生成代码与真实代码的似然性，保证合成数据的逻辑严谨性。

Result: 在这些重构轨迹上进行持续预训练显著提升了Llama-3-8B模型在多样化基准测试中的性能，包括长上下文理解、编码熟练度和智能体能力。

Conclusion: 反向工程静态代码库背后的潜在智能体轨迹（规划、推理、调试步骤）比原始代码本身提供了更丰富的监督信号，通过多智能体模拟和搜索优化生成这些轨迹，能够显著增强大语言模型的复杂软件工程能力。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in code generation, they often struggle with the deep, long-horizon reasoning required for complex software engineering. We attribute this limitation to the nature of standard pre-training data: static software repositories represent only the terminal state of an intricate intellectual process, abstracting away the intermediate planning, debugging, and iterative refinement. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel paradigm: understanding via reconstruction. We hypothesize that reverse-engineering the latent agentic trajectories -- the planning, reasoning, and debugging steps -- behind static repositories provides a far richer supervision signal than raw code alone. To operationalize this, we introduce a framework that synthesizes these trajectories using a multi-agent simulation. This process is grounded in the structural realities of the source repositories (e.g., dependency graphs and file hierarchies) to ensure fidelity. Furthermore, to guarantee the logical rigor of the synthetic data, we employ a search-based optimization technique that iteratively refines the Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to maximize the likelihood of the ground-truth code. Empirical results demonstrate that continuous pre-training on these reconstructed trajectories significantly enhances Llama-3-8B's performance across diverse benchmarks, including long-context understanding, coding proficiency, and agentic capabilities.

</details>


### [89] [Bridging Behavioral Biometrics and Source Code Stylometry: A Survey of Programmer Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11150)
*Marek Horvath,Emilia Pietrikova,Diomidis Spinellis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对源代码程序员归属识别研究的系统性映射研究，分析了2012-2025年间47篇相关文献，建立了特征类型与机器学习技术的分类体系，发现该领域主要关注封闭世界归属识别和风格特征，而行为信号、作者验证和可复现性研究较少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序员归属识别研究在软件工程、安全和数字取证等多个领域都有应用，但相关研究分散且方法多样，缺乏系统性整理。本文旨在通过系统性映射研究整合现有研究成果，建立统一框架，识别研究空白，为未来研究提供指导。

Method: 采用系统性映射研究方法，从135篇候选文献中通过结构化筛选过程选出47篇（2012-2025年）。从多个维度分析文献：作者归属任务、特征类别、学习与建模方法、数据集来源和评估实践。建立分类体系将风格和行为特征类型与常用机器学习技术关联，分析出版趋势、基准数据集和编程语言。

Result: 研究发现该领域主要关注封闭世界作者归属识别，大量使用风格特征，严重依赖少数基准数据集。行为信号、作者验证和可复现性研究相对较少。建立了统一的分类框架，识别出主要主题集群，并指出了方法学上的研究空白。

Conclusion: 本研究整合了程序员归属识别领域的现有研究，提供了系统性框架，揭示了当前研究重点（风格特征和封闭世界归属）与未充分探索的方向（行为信号、作者验证和可复现性）。为未来研究提供了方法论指导，有助于推动该领域向更全面、可复现的方向发展。

Abstract: Programmer attribution seeks to identify or verify the author of a source code artifact using stylistic, structural, or behavioural characteristics. This problem has been studied across software engineering, security, and digital forensics, resulting in a growing and methodologically diverse set of publications. This paper presents a systematic mapping study of programmer attribution research focused on source code analysis. From an initial set of 135 candidate publications, 47 studies published between 2012 and 2025 were selected through a structured screening process. The included works are analysed along several dimensions, including authorship tasks, feature categories, learning and modelling approaches, dataset sources, and evaluation practices. Based on this analysis, we derive a taxonomy that relates stylistic and behavioural feature types to commonly used machine learning techniques and provide a descriptive overview of publication trends, benchmarks, programming languages. A content-level analysis highlights the main thematic clusters in the field. The results indicate a strong focus on closed-world authorship attribution using stylometric features and a heavy reliance on a small number of benchmark datasets, while behavioural signals, authorship verification, and reproducibility remain less explored. The study consolidates existing research into a unified framework and outlines methodological gaps that can guide future work. This manuscript is currently under review. The present version is a preprint.

</details>


### [90] [ExecVerify: White-Box RL with Verifiable Stepwise Rewards for Code Execution Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11226)
*Lingxiao Tang,He Ye,Zhaoyang Chu,Muyang Ye,Zhongxin Liu,Xiaoxue Ren,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ExecVerify通过结合可验证的白盒执行轨迹奖励（如下一语句预测和变量值/类型预测）来改进代码LLM的执行推理能力，使用强化学习对齐训练目标与语义正确性，显著提升了小模型在代码推理和生成任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码LLM在执行推理方面仍有不足，特别是较小模型。传统监督微调方法依赖教师生成的解释（如I/O预测链和执行轨迹的自然语言描述），但中间执行步骤无法在训练中显式验证，导致训练目标可能简化为单纯模仿教师解释。此外，训练数据通常缺乏对任务难度的明确控制。

Method: 1. 通过基于约束的程序合成构建包含多个难度级别的数据集；2. 应用强化学习，奖励关于中间执行步骤和最终输出的正确答案，将训练目标与每个执行步骤的语义正确性对齐；3. 采用两阶段训练流程：先增强执行推理能力，然后迁移到代码生成任务。

Result: 使用ExecVerify训练的7B模型在代码推理基准测试中达到与32B模型相当的性能，在代码生成任务上比强后训练基线提升pass@1高达5.9%。

Conclusion: ExecVerify通过结合可验证的白盒执行轨迹奖励，超越了单纯的文本模仿，有效提升了代码LLM的执行推理能力。该方法特别适合较小模型，使其在代码推理和生成任务上达到与更大模型相当的性能。

Abstract: Code LLMs still struggle with code execution reasoning, especially in smaller models. Existing methods rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with teacher-generated explanations, primarily in two forms: (1) input-output (I/O) prediction chains and (2) natural-language descriptions of execution traces. However, intermediate execution steps cannot be explicitly verified during SFT, so the training objective can reduce to merely matching teacher explanations. Moreover, training data is typically collected without explicit control over task difficulty. We introduce ExecVerify, which goes beyond text imitation by incorporating verifiable white-box rewards derived from execution traces, including next-statement prediction and variable value/type prediction. Our work first builds a dataset with multiple difficulty levels via constraint-based program synthesis. Then, we apply reinforcement learning (RL) to reward correct answers about both intermediate execution steps and final outputs, aligning the training objective with semantic correctness at each execution step. Finally, we adopt a two-stage training pipeline that first enhances execution reasoning and then transfers to code generation. Experiments demonstrate that a 7B model trained with ExecVerify achieves performance comparable to 32B models on code reasoning benchmarks and improves pass@1 by up to 5.9\% on code generation tasks over strong post-training baselines.

</details>


### [91] [Unveiling Practical Shortcomings of Patch Overfitting Detection Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11262)
*David Williams,Ioakim Avraam,Aldeida Aleti,Matias Martinez,Justyna Petke,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 当前补丁过拟合检测工具在实践中的效果有限，简单的随机选择方法在71%-96%的情况下优于现有POD工具，表明需要新的检测技术和更现实的基准测试方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动程序修复（APR）可以减少开发人员调试时间，但生成的补丁可能存在过拟合问题（通过测试但不正确）。现有补丁过拟合检测技术的研究往往在孤立环境下评估，且数据集不能反映APR工具在实际使用中生成的正确定补丁与过拟合补丁的真实分布，因此需要了解这些技术在实际应用中的有效性。

Method: 本研究首次对多种补丁过拟合检测方法进行了全面的基准测试研究。首先，构建了反映现实假设的数据集（即在相同实验条件下由工具生成的补丁）。然后，使用这些数据对六种最先进的POD方法（包括静态分析、动态测试和学习方法）进行基准测试，并与两种基于随机抽样的基线方法（一种来自先前工作，一种本文提出）进行比较。

Result: 结果令人惊讶：简单的随机选择在71%到96%的情况下优于所有POD工具（具体取决于POD工具）。这表明：（1）当前的POD工具提供的实际效益有限，需要新的技术；（2）任何POD工具都必须在现实数据和随机抽样的基准上进行测试，以证明其实际有效性。

Conclusion: 当前补丁过拟合检测工具的实际效果有限，简单的随机选择方法表现更好。研究鼓励APR社区继续改进POD技术，并采用本文提出的实用基准测试方法。作者提供了数据和代码以促进这种采用。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) can reduce the time developers spend debugging, allowing them to focus on other aspects of software development. Automatically generated bug patches are typically validated through software testing. However, this method can lead to patch overfitting, i.e., generating patches that pass the given tests but are still incorrect.
  Patch correctness assessment (also known as overfitting detection) techniques have been proposed to identify patches that overfit. However, prior work often assessed the effectiveness of these techniques in isolation and on datasets that do not reflect the distribution of correct-to-overfitting patches that would be generated by APR tools in typical use; thus, we still do not know their effectiveness in practice.
  This work presents the first comprehensive benchmarking study of several patch overfitting detection (POD) methods in a practical scenario. To this end, we curate datasets that reflect realistic assumptions (i.e., patches produced by tools run under the same experimental conditions). Next, we use these data to benchmark six state-of-the-art POD approaches -- spanning static analysis, dynamic testing, and learning-based approaches -- against two baselines based on random sampling (one from prior work and one proposed herein).
  Our results are striking: Simple random selection outperforms all POD tools for 71% to 96% of cases, depending on the POD tool. This suggests two main takeaways: (1) current POD tools offer limited practical benefit, highlighting the need for novel techniques; (2) any POD tool must be benchmarked on realistic data and against random sampling to prove its practical effectiveness. To this end, we encourage the APR community to continue improving POD techniques and to adopt our proposed methodology for practical benchmarking; we make our data and code available to facilitate such adoption.

</details>


### [92] [Enhancing Requirements Traceability Link Recovery: A Novel Approach with T-SimCSE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11800)
*Ye Wang,Wenqing Wang,Kun Hu,Qiao Huang,Liping Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: T-SimCSE：一种基于SimCSE预训练语言模型的无监督需求可追溯性链接恢复方法，通过相似度计算和特异性指标重排序，在十个公开数据集上表现出优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练语言模型的需求可追溯性链接恢复方法存在两个主要问题：1）恢复的链接准确性不足；2）需要大量标注数据进行训练，而实际可用的标注数据集非常有限。

Method: 提出T-SimCSE方法，基于SimCSE预训练语言模型，首先计算需求与目标工件之间的相似度，然后使用新的特异性指标对目标工件进行重排序，最后在需求与排名前K的目标工件之间建立可追溯性链接。

Result: 在十个公开数据集上的评估表明，T-SimCSE在召回率和平均精度均值（MAP）方面均优于其他对比方法，表现出优越的性能。

Conclusion: T-SimCSE作为一种无监督方法，不需要标注数据，具有广泛适用性和良好性能，有效解决了现有需求可追溯性链接恢复方法在准确性和数据依赖方面的局限性。

Abstract: Requirements traceability plays an important role in ensuring software quality and responding to changes in requirements. Requirements trace links (such as the links between requirements and other software artifacts) underpin the modeling and implementation of requirements traceability. With the rapid development of artificial intelligence, more and more pre-trained language models (PLMs) techniques are applied to the automatic recovery of requirements trace links. However, the requirements traceability links recovered by these approaches are not accurate enough, and many approaches require a large labeled dataset for training. Currently, there are very few labeled datasets available. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel requirements traceability link recovery approach called T-SimCSE, which is based on a PLM -- SimCSE. SimCSE has the advantages of not requiring labeled data, having broad applicability, and performing well. T-SimCSE firstly uses the SimCSE model to calculate the similarity between requirements and target artifacts, and employs a new metric (i.e. specificity) to reorder those target artifacts. Finally, the trace links are created between the requirement and the top-K target artifacts. We have evaluated T-SimCSE on ten public datasets by comparing them with other approaches. The results show that T-SimCSE achieves superior performance in terms of recall and Mean Average Precision (MAP).

</details>


### [93] [QUARE: Multi-Agent Negotiation for Balancing Quality Attributes in Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11890)
*Haowei Cheng,Milhan Kim,Foutse Khomh,Teeradaj Racharak,Nobukazu Yoshioka,Naoyasu Ubayashi,Hironori Washizaki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QUARE是一个基于多智能体的质量感知需求工程框架，通过五个质量专业智能体（安全、效率、绿色、可信、责任）的结构化协商，系统性地发现和解决跨质量冲突，并生成符合行业标准的KAOS目标模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程对软件成功至关重要，但自动化面临挑战，因为需要平衡多个常冲突的质量属性同时保持利益相关者意图。现有LLM方法主要依赖单一推理或隐式聚合，限制了系统性地发现和解决跨质量冲突的能力。

Method: 提出QUARE多智能体框架，将需求分析构建为五个质量专业智能体（安全、效率、绿色、可信、责任）之间的结构化协商，由专用协调器协调。引入辩证协商协议，通过迭代提案、批评和合成明确暴露跨质量冲突。协商结果通过拓扑验证转换为结构合理的KAOS目标模型，并通过检索增强生成（RAG）验证是否符合行业标准。

Result: 在五个案例研究（来自MARE、iReDev基准和工业自动驾驶规范）中评估，QUARE实现了98.2%的合规覆盖率（比基线提高105%）、94.9%的语义保留（比最佳基线提高2.3个百分点）和高可验证性（4.96/5.0），同时比现有多智能体RE框架生成25-43%更多的需求。

Conclusion: 有效的需求工程自动化更依赖于原则性的架构分解、明确的交互协议和自动化验证，而不是模型规模。QUARE展示了通过多智能体协商系统性地解决质量冲突的可行性。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) is critical to software success, yet automating it remains challenging because multiple, often conflicting quality attributes must be balanced while preserving stakeholder intent. Existing Large-Language-Model (LLM) approaches predominantly rely on monolithic reasoning or implicit aggregation, limiting their ability to systematically surface and resolve cross-quality conflicts. We present QUARE (Quality-Aware Requirements Engineering), a multi-agent framework that formulates requirements analysis as structured negotiation among five quality-specialized agents (Safety, Efficiency, Green, Trustworthiness, and Responsibility), coordinated by a dedicated orchestrator. QUARE introduces a dialectical negotiation protocol that explicitly exposes inter-quality conflicts and resolves them through iterative proposal, critique, and synthesis. Negotiated outcomes are transformed into structurally sound KAOS goal models via topology validation and verified against industry standards through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We evaluate QUARE on five case studies drawn from established RE benchmarks (MARE, iReDev) and an industrial autonomous-driving specification, spanning safety-critical, financial, and information-system domains. Results show that QUARE achieves 98.2% compliance coverage (+105% over both baselines), 94.9% semantic preservation (+2.3 percentage points over the best baseline), and high verifiability (4.96/5.0), while generating 25-43% more requirements than existing multi-agent RE frameworks. These findings suggest that effective RE automation depends less on model scale than on principled architectural decomposition, explicit interaction protocols, and automated verification.

</details>


### [94] [A Collaborative and Pattern-Based Training Approach to Knowledge Acquisition and Decision-Making During the Design of Software Architectures Courses: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11904)
*Wilson Libardo Pantoja Yepez,Luis Mariano Bibbo,Julio Ariel Hurtado Alegría*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文描述了两所大学在软件架构课程中的协作学习经验，应用并评估了培训模式，通过全球开发活动整合团队，并使用TAM模型评估培训效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决软件架构培训面临的挑战：架构的抽象性、先验知识需求、难以重现真实项目环境、团队协作问题和资源限制。满足行业对软件架构设计技能的需求，提供高质量的结构化培训方案。

Method: 1. 两所大学比较学习目标、课程和教学策略，寻找共同基础；2. 选择并实施培训模式；3. 在课程最后阶段进行全球开发体验，整合两校团队；4. 使用技术接受模型(TAM)评估培训模式的效果、感知有用性和易用性；5. 提出培训模式目录来结构化架构知识。

Result: 成功实施了跨校协作的软件架构培训，培训模式帮助模拟行业环境并结构化架构知识。评估显示培训模式在提升能力、感知有用性和易用性方面取得积极效果。强调了通过多次项目经验逐步发展架构决策能力的重要性。

Conclusion: 培训模式目录为软件架构教育提供了有效的结构化方法，能够应对行业需求和教育挑战。协作学习经验和全球开发活动增强了学生的实践能力和团队协作技能。需要实用、结构良好的培训项目来培养架构决策能力。

Abstract: This article describes a collaborative learning experience on Software Architecture (SA) between Universidad del Cauca (UNICAUCA) in Colombia and Universidad Nacional de la Plata (UNPL) in Argentina. The goal was to apply and evaluate training patterns, identifying effective practices for replication in other contexts. During the planning phase, both universities compared learning objectives, curricula, and teaching strategies to find common ground for improving student training. Selected training patterns were implemented, and their impact on professors and students was measured. As an integrating activity, a global development experience was carried out in the final part of the course, merging the work teams of the two educational institutions in a development iteration. The evaluation of this experience focused on the competencies achieved through the training patterns, their perceived usefulness, and ease of use based on the Technology Acceptance Model (TAM). The training addressed industry needs for software architecture design skills despite challenges such as the abstract nature of architectures, prerequisite knowledge, difficulty in recreating realistic project environments, team collaboration challenges, and resource limitations. A catalog of training patterns was proposed to provide quality training. These patterns help simulate industry-like environments and structure architectural knowledge for incremental learning. The ability to make architectural decisions is developed over time and through multiple project experiences, emphasizing the need for practical, well-structured training programs.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Interpreting Contrastive Embeddings in Specific Domains with Fuzzy Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12227)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Mohammadreza Jamalifard,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合模糊规则分类系统和标准文本处理技术的方法，将特定领域特征映射到CLIP模型空间，并在临床报告和电影评论两个领域验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中自由文本数据（如法律程序和医疗记录）普遍存在，需要将其转换为结构化格式以便机器学习方法利用。虽然CLIP模型在零样本和少样本学习问题上表现出色，但在特定领域应用时仍存在问题。

Method: 使用模糊规则分类系统结合标准文本处理技术，将感兴趣的特征映射到CLIP模型创建的空间中。分析获得的规则和关联，并评估每个特征的重要性。

Result: 在临床报告和电影评论两个不同数据领域应用该方法，分别比较单独考虑和同时考虑两个领域的结果。展示了特征映射的有效性和领域适应性。

Conclusion: 讨论了该方法的局限性，并提出了进一步改进的方向。该方法为特定领域文本数据的结构化处理提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Free-style text is still one of the common ways in which data is registered in real environments, like legal procedures and medical records. Because of that, there have been significant efforts in the area of natural language processing to convert these texts into a structured format, which standard machine learning methods can then exploit. One of the most popular methods to embed text into a vectorial representation is the Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training model (CLIP), which was trained using both image and text. Although the representations computed by CLIP have been very successful in zero-show and few-shot learning problems, they still have problems when applied to a particular domain. In this work, we use a fuzzy rule-based classification system along with some standard text procedure techniques to map some of our features of interest to the space created by a CLIP model. Then, we discuss the rules and associations obtained and the importance of each feature considered. We apply this approach in two different data domains, clinical reports and film reviews, and compare the results obtained individually and when considering both. Finally, we discuss the limitations of this approach and how it could be further improved.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [The Laziness of the Crowd: Effort Aversion Among Raters Risks Undermining the Efficacy of X's Community Notes Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11120)
*Morgan Wack,Patrick Warren,Mustafa Alam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现众包内容审核系统（如Twitter/X的Community Notes）存在"难度惩罚"机制：明显虚假、易于驳斥的内容更可能获得公开标注，而看似合理、需要更多认知努力来验证的虚假信息则较少被标注，导致系统可能无法有效处理最可能欺骗用户的合理虚假信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索众包审核系统（如Twitter/X的Community Notes）中一个未被测试的机制：事实核查难度如何影响众包评分者的参与度。研究者假设，需要较少认知努力评估的明显虚假内容更可能获得公开标注，而看似合理、需要更多努力驳斥的虚假信息则较少被标注。

Method: 使用18个月的疫苗相关Community Notes数据（2,250条帖子）和382名调查参与者的评分。通过额外分析和利用LLM流水线的事实核查过程来排除替代解释，验证事实核查难度与公开标注获得率之间的关系。

Result: 研究发现，被认为更难进行事实核查的虚假信息显著更不可能获得"有帮助"/公开状态的标注。这种模式与评分者不愿投入所需认知努力来评估和评分看似合理的虚假信息相一致。

Conclusion: 众包审核系统可能系统性无法处理最可能欺骗用户的合理虚假信息。研究讨论了平台设计的影响，并提出了缓解众包内容审核系统中这种"难度惩罚"的机制。

Abstract: Crowdsourced moderation systems like Twitter/X's Community Notes program have been proposed as scalable alternatives to professional fact-checkers for combating online misinformation. While prior research has examined the effectiveness of such systems in reducing engagement with false content and their vulnerability to partisan bias, we identify a previously untested mechanism linking fact-check difficulty to systematic non-participation by crowdsourced raters. We hypothesize that claims requiring less cognitive effort to evaluate, specifically, those that are obviously false and easy to refute, are more likely to receive public notes than claims that are more plausible and require greater effort to debunk. Using eighteen months of vaccine-related Community Notes data (2,250 posts) and ratings from 382 survey participants, we show that claims perceived as more difficult to fact-check are significantly less likely to receive notes that achieve ``helpful''/public status. Following the conduct of additional analyses and a fact-checking process utilizing an LLM pipeline to help rule out alternative explanations, we interpret this pattern as consistent with an unwillingness among raters to invest the mental effort required to evaluate and rate notes for more plausible misinformation. These findings suggest that crowdsourced moderation may systematically fail to address the forms of plausible misinformation which are most likely to deceive. We discuss implications for platform design and propose mechanisms to mitigate this difficulty penalty in crowdsourced content moderation systems.

</details>


### [97] [LLMs in social services: How does chatbot accuracy affect human accuracy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11213)
*Jennah Gosciak,Eric Giannella,Zhaowen Guo,Michael Chen,Allison Koenecke*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 评估LLM聊天机器人建议对非营利组织个案工作者提供准确社会服务指导的影响，发现高质量AI建议能显著提升工作者准确性，但错误建议会严重降低表现，且存在"AI依赖不足平台期"现象


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会服务项目（如SNAP食品券）的资格规则复杂难懂，非营利组织个案工作者需要同时处理十多个复杂项目。LLM聊天机器人可能为处理不常见情况的客户提供更好、更快的帮助，但需要评估其对个案工作者指导准确性的实际影响

Method: 1) 创建包含770个困难但现实的多个选择题基准数据集；2) 在洛杉矶非营利外展组织中招募个案工作者进行随机实验；3) 对照组无聊天机器人建议，实验组接收不同准确率（53%-100%）的聊天机器人建议；4) 测量不同质量AI建议对个案工作者准确性的影响

Result: 1) 对照组平均准确率为49%；2) 高质量聊天机器人（96-100%准确）使个案工作者准确性提高27个百分点；3) 错误的聊天机器人建议显著降低个案工作者准确性，在对照组表现最好的简单问题上降低三分之二；4) 随着聊天机器人准确性提高，个案工作者准确性提升趋于平缓，出现"AI依赖不足平台期"现象

Conclusion: 高质量AI建议能显著提升个案工作者准确性，但错误建议危害很大。AI准确性提升带来的边际效益递减，存在"AI依赖不足平台期"，这对实际部署构成挑战，强调了在真实用户环境中评估人机协同工具的重要性

Abstract: Social service programs like the Supplemental Nutrition Assistance Program (SNAP, or food stamps) have eligibility rules that can be challenging to understand. For nonprofit caseworkers who often support clients in navigating a dozen or more complex programs, LLM-based chatbots may offer a means to provide better, faster help to clients whose situations may be less common. In this paper, we measure the potential effects of LLM-based chatbot suggestions on caseworkers' ability to provide accurate guidance. We first created a 770-question multiple-choice benchmark dataset of difficult, but realistic questions that a caseworker might receive. Next, using these benchmark questions and corresponding expert-verified answers, we conducted a randomized experiment with caseworkers recruited from nonprofit outreach organizations in Los Angeles. Caseworkers in the control condition did not see chatbot suggestions and had a mean accuracy of 49%. Caseworkers in the treatment condition saw chatbot suggestions that we artificially varied to range in aggregate accuracy from low (53%) to high (100%). Caseworker performance significantly improves as chatbot quality improves: high-quality chatbots (96-100% accurate) improved caseworker accuracy by 27 percentage points. At the question-level, incorrect chatbot suggestions substantially reduce caseworker accuracy, with a two-thirds reduction on easy questions where the control group performed best (without chatbot suggestions). Finally, improvements in caseworker accuracy level off as chatbot accuracy increases, a phenomenon that we call the "AI underreliance plateau," which is a concern for real-world deployment and highlights the importance of evaluating human-in-the-loop tools with their users.

</details>


### [98] [Understanding User Perceptions of Human-centered AI-Enhanced Support Group Formation in Online Healthcare Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11237)
*Pronob Kumar Barman,James R. Foulds,Tera L. Reynolds*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 在线健康社区中算法个性化支持小组的高接受度与条件性接受：用户重视同伴匹配但要求安全、透明和用户控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线健康社区规模庞大，患者和照护者难以找到最相关的同伴和内容，需要算法个性化支持小组来解决这一挑战

Method: 采用两阶段混合方法调查（N=165），研究在线健康社区参与模式、个性化优先级，以及对模拟个性化支持小组的接受度

Result: 模拟支持小组的感知价值高（平均4.55/5；62.8%评分为5/5），91.5%愿意加入；同伴匹配重要性与感知价值强相关（ρ=0.764，p<0.001）；条件性接受要求安全、透明、人工监督和用户数据控制

Conclusion: 个性化支持小组有需求，但只有在解决信任、隐私和算法治理问题后才会被采纳

Abstract: Peer support is critical to managing chronic health conditions. Online health communities (OHCs) enable patients and caregivers to connect with similar others, yet their large scale makes it challenging to find the most relevant peers and content. This study assessed perceived value, preferred features, and acceptance conditions for algorithmically personalized support group formation within OHCs. A two-phase, mixed-methods survey (N=165) examined OHC participation patterns, personalization priorities, and acceptance of a simulated personalized support group. Perceived value of the simulated support group was high (mean 4.55/5; 62.8% rated 5/5) and 91.5% would join this group. The importance participants placed on peer matching strongly correlated with perceived value (\r{ho}=0.764, p<0.001). Qualitative findings revealed conditional acceptance: participants demand security, transparency, human oversight, and user control over data. Personalized support groups may be desired, but they will not be adopted unless trust, privacy, and algorithmic governance concerns are addressed.

</details>


### [99] ["I followed what felt right, not what I was told": Autonomy, Coaching, and Recognizing Bias Through AI-Mediated Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11274)
*Atieh Taheri,Hamza El Alaoui,Patrick Carrington,Jeffrey P. Bigham*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究测试AI中介对话如何影响对能力歧视微侵犯的识别，比较了四种干预条件：偏见导向、包容导向、自我导向和阅读对照


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能力歧视微侵犯在日常互动中普遍存在，但帮助人们识别这些微侵犯的干预措施有限，需要探索AI中介对话在促进识别方面的潜力

Method: 160名参与者完成前测、干预和后测，分为四组：AI偏见导向（偏向偏见）、AI包容导向（偏向包容）、无引导对话（自我导向）和纯文本阅读对照。参与者评估场景的社会经验标准和情感影响，对话组提供质性反思

Result: 对话组比阅读组产生更强的识别能力，但轨迹不同：偏见导向改善了对偏见与中性的区分但增加了整体负面性；包容或无引导保持更平衡；阅读组增益较弱甚至下降。质性发现显示偏见导向常被拒绝，包容导向被采纳为支架

Conclusion: 研究贡献了验证的案例语料库、AI中介干预平台，以及设计启示：对话系统整合偏见相关提示时面临权衡，包容性提示比偏见导向提示更易被接受且有效

Abstract: Ableist microaggressions remain pervasive in everyday interactions, yet interventions to help people recognize them are limited. We present an experiment testing how AI-mediated dialogue influences recognition of ableism. 160 participants completed a pre-test, intervention, and a post-test across four conditions: AI nudges toward bias (Bias-Directed), inclusion (Neutral-Directed), unguided dialogue (Self-Directed), and a text-only non-dialogue (Reading). Participants rated scenarios on standardness of social experience and emotional impact; those in dialogue-based conditions also provided qualitative reflections. Quantitative results showed dialogue-based conditions produced stronger recognition than Reading, though trajectories diverged: biased nudges improved differentiation of bias from neutrality but increased overall negativity. Inclusive or no nudges remained more balanced, while Reading participants showed weaker gains and even declines. Qualitative findings revealed biased nudges were often rejected, while inclusive nudges were adopted as scaffolding. We contribute a validated vignette corpus, an AI-mediated intervention platform, and design implications highlighting trade-offs conversational systems face when integrating bias-related nudges.

</details>


### [100] [Bridging the Cognitive Gap: Co-Designing and Evaluating a Voice-Enabled Community Chatbot for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11303)
*Feng Chen,Luna Xingyu Li,Ray-Yuan Chung,Wenyu Zeng,Yein Jeon,Yizhou Hu,Oleg Zaslavsky*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究评估了在退休社区部署语音大语言模型聊天机器人，采用"玻璃盒"方法结合多模态可访问性和AI教育，显著提升老年人对AI的理解和透明度认知，但80岁以上用户使用语音输入时可用性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退休社区的数字门户对老年人造成物理和认知障碍，导致数字回避。生成式AI通过自然语言交互提供解决方案，但其"黑盒"特性和可用性挑战阻碍了采用。

Method: 在太平洋西北地区的持续护理退休社区评估语音大语言模型聊天机器人，采用混合方法的共同设计和素养工作坊(N=25)，应用"玻璃盒"方法结合多模态可访问性和有意的AI教育。

Result: 干预显著提高了参与者的技术理解(p=0.004)和感知透明度(p=0.001)，将他们的交互模式从盲目信任转变为优先考虑可验证证据的知情依赖。语音输入降低了认知负荷，但80岁及以上用户的可用性评分显著下降(r=-0.50)。

Conclusion: 真正年龄包容的AI必须超越基于触摸的界面，向零触摸导航发展。虽然语音交互减少了认知负荷，但对最年长用户的可用性挑战表明需要进一步优化界面设计。

Abstract: Digital portals in retirement communities often create physical and cognitive barriers for older adults, leading to digital avoidance. Generative AI offers a solution by enabling natural language interaction, yet its adoption is hindered by the opaque, "Black Box" nature of these systems and lingering usability challenges. To address this, we evaluated a voice-enabled Large Language Model (LLM) chatbot at a continuing care retirement community in the Pacific Northwest. Through a mixed-methods Co-Design and Literacy Workshop (N=25), we applied a "Glass Box" approach combining multimodal accessibility with intentional AI education. The intervention significantly improved participants' technical understanding (p=0.004) and perceived transparency (p=0.001), shifting their interaction model from blind trust to informed reliance prioritizing verifiable evidence. While voice input reduced cognitive load, usability scores dropped significantly for users aged 80 and older (r=-0.50), indicating that truly age-inclusive AI must evolve beyond touch-based interfaces toward zero-touch navigation.

</details>


### [101] [Ghost Framing Theory: Exploring the role of generative AI in new venture rhetorical legitimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11384)
*Greg Nyilasy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出幽灵框架理论(GFT)，解释创始人-投资者-生成式AI混合体如何共同生产、竞争和重新校准新创企业修辞合法化中的共鸣


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应生成式AI在创业框架中被大量使用但基本不可见的现状，需要理论来解释人-AI协作如何影响新创企业的修辞合法化过程

Method: 基于框架理论、微观合法性判断和社会物质可供性研究，识别生成式AI的修辞可供性（生成性、极端组合性、语气库、速度/能量和共享基础），理论化递归/迭代过程模型（幽灵路演、幽灵筛选、幽灵关系建立）

Result: 提出幽灵框架理论(GFT)，为生成式AI时代的修辞框架理论奠定基础，将人-AI协作研究与文化创业连接，并将可供性理论扩展到多参与者场景

Conclusion: GFT为理解生成式AI如何重塑创业修辞合法化提供了新理论框架，强调了可供性传递性和可见性在多参与者场景中的重要性

Abstract: Responding to the surging but largely invisible use of generative AI in entrepreneurial framing, I advance Ghost Framing Theory (GFT) to explain how hybrid founder- and investor-genAI ensembles co-produce, contest, and recalibrate resonance in the rhetorical legitimation of new ventures. Building on scholarship in framing, micro-level legitimacy judgments, and sociomaterial affordances, I identify genAI rhetorical affordances (generativeness, extreme combinatorics, tone repertoire, velocity/energy and shared substratum) and theorize a recursive/iterative process model (ghost pitching, ghost screening, ghost relationship-building), configuring emergent resonance and legitimation. GFT builds new rhetorical framing theory for the age of genAI, connects research on human-AI collaboration with cultural entrepreneurship and extends affordance theory into multi-actor scenarios where affordance transitivity and visibility emerge as key considerations.

</details>


### [102] [To Believe or Not To Believe: Comparing Supporting Information Tools to Aid Human Judgments of AI Veracity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11393)
*Jessica Irons,Patrick Cooper,Necva Bolucu,Roelien Timmer,Huichen Yang,Changhyun Lee,Brian Jin,Andreas Duenser,Stephen Wan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了三种支持信息类型（完整源文本、段落检索、LLM解释）在生成式AI数据提取工具中如何影响用户真实性评估的效率、效果、依赖和信任，发现段落检索在准确性和速度间取得较好平衡，而LLM解释会导致不当依赖和信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI幻觉风险意识增强，需要工具帮助用户评估AI生成答案的真实性。某些领域（如生物医学研究和法律）特别依赖即时AI数据提取，用户需承担真实性评估责任。现有研究提供了多种系统支持方式，但缺乏这些信息如何实际影响用户决策过程的实证证据。

Method: 在生成式AI数据提取工具背景下，通过用户研究比较三种支持信息类型：完整源文本、段落检索和LLM解释。从效率、效果、依赖和信任四个维度分析用户行为。同时考察信息需求复杂度的影响。

Result: 段落检索在准确性和速度间提供合理折衷，真实性判断效果与使用完整源文本相当。LLM解释虽能快速评估，但会培养对数据提取AI的不当依赖和信任，导致用户更不易检测错误。初步证据表明，对于复杂答案，不当依赖问题更严重。

Conclusion: 通过严格的用户评估，可以更好地开发支持有效且负责任的人类自主性在真实性评估过程中的系统。段落检索是较平衡的方案，而LLM解释需要谨慎使用以避免不当依赖。

Abstract: With increasing awareness of the hallucination risks of generative artificial intelligence (AI), we see a growing shift toward providing information tooling to help users determine the veracity of AI-generated answers for themselves. User responsibility for assessing veracity is particularly critical for certain sectors that rely on on-demand, AI-generated data extraction, such as biomedical research and the legal sector. While prior work offers us a variety of ways in which systems can provide such support, there is a lack of empirical evidence on how this information is actually incorporated into the user's decision-making process. Our user study takes a step toward filling this knowledge gap. In the context of a generative AI data extraction tool, we examine the relationship between the type of supporting information (full source text, passage retrieval, and Large Language Model (LLM) explanations) and user behavior in the veracity assessment process, examined through the lens of efficiency, effectiveness, reliance and trust. We find that passage retrieval offers a reasonable compromise between accuracy and speed, with judgments of veracity comparable to using the full source text. LLM explanations, while also enabling rapid assessments, fostered inappropriate reliance and trust on the data extraction AI, such that participants were less likely to detect errors. In additiona, we analyzed the impacts of the complexity of the information need, finding preliminary evidence that inappropriate reliance is worse for complex answers. We demonstrate how, through rigorous user evaluation, we can better develop systems that allow for effective and responsible human agency in veracity assessment processes.

</details>


### [103] [Evaluation format, not model capability, drives triage failure in the assessment of consumer health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11413)
*David Fraile Navarro,Farah Magrabi,Enrico Coiera*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文反驳了先前研究中关于ChatGPT健康分诊存在高误诊率的结论，指出原研究采用的考试式评估方法（强制ABCD选项、知识抑制、禁止澄清问题）与消费者实际使用健康聊天机器人的方式存在根本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（Ramaswamy et al.）在《自然医学》上报告ChatGPT健康分诊在51.6%的紧急情况下存在误诊，认为面向消费者的AI分诊存在安全风险。但该评估使用了考试式协议，与消费者实际使用健康聊天机器人的方式不同，因此需要验证评估方法对结果的影响。

Method: 测试了五个前沿LLM（GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3 Flash、Gemini 3.1 Pro），使用17个场景的部分复制库，在两种条件下进行测试：约束条件（考试式，1275次试验）和自然条件（患者式消息，850次试验）。进行了针对性消融实验，并使用作者发布的提示进行提示忠实性检查。

Result: 自然交互使分诊准确率提高了6.4个百分点（p=0.015）。糖尿病酮症酸中毒在所有模型和条件下100%正确分诊。哮喘分诊从48%提高到80%。强制ABCD格式是主要失败机制：三个模型在强制选择下得分0-24%，但在自由文本下得分100%（所有p<10^-8）。提示忠实性检查证实原研究的支架产生模型依赖、案例依赖的结果。

Conclusion: 头条误诊率高度依赖于评估格式，不应被解释为部署分诊行为的稳定估计。对消费者健康AI的有效评估需要在反映实际使用情况的条件下进行测试。

Abstract: Ramaswamy et al. reported in \textit{Nature Medicine} that ChatGPT Health under-triages 51.6\% of emergencies, concluding that consumer-facing AI triage poses safety risks. However, their evaluation used an exam-style protocol -- forced A/B/C/D output, knowledge suppression, and suppression of clarifying questions -- that differs fundamentally from how consumers use health chatbots. We tested five frontier LLMs (GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Opus 4.6, Gemini 3 Flash, Gemini 3.1 Pro) on a 17-scenario partial replication bank under constrained (exam-style, 1,275 trials) and naturalistic (patient-style messages, 850 trials) conditions, with targeted ablations and prompt-faithful checks using the authors' released prompts. Naturalistic interaction improved triage accuracy by 6.4 percentage points ($p = 0.015$). Diabetic ketoacidosis was correctly triaged in 100\% of trials across all models and conditions. Asthma triage improved from 48\% to 80\%. The forced A/B/C/D format was the dominant failure mechanism: three models scored 0--24\% with forced choice but 100\% with free text (all $p < 10^{-8}$), consistently recommending emergency care in their own words while the forced-choice format registered under-triage. Prompt-faithful checks on the authors' exact released prompts confirmed the scaffold produces model-dependent, case-dependent results. The headline under-triage rate is highly contingent on evaluation format and should not be interpreted as a stable estimate of deployed triage behavior. Valid evaluation of consumer health AI requires testing under conditions that reflect actual use.

</details>


### [104] [From Pen Strokes to Sleep States: Detecting Low-Recovery Days Using Sigma-Lognormal Handwriting Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11512)
*Chisa Tanaka,Andrew Vargo,Anna Scius-Bertrand,Andreas Fischer,Koichi Kise*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探索了从日常在线手写动态中推断睡眠相关恢复状态的可能性，发现基于Sigma-Lognormal模型特征的个人化分类框架能够有效检测低恢复日，为无创健康监测开辟新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手写研究主要关注字符识别和疾病分类，但手写动态反映健康个体日常生理波动的潜力尚未被探索。本研究旨在探索是否可以从在线手写动态中推断与睡眠相关的日常恢复状态变化。

Method: 提出了一个个人化二元分类框架，使用Sigma-Lognormal模型提取的特征来检测低恢复日。该模型捕捉笔划的神经运动生成过程。在为期28天的野外研究中，13名大学生每天记录三次手写数据，同时使用可穿戴戒指测量夜间心脏指标。将四个睡眠相关指标（HRV、最低心率、平均心率和总睡眠时长）的最低（或最高）四分位数定义为正类。采用留出一天交叉验证方法。

Result: 经过FDR校正后，所有四个变量的PR-AUC均显著超过基线（0.25），其中与心脏相关的变量表现最强。重要的是，分类性能在不同任务类型或记录时间上没有显著差异，表明恢复相关信号嵌入在一般运动动态中。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，从日常手写中可以检测到个体内部细微的自主恢复波动，这为无创、设备独立的健康监测开辟了新的方向，证明了手写动态作为生理状态监测工具的潜力。

Abstract: While handwriting has traditionally been studied for character recognition and disease classification, its potential to reflect day-to-day physiological fluctuations in healthy individuals remains unexplored. This study examines whether daily variations in sleep-related recovery states can be inferred from online handwriting dynamics. %
We propose a personalized binary classification framework that detects low-recovery days using features derived from the Sigma-Lognormal model, which captures the neuromotor generation process of pen strokes. In a 28-day in-the-wild study involving 13 university students, handwriting was recorded three times daily, and nocturnal cardiac indicators were measured using a wearable ring. For each participant, the lowest (or highest) quartile of four sleep-related metrics -- HRV, lowest heart rate, average heart rate, and total sleep duration -- defined the positive class. Leave-One-Day-Out cross-validation showed that PR-AUC significantly exceeded the baseline (0.25) for all four variables after FDR correction, with the strongest performance observed for cardiac-related variables. Importantly, classification performance did not differ significantly across task types or recording timings, indicating that recovery-related signals are embedded in general movement dynamics. These results demonstrate that subtle within-person autonomic recovery fluctuations can be detected from everyday handwriting, opening a new direction for non-invasive, device-independent health monitoring.

</details>


### [105] [Prediction of Grade, Gender, and Academic Performance of Children and Teenagers from Handwriting Using the Sigma-Lognormal Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11519)
*Adrian Iste,Kazuki Nishizawa,Chisa Tanaka,Andrew Vargo,Anna Scius-Bertrand,Andreas Fischer,Koichi Kise*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析日本中小学生数字手写数据，发现手写动力学特征能够编码学生的发展阶段和个体差异信息，特别是年级预测任务表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字手写采集能够捕获反映书写运动过程的详细时间和运动学信号。虽然手写分析在临床或成人群体中已得到广泛探索，但其在研究儿童发展和教育特征方面的潜力仍未得到充分研究。本研究旨在探索手写动力学是否编码与学生特征相关的信息。

Method: 使用日本中小学生的大规模在线数据集，系统比较三类手写衍生特征：运动学信号的基本统计描述符、基于熵的变异性度量、以及从sigma-lognormal模型获得的参数。尽管数据集包含密集的笔画级记录，但特征在学生层面进行聚合以实现表征间的受控比较。这些特征在三个预测任务中进行评估：年级预测、性别分类和学业成绩分类，使用线性/逻辑回归和随机森林模型在一致的实验设置下进行。

Result: 结果表明，手写动力学包含与发育阶段和个体差异相关的可测量信号，特别是在年级预测任务中表现显著。这些发现突显了运动学手写分析的潜力，并证实随着儿童发展，他们的手写朝着对数正态运动组织方向演变。

Conclusion: 手写动力学分析能够有效捕捉儿童发展阶段和个体差异信息，为教育评估和儿童发展研究提供了新的量化工具。研究证实了儿童手写发展过程中向对数正态运动组织演变的规律。

Abstract: Digital handwriting acquisition enables the capture of detailed temporal and kinematic signals reflecting the motor processes underlying writing behavior. While handwriting analysis has been extensively explored in clinical or adult populations, its potential for studying developmental and educational characteristics in children remains less investigated. In this work, we examine whether handwriting dynamics encode information related to student characteristics using a large-scale online dataset collected from Japanese students from elementary school to junior high school. We systematically compare three families of handwriting-derived features: basic statistical descriptors of kinematic signals, entropy-based measures of variability, and parameters obtained from the sigma-lognormal model. Although the dataset contains dense stroke-level recordings, features are aggregated at the student level to enable a controlled comparison between representations. These features are evaluated across three prediction tasks: grade prediction, gender classification, and academic performance classification, using Linear or Logistic Regression and Random Forest models under consistent experimental settings. The results show that handwriting dynamics contain measurable signals related to developmental stage and individual differences, especially for the grade prediction task. These findings highlight the potential of kinematic handwriting analysis and confirm that through their development, children's handwriting evolves toward a lognormal motor organization.

</details>


### [106] [Shadowless Projection Mapping for Tabletop Workspaces with Synthetic Aperture Projector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11551)
*Takahiro Okamoto,Masaki Takeuchi,Masataka Sawayama,Daisuke Iwai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出合成孔径投影映射系统，使用大量密集排列的投影仪实现无延迟、无阴影的桌面投影，无需计算补偿，并开发了离线模糊补偿方法解决分辨率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投影映射系统存在投影阴影问题（用户遮挡光路），而使用多个分布式投影仪的方法又因计算处理导致延迟，影响用户体验。需要解决这两个关键问题以实现更自然的增强现实体验。

Method: 采用合成孔径投影映射系统，使用大量密集排列的投影仪，通过物理布局而非计算补偿实现无阴影投影。开发离线模糊补偿方法解决子像素错位导致的分辨率下降问题，并建立最小化"投影感"的设计框架。

Result: 实现了无延迟、无阴影的桌面投影映射系统，模糊补偿方法计算时间与投影仪数量无关。通过用户研究验证了系统能有效减少投影感，实现更自然的材质属性改变效果。

Conclusion: 合成孔径投影映射系统通过大量密集投影仪的物理布局解决了传统系统的阴影和延迟问题，配合离线模糊补偿方法，为桌面工作空间提供了高质量的增强现实体验，并建立了最小化投影感的设计框架。

Abstract: Projection mapping (PM) enables augmented reality (AR) experiences without requiring users to wear head-mounted displays and supports multi-user interaction. It is regarded as a promising technology for a variety of applications in which users interact with content superimposed onto augmented objects in tabletop workspaces, including remote collaboration, healthcare, industrial design, urban planning, artwork creation, and office work. However, conventional PM systems often suffer from projection shadows when users occlude the light path. Prior approaches employing multiple distributed projectors can compensate for occlusion, but suffer from latency due to computational processing, degrading the user experience. In this research, we introduce a synthetic-aperture PM system that uses a significantly larger number of projectors, arranged densely in the environment, to achieve delay-free, shadowless projection for tabletop workspaces without requiring computational compensation. To address spatial resolution degradation caused by subpixel misalignment among overlaid projections, we develop and validate an offline blur compensation method whose computation time remains independent of the number of projectors. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our shadowless PM plays a critical role in achieving a fundamental goal of PM: altering material properties without evoking projection-like impression. Specifically, we define this perceptual impression as ``sense of projection (SoP)'' and establish a PM design framework to minimize the SoP based on user studies.

</details>


### [107] [High-Contrast Projection Mapping under Light Field Illumination with LED Display and Aperiodic Lens Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11573)
*Kotaro Fujimura,Hiroki Kusuyama,Masaki Takeuchi,Daisuke Iwai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种新型目标排除照明方法，通过LED显示面板与非周期透镜阵列结合，实现选择性环境照明，克服投影映射的暗室约束


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影映射技术需要暗环境才能保证高质量投影，这限制了其实际应用。为了解决这一"暗室约束"问题，需要开发能在明亮环境中保持高对比度投影的方法。

Method: 1. 提出目标排除照明方法，选择性照亮周围环境而避开投影目标；2. 结合LED显示面板与优化的非周期透镜阵列实现光场照明；3. 开发计算技术优化非周期透镜布局以抑制串扰引起的暗斑；4. 设计高效计算LED亮度模式的方法以支持动态投影映射。

Result: 原型系统实验表明，该方法能在明亮环境中实现高对比度的投影映射，同时保持自然柔和的阴影效果和精确的空间控制能力。

Conclusion: 提出的目标排除照明方法成功克服了投影映射的暗室约束，为在明亮环境中实现高质量投影映射提供了可行的技术方案，扩展了投影映射的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Projection Mapping (PM) is a technology that projects images onto the surfaces of physical objects, allowing multiple users to share an augmented reality experience without special devices. However, its practical use has been constrained by the need for dark environments to ensure high-quality projection. To overcome this ``dark-room constraint,'' we propose a novel target-excluding lighting method that selectively illuminates the surrounding environment while avoiding the PM target. Our system achieves light-field illumination by combining an LED display panel with an optimized aperiodic lens array. The key contributions include a compact form factor that provides a large effective light source area, reproducing natural soft shadows comparable to typical lighting, while maintaining the spatial controllability needed to precisely avoid the target. We also introduce a computational technique for optimizing aperiodic lens placement to suppress undesired dark spots caused by crosstalk, and efficient methods for computing LED luminance patterns that enable dynamic PM. Experiments with a prototype system demonstrate that our approach achieves high-contrast PM even in bright environments.

</details>


### [108] [From Pets to Robots: MojiKit as a Data-Informed Toolkit for Affective HRI Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11632)
*Liwen He,Pingting Chen,Ziheng Tang,Yixiao Liu,Jihong Jeung,Teng Han,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MojiKit：基于宠物互动分析的系统化情感机器人行为设计工具包，包含参考卡片、机器人原型和行为控制工作室，降低技术门槛并提升创意多样性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前动物启发式社交机器人的情感行为设计主要依赖直觉和个人经验，导致设计结果零散且缺乏系统性。需要为宠物启发式情感人机交互提供更系统化的设计指导

Method: 1. 编码分析人宠互动视频，通过文献和访谈验证洞察；2. 创建结构化参考卡片，映射宠物启发式情感交互设计空间；3. 开发MojiKit工具包（参考卡片+仿生机器人原型MomoBot+行为控制工作室）；4. 在18人参与的共创工作坊中进行评估

Result: MojiKit帮助参与者设计了35种超出个人宠物经验的情感交互模式；无代码工作室降低了技术门槛并增强了创意自主性；参与者能够系统化地创建更丰富、更多样化的情感机器人行为

Conclusion: MojiKit通过数据驱动的结构化资源、整合参考材料与动手原型的工具包，以及实证证据，为用户提供了系统化设计宠物启发式情感机器人行为的能力，推动了该领域从直觉驱动向系统化设计的转变

Abstract: Designing affective behaviors for animal-inspired social robots often relies on intuition and personal experience, leading to fragmented outcomes. To provide more systematic guidance, we first coded and analyzed human-pet interaction videos, validated insights through literature and interviews, and created structured reference cards that map the design space of pet-inspired affective interactions. Building on this, we developed MojiKit, a toolkit combining reference cards, a zoomorphic robot prototype (MomoBot), and a behavior control studio. We evaluated MojiKit in co-creation workshops with 18 participants, finding that MojiKit helped them design 35 affective interaction patterns beyond their own pet experiences, while the code-free studio lowered the technical barrier and enhanced creative agency. Our contributions include the data-informed structured resource for pet-inspired affective HRI design, an integrated toolkit that bridges reference materials with hands-on prototyping, and empirical evidence showing how MojiKit empowers users to systematically create richer, more diverse affective robot behaviors.

</details>


### [109] [PhiPlot: A Web-Based Interactive EDA Environment for Atmospherically Relevant Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11751)
*Matias Loukojärvi,Ananth Mahadevan,Katsiaryna Haitsiukevich,Kai Puolamäki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PhiPlot：基于Web的交互式探索环境，用于大气化学中高维分子数据集的可视化、聚类和知识引导的降维分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算化学的进展产生了大量大气相关分子的高维数据集，需要有效工具来探索这些数据，特别是用于研究大气气溶胶形成过程

Method: 开发PhiPlot：基于Web的交互式环境，集成可视化、聚类和领域知识引导的嵌入精炼功能，连接到现有的分子数据库集合

Result: 创建了一个支持数据模式发现和假设生成的可访问界面，为大气化学的数据驱动研究提供了有效工具

Conclusion: PhiPlot为大气化学领域的高维分子数据集探索提供了创新的交互式解决方案，支持知识引导的降维和模式发现，有助于推动大气气溶胶形成等关键问题的研究

Abstract: Advances in computational chemistry have produced high-dimensional datasets on atmospherically relevant molecules. To aid exploration of such datasets, particularly for the study of atmospheric aerosol formation, we introduce PhiPlot: a web-based environment for interactive exploration and knowledge-based dimensionality reduction. The integration of visualisation, clustering, and domain knowledge-guided embedding refinement enables the discovery of patterns in the data and supports hypothesis generation. The application connects to an existing, evolving collection of molecular databases, offering an accessible interface for data-driven research in atmospheric chemistry.

</details>


### [110] [Modeling Trial-and-Error Navigation With a Sequential Decision Model of Information Scent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11759)
*Xiaofu Jin,Yunpeng Bai,Antti Oulasvirta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究扩展信息气味理论，将导航视为受记忆约束的序列决策问题，解释用户为何会过早选择错误链接并依赖回溯恢复


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息气味理论假设用户在选择前会查看所有可用链接，但实践中用户经常快速选择链接、忽略相关线索，然后依赖回溯纠正错误。需要解释这种试错行为

Method: 将导航框架化为受记忆约束的序列决策问题，假设用户不会扫描整个页面而是策略性检查，考虑局部（当前页）和全局（站点）气味，但都受记忆限制

Result: 模型能够复现关键导航行为：过早选择、错误转向和通过回溯恢复。与经验数据比较显示模型有效

Conclusion: 当考虑导航问题的序列性和有限性特征时，试错行为可以通过信息气味理论得到很好解释

Abstract: Users often struggle to locate an item within an information architecture, particularly when links are ambiguous or deeply nested in hierarchies. Information scent has been used to explain why users select incorrect links, but this concept assumes that users see all available links before deciding. In practice, users frequently select a link too quickly, overlook relevant cues, and then rely on backtracking when errors occur. We extend the concept of information scent by framing navigation as a sequential decision-making problem under memory constraints. Specifically, we assume that users do not scan entire pages but instead inspect strategically, looking "just enough" to find the target given their time budget. To choose which item to inspect next, they consider both local (this page) and global (site) scent; however, both are constrained by memory. Trying to avoid wasting time, they occasionally choose the wrong links without inspecting everything on a page. Comparisons with empirical data show that our model replicates key navigation behaviors: premature selections, wrong turns, and recovery from backtracking. We conclude that trial-and-error behavior is well explained by information scent when accounting for the sequential and bounded characteristics of the navigation problem.

</details>


### [111] [Stuck on Suggestions: Automation Bias, the Anchoring Effect, and the Factors That Shape Them in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11821)
*Emely Rosbach,Jonas Ammeling,Jonathan Ganz,Christof Albert Bertram,Thomas Conrad,Andreas Riener,Marc Aubreville*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI辅助提升病理诊断性能，但引入7%自动化偏见率；时间压力加剧偏见严重性而非频率；专业经验与自我效能降低AI依赖


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI驱动的决策支持系统在计算病理学中的应用，关注人机协作中可能引入的认知偏见（如自动化偏见和锚定偏见），特别是在时间压力和个体用户特征影响下的表现

Method: 在线实验设计：28名病理专家独立估计肿瘤细胞百分比，然后在AI支持下重复估计；部分实验在时间压力下进行；使用线性混合效应模型分析AI建议的锚定效应和自动化依赖

Result: AI辅助整体提升诊断性能，但引入7%自动化偏见率；时间压力不增加偏见频率但加剧其严重性；AI建议存在显著锚定效应，时间压力下更明显；专业经验和自我效能与较低AI依赖相关，而AI辅助决策中的高信心与较高AI依赖相关

Conclusion: AI集成临床工作流具有双重性：提升性能同时引入偏见驱动的诊断错误风险；需要针对性干预措施减轻认知偏见，特别是对经验较少或高信心用户

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI)-driven decision support systems can improve diagnostic accuracy and efficiency in computational pathology. However, collaboration between human experts and AI may introduce cognitive biases such as automation and anchoring bias, where users adopt system predictions blindly or are disproportionately influenced by AI advice, even when inaccurate. These effects may be amplified under time pressure, common in routine pathology, or shaped by individual user characteristics. We conducted an online experiment in which pathology experts (n = 28) estimated tumor cell percentages: once independently and once with AI support. A subset of estimations in each condition was performed under time strain. Overall, AI assistance improved diagnostic performance but introduced a 7% automation bias rate, defined as accepted negative consultations where previously correct independent judgments were overturned by incorrect AI advice. While time pressure did not increase the frequency of automation bias, it appeared to intensify its severity, reflected in stronger performance declines associated with increased AI reliance under cognitive load. A linear mixed-effects model (LMM) simulating weighted averaging showed a statistically significant positive coefficient for AI advice, indicating moderate anchoring on system output. This effect increased under time pressure, suggesting anchoring bias becomes more pronounced when cognitive resources are limited. A second LMM assessing automation reliance, a proxy for automation and anchoring bias, showed that professional experience and self-efficacy were associated with lower dependence on AI, whereas higher confidence during AI-assisted decisions was tied to increased AI reliance. These findings highlight the dual nature of AI integration in clinical workflows: improving performance while introducing risks of bias-driven diagnostic errors.

</details>


### [112] [Design Exploration of Lightweight Interactions for Awareness-Supporting Technologies in Hybrid Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11977)
*Lu Liu,Harm van Essen,Berry Eggen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索如何在混合工作环境中通过轻量级交互设计促进非正式沟通，关注信息感知而非显式内容交换


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合工作环境缺乏自然产生的非正式沟通和同事活动的环境感知，这可能阻碍团队协作

Method: 采用体验式设计方法，通过头脑风暴、推测和原型设计探索小型混合团队的设计空间，通过标注和分析设计概念、推测场景和原型来开发框架

Result: 开发了一个框架，识别了轻量级交互的设计选项以及将其与信息显示集成的方法

Conclusion: 通过关注信息感知而非显式内容交换的轻量级交互设计，可以有效支持混合工作环境中的非正式沟通

Abstract: Hybrid work settings often lack the informal communication that naturally emerges from spontaneous encounters and ambient awareness of coworkers' activities, potentially hindering team collaboration. To address this challenge, we explored how lightweight interactions can be integrated into awareness-supporting technologies for fostering informal communication. Our experiential design approach focused on how information is perceived and processed rather than explicit content exchange. Through brainstorming, speculating, and prototyping, we explored the design space for small hybrid teams. By annotating and analyzing design concepts, speculative scenarios, and prototypes, we developed a framework that identified design options for lightweight interactions and methods for integrating them with information displays.

</details>


### [113] [ConvScale: Conversational Interviews for Scale-Aligned Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11988)
*Peinuan Qin,Jingzhu Chen,Yitian Yang,Han Meng,Zicheng Zhu,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ConvScale：一种AI支持的方法，将心理测量量表转换为自然对话访谈，同时保持原始测量结构，用于预测项目级分数并生成量表评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话访谈通常用于补充结构化调查，能引出丰富的情境化回答，但通常只进行定性分析。它们在定量测量方面的潜力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: ConvScale方法将心理测量量表转换为自然对话访谈，同时保持原始测量结构。基于访谈数据，预测项目级分数并聚合生成量表评估。

Result: 在18名参与者的组内研究中，ConvScale得出的分数在项目和构念层面都与参与者自我报告分数高度一致，同时保持中等内部信度；但结构效度不足。

Conclusion: 讨论了通过访谈支持定量测量的潜力，并为未来设计提出了启示。虽然ConvScale在分数一致性方面表现良好，但需要改进结构效度。

Abstract: Conversational interviews are commonly used to complement structured surveys by eliciting rich and contextualized responses, which are typically analyzed qualitatively. However, their potential contribution to quantitative measurement remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce ConvScale, an AI-supported approach that transforms psychometric scales into natural conversational interviews while preserving the original measurement structure.
