Method: 1. 引入HumDex便携式遥操作系统，利用IMU运动追踪解决便携性与精度的权衡；2. 提出基于学习的重定向方法，无需手动参数调整即可生成平滑自然的手部动作；3. 构建两阶段模仿学习框架：先在多样化人类运动数据上预训练学习可泛化先验，然后在机器人数据上微调以弥合具身差距。

Result: 系统显著提高了对新配置、物体和背景的泛化能力，同时最小化了数据采集成本。整个系统完全可复现并已开源。

Conclusion: HumDex系统有效解决了人形机器人全身灵巧操作中高质量演示数据收集的瓶颈问题，通过创新的遥操作技术和两阶段学习框架，为复杂全身任务的机器人学习提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation, where the efficient collection of high-quality demonstration data remains a central bottleneck.
  Existing teleoperation systems often suffer from limited portability, occlusion, or insufficient precision, which hinders their applicability to complex whole-body tasks. To address these challenges, we introduce HumDex, a portable teleoperation system designed for humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation. Our system leverages IMU-based motion tracking to address the portability-precision trade-off, enabling accurate full-body tracking while remaining easy to deploy. For dexterous hand control, we further introduce a learning-based retargeting method that generates smooth and natural hand motions without manual parameter tuning. Beyond teleoperation, HumDex enables efficient collection of human motion data. Building on this capability, we propose a two-stage imitation learning framework that first pre-trains on diverse human motion data to learn generalizable priors, and then fine-tunes on robot data to bridge the embodiment gap for precise execution. We demonstrate that this approach significantly improves generalization to new configurations, objects, and backgrounds with minimal data acquisition costs. The entire system is fully reproducible and open-sourced at https://github.com/physical-superintelligence-lab/HumDex.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Wide-Area GNSS Spoofing and Jamming Detection Using AIS-Derived Spatiotemporal Integrity Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11055)
*Sanghyeon Park,DeukJae Cho,Pyo-Woong Son*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段AIS框架，通过预过滤通信层缺陷、提取运动学异常、时空聚类分析，实现GNSS欺骗和干扰检测，在韩国沿海水域测试中显著降低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS欺骗和干扰威胁海上导航，但原始AIS消息包含通信层缺陷（重复MMSI、时间戳错误、陈旧重传、多站重播延迟），这些缺陷可能模仿欺骗或干扰，使得AIS位置不可靠，需要预过滤才能用于GNSS干扰检测。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1) 使用基于规则的诊断丢弃通信故障；2) 应用交互多模型滤波器和传输间隔分析提取运动学一致性和连续性异常；3) 应用时空DBSCAN按多船一致性和时间持续性对异常进行分组，分类为传感器故障、欺骗或干扰。

Result: 在韩国沿海水域约9.66亿条AIS消息上测试，框架检测到17个欺骗集群和343个干扰集群，相对于朴素聚类方法将误报率降低了98.6%。

Conclusion: 经过严格的预过滤后，AIS数据可以在没有专用传感器的情况下实现广域GNSS干扰检测，为海上导航安全提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) spoofing and jamming threaten maritime navigation by corrupting positions from Automatic Identification System (AIS) transponders. Crucially, raw AIS messages contain communication-layer defects (duplicated MMSIs, timestamp errors, stale retransmissions, and multi-station rebroadcast delays) that can mimic spoofing or jamming. Thus, AIS positions are unreliable without pre-filtering. We propose a three-stage AIS-based framework that (1) uses rule-based diagnostics to discard communication faults, (2) applies an interacting multiple model filter and transmission-interval analysis to extract kinematic-consistency and continuity anomalies, and (3) applies spatiotemporal DBSCAN to group anomalies by multi-vessel coherence and temporal persistence and classify them as sensor faults, spoofing, or jamming. Tested on approximately 966 million AIS messages from Korean coastal waters, the framework detected 17 spoofing and 343 jamming clusters and reduced false alarms by 98.6% relative to naive clustering. These results show that, after rigorous pre-filtering, AIS data can enable wide-area GNSS interference detection without dedicated sensors.

</details>


### [153] [The Attack and Defense Landscape of Agentic AI: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11088)
*Juhee Kim,Xiaoyuan Liu,Zhun Wang,Shi Qiu,Bo Li,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首篇系统性综述AI智能体安全，涵盖设计空间、攻击面、防御机制分析，提出首个安全风险与防御策略框架，识别现有差距与开放挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型与非AI系统组件结合的AI智能体在现实应用中快速发展，提供前所未有的自动化和灵活性，但这种灵活性带来了与传统软件系统根本不同的复杂安全挑战，需要系统性研究

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，分析AI智能体设计空间、攻击面（攻击场景）和防御机制；通过案例研究识别现有安全差距；提出首个理解AI智能体安全风险与防御策略的系统性框架

Result: 建立了AI智能体安全的全面分析框架，识别了智能体AI系统安全方面的现有差距，指出了该新兴领域的开放挑战，为构建安全智能体系统和推进相关研究奠定了基础

Conclusion: AI智能体安全是一个关键且新兴的研究领域，需要系统性框架来理解其独特的安全挑战；本文提出的框架为构建安全智能体系统和推进该领域研究提供了基础，并识别了未来研究方向

Abstract: AI agents that combine large language models with non-AI system components are rapidly emerging in real-world applications, offering unprecedented automation and flexibility. However, this unprecedented flexibility introduces complex security challenges fundamentally different from those in traditional software systems. This paper presents the first systematic and comprehensive survey of AI agent security, including an analysis of the design space, attack landscape, and defense mechanisms for secure AI agent systems. We further conduct case studies to point out existing gaps in securing agentic AI systems and identify open challenges in this emerging domain. Our work also introduces the first systematic framework for understanding the security risks and defense strategies of AI agents, serving as a foundation for building both secure agentic systems and advancing research in this critical area.

</details>


### [154] [Primitive-Root Determinant Densities over Prime Fields and Implications for PRIM-LWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11196)
*Vipin Singh Sehrawat*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文解决了关于prim-LWE问题中维度均匀归约常数c(p)的公开问题，证明了inf_{p prime} c(p)=0，并给出了c(p)的精确渐近行为和分布特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Sehrawat等人提出的关于prim-LWE问题中维度均匀归约常数c(p)是否趋近于0的公开问题，该问题原本依赖于原数素数的猜想。作者希望绕过这一猜想，无条件地解决该问题。

Method: 使用狄利克雷定理和梅滕斯乘积公式等经典数论工具，分析n×n矩阵在有限域F_p上具有原根行列式的密度c_n(p)。通过研究c(p)=inf_{m≥1} c_m(p)的渐近行为，建立其与素数分布的关系。

Result: 1. 无条件证明了inf_{p prime} c(p)=0；2. 建立了min_{p≤x} c(p) ≍ 1/log log x的精确阶；3. 证明了c(p)在素数上具有连续的纯奇异极限分布，支撑集为[0,1/2]；4. 给出了密码学相关素数q的显式下界，仅依赖于ω(q-1)；5. 对NIST标准化模数q=3329和q=8380417，维度均匀拒绝采样开销上界分别为2.17和3.42。

Conclusion: 该论文完全解决了prim-LWE问题中维度均匀归约常数的公开问题，提供了无条件的结果和实用的密码学界限。结果表明，对于密码学应用，拒绝采样开销是可管理的，且存在显式界限可用于实际参数选择。

Abstract: The \textsc{prim-lwe} problem (Sehrawat, Yeo, and Desmedt, \emph{Theoret.\ Comput.\ Sci.}\ 886, 2021) is a variant of Learning with Errors requiring the secret matrix to have a primitive-root determinant. The dimension-uniform reduction constant is $c(p)=\inf_{m\ge 1}c_m(p)$, where $c_n(p)$ is the density of $n\times n$ matrices over~$\mathbb{F}_p$ with primitive-root determinant. Those authors asked whether $\inf_{p\text{ prime}}c(p)=0$, noting that an affirmative answer would follow from the conjectural infinitude of primorial primes.
  We resolve this unconditionally using only Dirichlet's theorem and Mertens' product formula, bypassing the primorial-prime hypothesis entirely. We establish the sharp order $\min_{p\le x}c(p)\asymp 1/\log\log x$, prove that $c(p)$ possesses a continuous purely singular limiting distribution over the primes with support exactly $[0,1/2]$, and derive explicit lower bounds on $c(q)$ for primes of cryptographic interest parameterized solely by~$ω(q{-}1)$, the number of distinct prime factors of~$q{-}1$. These bounds apply to every prime~$q$ whose predecessor has controlled factorization structure, as measured by~$ω(q{-}1)$; this includes many NTT-friendly moduli, though NTT-friendliness alone does not imply the needed bound. For the NIST-standardized moduli $q=3329$ (ML-KEM) and $q=8380417$ (ML-DSA), the dimension-uniform expected rejection-sampling overhead~$1/c(q)$ is at most $2.17$ and $3.42$, respectively. As a simple conservative bound, for any prime $q>2^{30}$ one has $1/c(q)\le 1.79\log q$. The worst-case overhead among primes $p\le x$ is $Θ(\log\log x)$, and pointwise $1/c(q)=O(\log\log q)$.

</details>


### [155] [Security-by-Design for LLM-Based Code Generation: Leveraging Internal Representations for Concept-Driven Steering Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11212)
*Maximilian Wendlinger,Daniel Kowatsch,Konstantin Böttinger,Philip Sperl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SCS-Code方法，通过引导CodeLLMs内部表征来生成安全代码，解决了现有方法在功能正确性和安全性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CodeLLMs在生成代码时经常产生功能正确但不安全的代码，现有改进方法效果有限，主要因为缺乏对代码生成内部机制和安全漏洞根本原因的理解。

Method: 研究CodeLLMs内部安全概念表征，发现模型在生成不安全代码时通常意识到漏洞存在。基于此提出SCS-Code方法，在token生成过程中引导LLMs内部表征朝向安全和功能正确的代码输出。

Result: SCS-Code在多个安全编码基准测试中优于现有最先进方法，实现了轻量级、模块化的安全代码生成机制。

Conclusion: 通过理解CodeLLMs内部安全概念表征并引导这些表征，可以显著提高AI生成代码的安全性，为实际应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show remarkable capabilities in understanding natural language and generating complex code. However, as practitioners adopt CodeLLMs for increasingly critical development tasks, research reveals that these models frequently generate functionally correct yet insecure code, posing significant security risks. While multiple approaches have been proposed to improve security in AI-based code generation, combined benchmarks show these methods remain insufficient for practical use, achieving only limited improvements in both functional correctness and security. This stems from a fundamental gap in understanding the internal mechanisms of code generation and the root causes of security vulnerabilities, forcing researchers to rely on heuristics and empirical observations. In this work, we investigate the internal representation of security concepts in CodeLLMs, revealing that models are often aware of vulnerabilities as they generate insecure code. Through systematic evaluation, we demonstrate that CodeLLMs can distinguish between security subconcepts, enabling a more fine-grained analysis than prior black-box approaches. Leveraging these insights, we propose Secure Concept Steering for CodeLLMs (SCS-Code). During token generation, SCS-Code steers LLMs' internal representations toward secure and functional code output, enabling a lightweight and modular mechanism that can be integrated into existing code models. Our approach achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods across multiple secure coding benchmarks.

</details>


### [156] [LROO Rug Pull Detector: A Leakage-Resistant Framework Based on On-Chain and OSINT Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11324)
*Fatemeh Shoaei,Mohammad Pishdar,Mozafar Bag-Mohammadi,Mojtaba Karami*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种泄漏感知的早期拉地毯骗局检测框架，结合链上行为指标与时间对齐的开源情报信号，在流动性撤出前进行预测，使用TabPFN处理多模态表格数据，实现了强判别性能和改进的概率校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约生态系统的不可变性和无需许可特性虽然支持去中心化应用，但也为大规模欺诈提供了便利。拉地毯骗局是最普遍的攻击之一，项目方在人为抬高代币价值后突然撤出流动性。现有检测方法主要依赖反应性的链上信号，且常受时间数据泄漏影响，限制了其实际可靠性。

Method: 提出泄漏感知的早期拉地毯检测框架，整合链上行为指标与时间对齐的开源情报信号。构建了包含1,000个代币项目的手动标注数据集，所有特征均在流动性撤出前提取以保证因果有效性。数据集结合结构化的链上指标和来自社交媒体活动及搜索趋势的外部关注信号。在该框架中，使用TabPFN作为核心建模组件，在严格时间约束下从多模态表格数据中学习。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提框架相比经典基线方法实现了强判别性能和改进的概率校准，同时保持较低假阴性率。通过将拉地毯检测构建为因果多模态预测问题，该工作强调了泄漏弹性评估和校准风险估计在区块链安全系统中部署的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的泄漏感知框架通过整合链上行为指标与时间对齐的开源情报信号，为早期拉地毯骗局检测提供了有效解决方案。该方法强调了在严格时间约束下保持因果有效性的重要性，并通过TabPFN模型实现了对多模态表格数据的高效学习，为区块链安全系统的实际部署提供了可靠的风险评估工具。

Abstract: Smart contract-based ecosystems enable decentralized applications without trusted intermediaries, but their immutability and permissionless design also facilitate large-scale fraud. One of the most prevalent attacks is the rug pull, where project operators abruptly withdraw liquidity after artificially inflating token value. Existing detection methods primarily rely on reactive on-chain signals and often suffer from temporal data leakage, limiting their real-world reliability. This paper proposes a leakage-aware framework for early rug-pull detection that integrates on-chain behavioral metrics with temporally aligned Open Source Intelligence (OSINT) signals. We construct a hand-labeled dataset of 1,000 token projects, spanning DeFi and non-DeFi settings, with all features extracted strictly prior to any liquidity withdrawal to preserve causal validity. The dataset combines structural on-chain indicators with external attention signals derived from social media activity and search trends. Within this framework, TabPFN is employed as a core modeling component for learning from multimodal tabular data under strict temporal constraints. Experimental results show that the proposed framework achieves strong discriminative performance and improved probability calibration compared to classical baselines, while maintaining low false-negative rates. By framing rug-pull detection as a causal, multimodal forecasting problem, this work emphasizes the necessity of leakage-resilient evaluation and calibrated risk estimation for deployment in blockchain security systems.

</details>


### [157] [Unclonable Encryption in the Haar Random Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11437)
*James Bartusek,Eli Goldin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在Haar随机预言机模型中构建可重用、支持任意长度消息的不可克隆加密方案，首次证明计算假设下的可重用UE可能在"微密码"世界中存在


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在"微密码"（micocrypt）世界中构建不可克隆加密的可能性，该世界可能不存在单向函数，但需要计算假设来实现可重用的UE方案

Method: 在Haar随机预言机模型中构建UE方案，所有参与方可以查询U、U†、U*、UT，利用路径记录框架证明"酉重编程引理"作为关键技术贡献

Result: 成功构造了满足标准不可克隆不可区分性安全性的UE方案，支持密钥重用和任意长度消息加密，为可重用UE在微密码世界中的存在提供了首个证据

Conclusion: 证明了即使在可能不存在单向函数的微密码世界中，基于计算假设的可重用不可克隆加密方案仍然可能存在，酉重编程引理是该工作的核心技术贡献

Abstract: We construct unclonable encryption (UE) in the Haar random oracle model, where all parties have query access to $U,U^\dagger,U^*,U^T$ for a Haar random unitary $U$. Our scheme satisfies the standard notion of unclonable indistinguishability security, supports reuse of the secret key, and can encrypt arbitrary-length messages. That is, we give the first evidence that (reusable) UE, which requires computational assumptions, exists in "micocrypt", a world where one-way functions may not exist.
  As one of our central technical contributions, we build on the recently introduced path recording framework to prove a natural ``unitary reprogramming lemma'', which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [158] [Software-Hardware Binding for Protection of Sensitive Data in Embedded Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11727)
*Bernhard Fischer,Daniel Dorfmeister,Flavio Ferrarotti,Manuel Penz,Michael Kargl,Martina Zeinzinger,Florian Eibensteiner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合硬件指纹和布尔逻辑的嵌入式软件IP保护机制，使未经授权的软件副本在克隆设备上只能次优运行，无需额外硬件支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业系统嵌入式软件包含经过大量资源调优的关键数据，这些数据是企业的宝贵知识产权。现有保护机制存在不足，需要一种更难以绕过且无需额外硬件的保护方法。

Method: 结合硬件指纹（基于SRAM的物理不可克隆函数PUF）与布尔逻辑，将软件参数（如PID控制器参数）绑定到特定微控制器单元（MCU）。通过PUF生成的硬件唯一指纹作为密钥，保护关键数据。

Result: 安全评估表明，只有在真实目标设备上对受保护软件进行复杂的动态分析才能揭示秘密数据，使保护机制更难以绕过。该方法无需额外硬件，仅需相对简单的软件更新。

Conclusion: 提出的保护机制有效保护嵌入式软件IP，使未经授权的副本在克隆设备上只能次优运行，提高了保护强度且降低了实施成本。

Abstract: Embedded software used in industrial systems frequently relies on data that ensures the correct and efficient operation of these systems. Thus, companies invest considerable resources in fine-tuning this data, making it their valuable intellectual property (IP). We present a novel protection mechanism for this IP that combines hardware fingerprints with Boolean logic. Unlike usual copy-protection approaches, unauthorised copies of the software still run on cloned devices but suboptimally. According to our security evaluation, only a complex dynamic analysis of the protected software running on the genuine target device can reveal the secret data. This makes the protection offered by our method more difficult to bypass. Notably, our approach does not require additional hardware, relying only on relatively simple updates to the software. We evaluate our protection mechanism by binding the parameters of a PID controller to a microcontroller unit (MCU) by using a physically unclonable function (PUF) based on its SRAM.

</details>


### [159] [Understanding LLM Behavior When Encountering User-Supplied Harmful Content in Harmless Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11914)
*Junjie Chu,Yiting Qu,Ye Leng,Michael Backes,Yun Shen,Savvas Zannettou,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了大语言模型在内容层面的伦理漏洞：即使执行看似无害的任务，当用户提供有害内容时，主流LLMs仍会继续处理而非拒绝，存在被滥用的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs的对齐训练主要关注任务层面的伦理（拒绝直接有害任务），但忽视了内容层面的伦理问题：当执行看似无害任务时，如果用户提供的材料中包含有害内容，LLMs是否会像有道德意识的人类一样拒绝继续处理？

Method: 1) 构建包含1,357条条目、覆盖10个有害类别的有害知识数据集（不符合OpenAI使用政策）；2) 设计9个无害任务（符合OpenAI政策），按所需用户提供内容量分为广泛、中等、有限三类；3) 评估9个LLMs在执行无害任务时遇到用户提供有害内容时的行为；4) 分析有害知识类别与任务类型之间的动态关系如何影响不同LLMs。

Result: 1) 当前LLMs（包括最新的GPT-5.2和Gemini-3-Pro）在执行无害任务时经常无法坚持人类对齐的伦理，继续处理有害内容；2) "暴力/图像"类别的外部知识和"翻译"任务更容易引发LLMs的有害响应；3) 通过广泛的消融研究探讨了影响这种新型滥用漏洞的潜在因素。

Conclusion: LLMs存在被忽视的内容层面伦理风险，即使执行无害任务也可能被滥用处理有害内容。研究呼吁利益相关者加强安全措施以缓解这种风险，特别是在"暴力/图像"内容和"翻译"任务方面需要特别关注。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly trained to align with human values, primarily focusing on task level, i.e., refusing to execute directly harmful tasks. However, a subtle yet crucial content-level ethical question is often overlooked: when performing a seemingly benign task, will LLMs -- like morally conscious human beings -- refuse to proceed when encountering harmful content in user-provided material? In this study, we aim to understand this content-level ethical question and systematically evaluate its implications for mainstream LLMs. We first construct a harmful knowledge dataset (i.e., non-compliant with OpenAI's usage policy) to serve as the user-supplied harmful content, with 1,357 entries across ten harmful categories. We then design nine harmless tasks (i.e., compliant with OpenAI's usage policy) to simulate the real-world benign tasks, grouped into three categories according to the extent of user-supplied content required: extensive, moderate, and limited. Leveraging the harmful knowledge dataset and the set of harmless tasks, we evaluate how nine LLMs behave when exposed to user-supplied harmful content during the execution of benign tasks, and further examine how the dynamics between harmful knowledge categories and tasks affect different LLMs. Our results show that current LLMs, even the latest GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3-Pro, often fail to uphold human-aligned ethics by continuing to process harmful content in harmless tasks. Furthermore, external knowledge from the ``Violence/Graphic'' category and the ``Translation'' task is more likely to elicit harmful responses from LLMs. We also conduct extensive ablation studies to investigate potential factors affecting this novel misuse vulnerability. We hope that our study could inspire enhanced safety measures among stakeholders to mitigate this overlooked content-level ethical risk.

</details>


### [160] [Delayed Backdoor Attacks: Exploring the Temporal Dimension as a New Attack Surface in Pre-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11949)
*Zikang Ding,Haomiao Yang,Meng Hao,Wenbo Jiang,Kunlan Xiang,Runmeng Du,Yijing Liu,Ruichen Zhang,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了延迟后门攻击（DBA），挑战了传统后门攻击的"即时性假设"，通过引入时间维度使攻击激活与触发器暴露时间解耦，从而允许使用常见日常词汇作为触发器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预训练模型后门攻击基于"即时性假设"，即恶意行为在触发器出现时立即激活。这种假设限制了攻击的隐蔽性，因为需要不常见的触发器词汇。作者认为时间维度是解锁使用常见日常词汇作为触发器的攻击的关键，这类攻击先前被认为是不可行的。

Method: 设计了基于非线性衰减的延迟后门攻击原型（DND），嵌入轻量级有状态逻辑模块，将攻击激活推迟到达到可配置阈值时。提出了形式化模型描述延迟行为，并设计了双指标评估框架（ASR和ASR_delay）来经验性测量延迟效果。

Result: 在四个NLP基准测试上的广泛实验验证了DND的核心能力：可在可控时间内保持休眠状态，保持高清洁准确率（≥94%），实现接近完美的激活后攻击成功率（≈99%，其他方法平均低于95%）。DND对多种最先进防御方法表现出抗性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次提供了经验证据，表明时间维度构成了预训练模型中可行但未受保护的攻击面，强调需要开发下一代有状态且时间感知的防御机制。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks against pre-trained models (PTMs) have traditionally operated under an ``immediacy assumption,'' where malicious behavior manifests instantly upon trigger occurrence. This work revisits and challenges this paradigm by introducing \textit{\textbf{Delayed Backdoor Attacks (DBA)}}, a new class of threats in which activation is temporally decoupled from trigger exposure. We propose that this \textbf{temporal dimension} is the key to unlocking a previously infeasible class of attacks: those that use common, everyday words as triggers. To examine the feasibility of this paradigm, we design and implement a proof-of-concept prototype, termed \underline{D}elayed Backdoor Attacks Based on \underline{N}onlinear \underline{D}ecay (DND). DND embeds a lightweight, stateful logic module that postpones activation until a configurable threshold is reached, producing a distinct latency phase followed by a controlled outbreak. We derive a formal model to characterize this latency behavior and propose a dual-metric evaluation framework (ASR and ASR$_{delay}$) to empirically measure the delay effect. Extensive experiments on four (natural language processing)NLP benchmarks validate the core capabilities of DND: it remains dormant for a controllable duration, sustains high clean accuracy ($\ge$94\%), and achieves near-perfect post-activation attack success rates ($\approx$99\%, The average of other methods is below 95\%.). Moreover, DND exhibits resilience against several state-of-the-art defenses. This study provides the first empirical evidence that the temporal dimension constitutes a viable yet unprotected attack surface in PTMs, underscoring the need for next-generation, stateful, and time-aware defense mechanisms.

</details>


### [161] [EmbTracker: Traceable Black-box Watermarking for Federated Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12089)
*Haodong Zhao,Jinming Hu,Yijie Bai,Tian Dong,Wei Du,Zhuosheng Zhang,Yanjiao Chen,Haojin Zhu,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EmbTracker是一个针对联邦语言模型的服务器端、可追踪的黑盒水印框架，通过嵌入基于后门的水印实现黑盒验证，并为每个客户端注入独特的身份特定水印，实现客户端级别的溯源能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦语言模型（FedLM）允许在不共享原始数据的情况下进行协作学习，但引入了关键漏洞，因为每个不可信的客户端都可能泄露接收到的功能模型实例。现有的FedLM水印方案通常需要白盒访问和客户端协作，仅提供组级别的所有权证明而非个体可追溯性。

Method: EmbTracker采用服务器端、可追踪的黑盒水印框架，通过嵌入基于后门的水印实现黑盒验证（可通过简单API查询检测），并通过为每个客户端注入独特的身份特定水印实现客户端级别的可追溯性。

Result: 在各种语言和视觉语言模型上的广泛实验表明，EmbTracker实现了接近100%验证率的稳健可追溯性，对移除攻击（微调、剪枝、量化）具有高弹性，对主要任务性能影响可忽略（通常在1-2%以内）。

Conclusion: EmbTracker为联邦语言模型提供了一个有效的服务器端黑盒水印解决方案，能够在非合作参与者的情况下实现客户端级别的模型泄露溯源，解决了现有水印方案在可追溯性和实用性方面的局限性。

Abstract: Federated Language Model (FedLM) allows a collaborative learning without sharing raw data, yet it introduces a critical vulnerability, as every untrustworthy client may leak the received functional model instance. Current watermarking schemes for FedLM often require white-box access and client-side cooperation, providing only group-level proof of ownership rather than individual traceability. We propose EmbTracker, a server-side, traceable black-box watermarking framework specifically designed for FedLMs. EmbTracker achieves black-box verifiability by embedding a backdoor-based watermark detectable through simple API queries. Client-level traceability is realized by injecting unique identity-specific watermarks into the model distributed to each client. In this way, a leaked model can be attributed to a specific culprit, ensuring robustness even against non-cooperative participants. Extensive experiments on various language and vision-language models demonstrate that EmbTracker achieves robust traceability with verification rates near 100\%, high resilience against removal attacks (fine-tuning, pruning, quantization), and negligible impact on primary task performance (typically within 1-2\%).

</details>


### [162] [Understanding Disclosure Risk in Differential Privacy with Applications to Noise Calibration and Auditing (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12142)
*Patricia Guerra-Balboa,Annika Sauer,Héber H. Arcolezi,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的差分隐私风险度量标准——重构优势，用于统一评估成员推断、属性推断和数据重构攻击风险，解决了现有方法在现实假设下的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私风险分析主要关注成员推断攻击（威胁范围有限）或依赖重构鲁棒性（ReRo），但在现实假设下，ReRo可能产生误导性风险估计并违反声称的边界，限制了其在原则性DP校准和审计中的实用性。

Method: 引入重构优势作为统一的风险度量标准，推导了差分隐私噪声与对抗优势之间的紧致边界，并描述了针对任意DP机制和攻击者知识的最优对抗策略。

Result: 重构优势提高了差分隐私审计的准确性和范围，实现了更有效的效用-隐私权衡，为风险驱动的噪声校准提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 重构优势为差分隐私风险分析提供了统一框架，支持系统化的DP审计，并改进了DP数据管理系统的效用-隐私权衡。

Abstract: Differential Privacy (DP) is widely adopted in data management systems to enable data sharing with formal disclosure guarantees. A central systems challenge is understanding how DP noise translates into effective protection against inference attacks, since this directly determines achievable utility. Most existing analyses focus only on membership inference -- capturing only a threat -- or rely on reconstruction robustness (ReRo). However, under realistic assumptions, we show that ReRo can yield misleading risk estimates and violate claimed bounds, limiting their usefulness for principled DP calibration and auditing.
  This paper introduces reconstruction advantage, a unified risk metric that consistently captures risk across membership inference, attribute inference, and data reconstruction. We derive tight bounds that relate DP noise to adversarial advantage and characterize optimal adversarial strategies for arbitrary DP mechanisms and attacker knowledge. These results enable risk-driven noise calibration and provide a foundation for systematic DP auditing. We show that reconstruction advantage improves the accuracy and scope of DP auditing and enables more effective utility-privacy trade-offs in DP-enabled data management systems.

</details>


### [163] [Privacy in ERP Systems: Behavioral Models of Developers and Consultants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12195)
*Alicia Pang,Katsiaryna Labunets,Olga Gadyatskaya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究ERP系统中隐私意识，分析开发者和顾问的隐私行为模型，提出改进隐私相关行为的方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ERP系统存储敏感数据，企业需遵守GDPR等法规，但开发者缺乏隐私知识，实施隐私保护面临挑战

Method: 在国际咨询公司背景下，调查ERP系统开发者及管理顾问的隐私意识，应用主题分析和Fogg行为模型框架建立隐私行为模型

Result: 通过FBM框架建立了两个群体的隐私行为模型，为促进开发者和顾问更适当的隐私相关行为提供了方法

Conclusion: 研究结果为提升ERP系统中的隐私保护行为提供了实用框架，有助于企业更好地遵守数据保护法规

Abstract: Applications like Enterprise Resource Planning (ERP) systems have become an indispensable part of the corporate digital infrastructure. These systems store sensitive data about customers, suppliers, and employees, and thus companies have to process these data in accordance with applicable regulations like the GDPR (the EU General Data Protection Regulation). This can be challenging due to a variety of reasons. For example, prior research has shown that developers sometimes lack knowledge about privacy.
  In this work, we focus on privacy in ERP systems in the context of an international consultancy firm. We investigate the privacy awareness regarding privacy-by-design and data minimization of two important populations: developers of ERP systems and managers and consultants responsible for services related to ERP systems. Applying thematic analysis, we elicit privacy behavioral models of these two populations using Fogg's Behavioral Model (FBM) framework. Our findings provide a means to stimulate more adequate privacy-related behaviors for developers and consultants.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Probabilistic Disjunctive Normal Forms in Temporal Logic and Automata Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11083)
*Alexander Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: PDNFs扩展经典DNF以表示逻辑系统中的不确定性，通过实值权重编码变量存在、缺失或否定的概率信息，构建向量空间进行代数证据组合，并建立与概率分布和可积函数的双重联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为逻辑系统中的不确定性表示和推理提供统一框架，弥合逻辑、数值方法和连续概率之间的鸿沟，实现代数证据组合和概率解释。

Method: 引入概率析取范式(PDNFs)，为变量分配实值权重编码概率信息；构建PDNFs的向量空间支持代数运算；建立PDNFs作为venjunctions概率分布和分区区间上可积函数的双重解释；在指数参数化下将PDNF加法与贝叶斯证据融合对齐。

Result: PDNFs成功构建了具有Banach空间结构的统一框架，在指数参数化下PDNF加法等价于贝叶斯证据融合，并推导了从随机样本进行结果识别的边界。

Conclusion: PDNFs为逻辑系统中的不确定性表示提供了强大框架，通过代数、概率和函数分析方法的融合，实现了逻辑推理、数值计算和连续概率理论的统一。

Abstract: This article introduces probabilistic disjunctive normal forms (PDNFs) as a framework for representing and reasoning about uncertainty in logical systems. Unlike classical DNFs, PDNFs assign real-valued weights to variables, encoding probabilistic information about their presence, absence, or negation. Then we construct a vector space of PDNFs that allows algebraic evidence combination. PDNFs are interpreted as probability distributions over venjunctions (temporal logic constructs) and as integrable functions over partitioned intervals, where the integrals determine variable probabilities. This dual perspective allows for a Banach space structure and the application of functional analysis. We demonstrate that, under exponential parametrisation, PDNF addition aligns with Bayesian evidence fusion and derive bounds for outcome identification from random samples. The formalism thus bridges logic, numerical methods, and continuous probability.

</details>


### [165] [Witnesses for Fixpoint Games on Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11908)
*Barbara König,Karla Messing*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了用于推导定点博弈策略的见证，并证明了函数最小定点与给定边界的关系。基于格论方法，通过Galois连接连接逻辑宇宙和行为宇宙两个格，研究原始和对偶两种博弈类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是开发一种系统化的方法来验证函数最小定点与给定边界的关系，为各种验证问题提供统一的见证构造框架。通过连接逻辑宇宙和行为宇宙，将复杂的定点计算问题转化为可操作的博弈策略。

Method: 采用格论方法，建立两个格之间的Galois连接：一个表示"逻辑宇宙"（见证所在），另一个表示"行为宇宙"（函数定义域）。研究两种博弈类型——原始博弈和对偶博弈，展示如何从见证推导博弈的获胜策略，以及如何从策略构造见证。

Result: 成功构建了从见证到博弈策略的双向转换框架：可以从见证推导出定点博弈的获胜策略，也可以从博弈策略构造相应的见证。该理论可实例化到已知案例，如标准互模拟的区分公式构造和概率系统的行为度量，并应用于马尔可夫链终止概率下界认证的新案例研究。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个统一的格论框架，用于构造定点博弈的见证和策略，建立了逻辑宇宙与行为宇宙之间的系统连接。该框架具有广泛适用性，可扩展到多种验证场景，包括概率系统的行为度量和马尔可夫链终止概率分析。

Abstract: We construct witnesses that can be used to derive strategies in fixpoint games and provide proof that the least fixpoint of a function is either above or not below some given bound. We rely on a lattice-theoretical approach, including a Galois connection that connects a lattice representing the "logic universe", where the witness lives, with another lattice representing the "behaviour universe", over which the function is defined. In fact we consider two types of games -- primal and dual games -- and in both cases show how to derive winning strategies in the game from witnesses and construct witnesses from strategies. The two games differ wrt. their rules and the choice of basis of the lattice.
  The theory can be instantiated to well-known examples: in particular we compare with the construction of distinguishing formulas in standard bisimilarity and behavioural metrics for probabilistic systems. As a new case study we consider witnesses for certifying lower bounds for the termination probability for Markov chains.

</details>


### [166] [When do modal definability and preservation theorems transfer to the finite?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12171)
*Johan van Benthem,Balder ten Cate,Xi Yang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 研究模态逻辑在有限结构上的可定义性和保持性结果，发现一些经典定理在有限域中仍然成立，而另一些则失效


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索经典模态逻辑理论在有限结构上的适用性，因为许多一阶逻辑的转移定理在有限域中失效，需要验证模态逻辑相关结果在有限结构中的表现

Method: 系统分析模态公式类在有限结构上的语义特征，检验基本框架操作的一阶保持定理在有限域中的有效性，证明双模拟安全定理在有限结构中的转移性

Result: 多个模态公式类的语义特征在有限结构中保持有效，但许多基本框架操作的一阶保持定理在有限域中失效；主要正面结果是双模拟安全定理成功转移到有限结构

Conclusion: 模态逻辑在有限结构上的表现与无限结构存在显著差异：一些经典结果保持，另一些失效；双模拟安全定理的有限转移性为有限域模态逻辑提供了重要理论基础

Abstract: We study which classic modal definability and preservation results survive when attention is restricted to finite structures, where many first-order transfer theorems are known to break down. Several semantic characterizations for modal formula classes survive the passage to the finite, while a number of first-order preservation theorems for basic frame operations fail. Our main positive result is that the Bisimulation Safety Theorem does transfer to finite structures. We also discuss computability aspects, and analogues in the finite for the Goldblatt-Thomason theorem and for modal correspondence theory.

</details>


### [167] [Coalgebraic Path Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12204)
*Todd Schmid*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种称为"等式路径约束"的新方法，用于公理化余代数余簇，作为传统余方程方法的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为余代数余簇提供公理化的方法不如代数簇的公理化直观。现有技术来自余代数模态逻辑、模式避免规范和隐藏代数，需要更直观的代数风格替代方案。

Method: 引入等式路径约束：为余代数中的每条路径分配一对值，并假设这两个值相等。该方法将路径计算值与代数方程相结合，形成一种行为约束。

Result: 证明等式路径约束能够定义余簇，并在具体案例中构建了相对于这些约束的最终余代数。建立了当路径计算值位于单子中时，等式路径约束与余方程的联系，并计算了表达余方程所需颜色数量的上界。

Conclusion: 等式路径约束提供了一种行为良好、相对易于描述的有限行为属性类，为余方程提供了代数风格的替代方案，在自动机理论、微分方程、双无限流和框架条件等领域有应用前景。

Abstract: Axiomatizing covarieties of coalgebras for an endofunctor is less intuitive than axiomatizing varieties of algebras via equations (Dahlqvist and Schmid, 2022). Existing techniques come from coalgebraic modal logic, pattern avoidance specifications, and hidden algebra. We introduce equational path constraints, a well-behaved and relatively easy to describe class of finitary behavioural properties that provide an algebra-flavoured alternative to coequations. The basic idea is to assign a pair of values to each path through a coalgebra and posit that the two values coincide. We show that equational path constraints define covarieties and construct final coalgebras relative to equational path constraints in some concrete cases. We connect equational path constraints to coequations when values computed from paths live in a monad, and we compute an upper bound on the number of colours needed to express the coequation. One of our constructions is reminiscent of the initial/terminal sequences of (Adámek, 1974) and (Barr, 1993). Motivating examples include commutativity conditions in automata theory, differential equations, bi-infinite streams, and frame conditions.
